## Chapter 1

**zuko's POV**

*"i cannot believe my ears!" father shouts so loud that it's a miracle his vocal chords are still intact.* *"i thought i pulled all those foolish ideas out of your head!"*

*'pulling out my ideas', actually means he beat the shit out of me.*

*he throws a vase at the wall behind me, almost striking my head. i shudder while thousands of* *ceramic pieces fall scattered on the floor.*

*huh, just like my heart right now, what a coincidence* *!*

*"i told you, dad, it's not like i can change anything" i attempt to explain while crying, but it's like* *trying to teach a sofa how to write.*

*"you dare speak to me with such insolence?" he bursts. "i shall teach you how to behave like a* *man! men do not act like you do and they certainly do not cry!" he exclaims even angrier and* *brutally grabs my forearm.*

*his nails sink deep into my skin and i try to break his grip with no success.*

*"father, please-" i try to beg him, but he interrupts me.*

*"silence, you disobedient child! you will learn to respect our family and our traditions once and for* *all!"*

*he pulls me to the fireplace and starts pushing my head closer to the flames.*

*"dad, what are you-" my voice cracks and i don't get to finish the sentence.*

*suddenly, all i can feel is excruciating pain in every fiber, every mucle, every goddamn cell in my* *body. i feel it all the way to my fingertips.*

*i think i'm screaming, but i'm not certain. i can't hear anything around me, but my throat feels like* *it's being torn apart by some long, merciless claws.*

*the fire is kissing my skin hungrily, melting everything in its path recklessly.*

*this hell might as well have lasted 5 seconds, half a minute, or hours, i couldn't tell.*

*i just wish my mom was here now.*

*--*

i gasp for air desperately and clench my fists around the bedsheets involuntarily. it takes me a moment to realise where i am and i sigh in relief. it was just another nightmare.

i've been having them every single night since my father decided to scar me for life, leaving me partially blind and deaf on the left side of my face, at only 15 years old.

it's been 3 years, but it's impossible for me to forget.

i mean, how could i? i have a huge mark on my fucking face that makes sure to remind me of my trauma every goddamn day.

today is my first day at Ba Sing Se Highschool and i have absolutely no motivation to even get out of bed.

i used to go to a private highschool, but my father kicked me out of the house a month ago and disowned me, so i've been living with my uncle since then.

he's been a fatherly figure to me all my life, way more than my actual dad ever was. after i got burned, he was the one who made sure it didn't get infected and he gave me different creams and natural treatments so i didn't lose my sight completely on my left eye.

i love him with all my heart and i could never repay him for everything he's done for me. he's the only person in the world i trust and he gives the most useful pieces of advice you could ask for.

uncle iroh owns the best tea shop in town, the Jasmine Dragon, where we both work.

we can't afford to pay the taxes requiered at my old school, so i have no option but to finish highschool at Ba Sing Se.

i'm not hoping to make any friends, i don't even want to. it's my last year and there's no point in getting attached to anyone anyways. everybody leaves or lets me down at some point, so it's better not to create those bonds in the first place.

i put on some black cuffed jeans, a white collared shirt tucked in, and a black and red plaid wool vest over it.

i put some rings on my fingers, get my white sneakers and backpack, i stick a yellow flower in my messy hair just because i fucking like it and i'm good to go.

"good luck on your first day, zuko! remember, just be yourself and everyone will love you as much as i do!" uncle cheers me after i barely eat anything from the brakfast he made me.

"yeah, yeah, thanks, i will." i mumble and walk out the front door waving him goodbye, aware of the minor chances of someone else accepting me for who i am like he does.

i head to school on foot, as i don't own a car anymore and despite it being a slightly long distance, i don't mind walking. this way i can clear my head and take in some fresh air as well as warm sunlight while blasting music in my headphones.

&#45;-

the courtyard is swarming with people. social anxiety fills up every cell in my body and my heart starts racing, but i try to calm down.

*'it's all good, zuko, it doesn't matter what people think about you, you don't give a shit, you're not* *here to please others, you're here to educate yourself'* i try to encourage myself.

i take in a deep breath, letting the air fill my lungs and i gather the courage to walk across the yard.

i don't know if the feeling of millions of eyes on me is a result of my anxiety or if everyone is staring at the huge scar i am desperately trying to hide behind my long bangs.

"who the hell is that guy?" someone says. "i have no idea" i manage to hear a piece of conversation between two girls i pass by on my way.

i sigh as i reach the double wooden doors of the building, i push them and step inside.

*'just be cool, nobody hates you, they don't even know you, it's all in your head, zuko'*

it's hard to believe myself sometimes.

i ask a random teacher where the principal's office is and i follow him to a brown door with a window covering almost half of it.

i try to make it as fast as i can, i just need to get my schedule and get out.

fortunately, the principal pays little attention to me, as he is preoccupied with some official paperwork i assume, and gives me what i need with no further questions.

i hear the bell ringing and everyone soon hurries to their classes, leaving me alone in the hallway, with absolutely no clue where my literature class is.

i start looking for room 23 and just when i'm about to give up and run to the bathroom to cry, i spot it.

spirits, i'm late on my first day, that's amazing zuko, good job!

i lightly knock on the door and a voice invites me in.

"hello, sorry i'm late, i kinda got lost" i excuse myself and i hear some people chuckling. i choose to ignore them.

"oh, you must be the new kid, introduce yourself to the class quickly and then take a seat. there's one back there, next to sokka" the woman says.

i glance to where she is pointing to and a boy with caramel skin and a ponytail waves at me with a smile painted on his face.

i turn to face the whole class and they all are really staring at me.

"um- hello, zuko here, i- um- i'm 18 and ugh- i like to draw- i guess, um- yeah, that's kinda it" i manage to say scratching the back of my neck in embarassment.

i'm pathetic.

"what happened to your face, dude?" a guy asks giggling.

*'don't react, just ignore'*

i don't answer, i just rush to my seat, let my backpack fall on the floor and i bury my face in my hands.

"ugh, i guess someone's touchy" a girl's voice adds.

i already hate it here. i give her a death glance and she turns back to her seat rolling her eyes.

"hey, shut up!" the guy that is my deskmate tells them and i'm surprised.

"you ok, buddy?" he turns to me and pats my back.

"yeah, i'm fine. thanks, but i can handle bullies" i assure him, keeping my eyes locked on the teacher that started her lesson without me even realising.

"i'm sure you can" he says with a soft chuckle. "i'm sokka by the way"

he extends his arm and i assume he expects me to shake his hand. i look at his hand, not intending to accept the handshake, then i lock eyes with him.

i've never seen eyes so blue. they're like a cloudless sky of a heated summer day, that you could just stare at for ages and not get enough of it.

he raises an eyebrow and i realise i'm staring. dumb, dumb, dumb. i'm so dumb.

"i'm zuko" i say at last, but of course he already knows that, i just presented myself in front of the class and made a fool of myself cause i can't even put 2 words together.

"nice to meet you, zuko" he smiles.

i don't return the smile, i just open my notebook and begin copying what the teacher wrote on the board.

after class, i leave the room, but i feel someone tapping on my shoulder. i turn around and it's that sokka kid. he's almost as tall as me, maybe an inch or two shorter.

"hey, i was wondering, maybe you'd want someone to show you around so you don't get lost again? i can do that if you're ok with it" he offers.

*'no friends, zuko, they will leave you and you know it'*

"i'm good" i respond and proceed to walk like a chicken without a head, confused of its surroundings, cause i don't know where i should go.

he doesn't come after me and i'm thankful for that.

&#45;-

i didn't bring any food with me so, here i am, at lunch break, sitting outside, on the grass, with my back up against a huge tree, reading a book and enjoying some of my uncle's jasmine tea.

every other person is sitting at tables, laughing and eating, but i enjoy solitude. i find it peaceful. and i also have no friends, which i think was already obvious.

suddenly, a paper ball which appears to be a note hits my head then falls in my lap.

who could it be from? maybe someone wants to be my friend? perhaps sokka from literature?

i unfold it, my heart racing in my chest, but as soon as i see it's conent, it drops. there's only one word written.

'faggot'

i crush the note in my fist and put it in my pocket, not wanting to throw garbage on the ground.

i look around, hoping i could catch a glimpse of someone seeming suspect of having written this shitty note, but i don't see anything.

i am too angry to notice that sokka is now standing up next to me, until he starts talking.

"hey, would you like to sit with me and my friends?"

"thanks, but-" i start responding, but right then i see a guy pointing at me with a few other people, laughing so fucking hard that i wanna smash his face really bad.

i move mechanically and i don't realise i got up until i am facing the boy with brown shaggy hair and a toothpick between his lips.

my hands make contact with his chest and i push him so hard, he would have fallen if his friends weren't there to catch him.

"what the fuck is your problem?" i spit the words angrily at him.

"woah, the twink has some fire in him!" he laughs and so do his friends. "what are you gonna do, paint my nails like a girl and put *flowers* in my hair? he mocks me, ripping the yellow flower from my hair and smashing it between his thumb and index.

*'zuko, don't punch him, you'll get expelled'* my inner voice advises me.

i close my eyes for a second to calm down and then i answer him.

"so what if i paint my nails? is your masculinity so fragile that some nail polish can destroy it?" i pout sarcastically.

he is mad now, but also surprised.

"why the fuck did you come here, freak? to scare kids with that hideous thing on your face? cause it's working wonders"

"how about you mind your own business? you sound like a closeted gay with internalised homophobia" i tell him, then turn around and walk away, leaving everyone with their jaws on the floor.

i guess bullies aren't used to being called out. i almost smile, the left corner of my mouth lifting up just a bit.

"wow, you really put jet in his place!" sokka somehow appears next to me. "that asshole just never knows when to stop"

"yeah, as i said, i can handle bullies"

i return to the spot i was occupying before, and sokka sits down next to me.

"but what did he do to you? i'm sure he deserved what he got, i'm just naturally curious" he asks.

"uhm-" i hesitate, considering if i should tell him or not. "he gave me a note with a very rude word on it" i decide to inform him.

"spirits, what a douchebag!" he exclaims in disgust.

"yeah, tell me about it"

there is a moment of silence between us, but not the awkward type, so i don't mind it.

"it's a shame he destroyed your flower, though. it was really pretty. yellow truly brings out your eyes"

i make eye contact with him for a few seconds, but i break it quicky to avoid staring at him again.

"thanks, but it's fine, i got more of those at home" i say brushing some invisible particles off my jeans just to seem busy.

the bell rings, announcing the start of another lesson. sokka gets up and dusts himself.

"i really gotta go. i've got an important test in maths and i suck at it. wish me luck!" he says and doesn't actually give me time to respond, cause he takes off.

"good luck, sokka" i whisper even though i know he can't hear me. i get my things and head to class.

as i walk down the hallway, i catch myself thinking of those ocean eyes.

i brush the thought off quickly.

## Chapter 2

**zuko's POV (the next day)**

i hate PE class. why do i have to get all sweaty and tired in front of other people? it's so disgusting.

at my old school, we were taught self defense and martial arts, even sword fight, but here... why the hell do i need to play *basketball*?

i even have to wear the school sports uniform which consists of an orange tank top and some - also orange - shorts. great, just like prisoners. i hate orange, it makes me wanna puke my guts out.

right now i'm in a locker room full of naked dudes, i'm as red as a tomato and i'm trying to change into the uniform as fast as i can, without looking at anybody. if i can't see them, they can't see me, right?

i can't say i hate my body, but i'm not comfortable with other people seeing my bare skin. especially because of all the scars left by my father in certain places. i'm kind of ashamed of those.

after i put my clothes in the locker, the door opens and someone bursts inside, meeting cheers from every person in the room. i recognise the guy with the ponytail immediately.

"yo, sokka, my man!" a guy exclaims, bumping his fist into sokka's. "are you ready to get your ass kicked on the field today?"

"haha" sokka laughs. "in your dreams! you know you can't beat me"

he seems to know everybody, and everybody seems to know and love him.

extroverts terrify me.

he exchanges a few words with almost each person and then heads to his locker, which appears to be near mine.

"hey there, zuko! what's up?" he asks me with a smile printed on his face.

"um- i'm ok, i guess" i respond breifly. "i'm not a fan of basketball though"

"oh, that's a shame, but you might change your mind with me as the captain" he says and winks at me.

my cheeks get flushed in a second and, as if the wink wasn't enough, he takes his shirt and pants off.

i look away because it would just be disrespectful and awkward to stare at him while he changes.

"sokka, why are you talking to that freak? come over here!" a boy's voice states from the opposite side of the room.

"mason, you take that back" sokka says in a slightly aggressive tone.

he turned his back on the locker and is now facing this mason guy. oh, i have to mention he still didn't put on his uniform. how can he be so comfortable almost naked in front of so many people?

"what, you're friends with that faggot now?" mason laughs mockingly.

sokka seems very tense and about to punch that guy in the face. i think he's really gonna do that, but i grab his shoulder to stop him.

"it's ok, sokka, just leave him be, i'm used to it" i tell him and he eases up a bit.

i take my hand off him, but i can still feel it imprinted on fingertips. his skin was soft and warm and-

*'shit, why am i thinking that? stop the nonsense, zuko!'*

spirits, can't he just put a damn shirt on?

"no, zuko, it's not ok. i'm sick of seeing you and other people being mocked for no fucking reason by people who think they are better than everyone!" sokka almost yells and now all the locker room noise has gone out.

i start twirling a strand of hair around my index nervously. now i'm being stared at by dozens of eyes and i feel my scar burning, as if to remind me it's still there, very noticeable, and very obvious to everyone.

"from now on, if i hear you say something rude to zuko or any other kid, i'll kick your asses out of the team, is that clear?"

in response, he gets a collection of mumbles and nods in approval. wow, he really has influence around here.

&#45;-

when it's time to establish the teams, the coach designates sokka and mason as the captains of each team. i expect to be picked last, but surprisingly, sokka chooses me first.

of course, when we start running on the feild, i have no idea what to do, even though the coach explained me the rules before we began.

i find myself holding the ball a few times, but every time i pass it to someone else quickly, like it's covered in poison.

"zuko, catch!" i hear sokka shout and our eyes lock for a second as he throws me the ball.

could i possibly have caught the stupid ball? yes, sure i could have, but, being the idiot that i am, i fucking forgot how to move for a second and the ball made contact with my forhead, so i fell on the ground.

i groan and raise a hand to my head, but fortunately, it's not bleeding.

"oh, spirits, are you alright?" sokka runs over to me and now i'm sitting on my butt trying to stop my head from spinning.

"it's nothing, i've been through worse" i say attempting to smile to let him know i'm ok.

of course my smile looks more like a grimace, cause nothing can ever work out in my favour. "i'm so, so, so sorry zuko! let's go get you some ice" sokka apologises.

"it's not your fault i'm clueless at this game" i try to confort him so he doesn't feel guilty.

well, it *kind of* is his fault cause he's so distracting, but of course i can't tell him that.

he brings me a bag of ice and sits me on a bench, while the others go on with the game.

"here, hold it like this" he positiones the bag on my forehead and then lets me grab hold of it.

"thanks... and for the record, i still hate basketball, even with you as the captain" i say and smile shyly.

he laughs.

"hmm, does it have to do with the fact that i hit your head?" he jokes.

"what made you assume that?" i raise my eyebrow, acting surprised.

we both giggle and he hits me in the arm jokingly.

"sokka! back on the field! now!" the coach yells angrily at him.

"ugh, i guess that's my cue to go" he says. "maybe i can teach you how to avoid getting hit in the head sometime" he adds while he starts jogging back to the field.

"i'd like that" i say to myself, cause he is way too far to be able to hear me.

i spend the rest of the class watching the game, as well as the cheerleaders encouraging the guys from the side of the field.

i'm gonna be honest, i mostly kept my eyes on sokka, but i promise, i can explain!

i never thought that horrible colour, orange, could look SO FUCKING GOOD on someone until he proved me wrong.

*perhaps orange isn't such a terrible colour after all...*

the sunlight is falling perfectly on his warm skin, his hair is still held in that ponytail, but a few hairs have fallen out of place, making him look efortlessly hot and his arms in that tank top are-

*why the fuck am i thinking that, spirits, just stop it, brain!*

i litterally met the guy yesterday, what the hell is wrong with me? i gotta stop thinking about anything related to him.

the class is over, sokka's team won apparently, but i wouldn't know, cause i didn't even pay attention to the actual game.

i see a girl from the cheerleading team running to sokka and jumping in his arms, her legs wrapping around him. she then kisses him and he pulls her even closer by the waist.

for some reason, that scene makes my heart sink a little. of course he has a girlfriend, and she is so beautiful! her dark hair is just above her shoulders, her eyes are blue and her lips are full, why wouldn't he love her?

they really look good together and i decide to be happy for them.

&#45;-

i'm finally back in my clothes, a black sweater and some red pants with hanging chains on one side.

i'm walking down the hallway with the large hood pulled over my head, going to the eating area, as it's lunch break, but something catches my attention.

that guy *jet* again. he's picking on a little kid, most certainly a freshman, pulling his orange beanie off his bald head and pushing him around.

"hey, jackass!" i shout at him. "leave the kid alone, can't you see you're twice his size?"

jet's attention is now directed at me and he is shooting lasers from his eyes.

"it's none of your business, twink! just go do your make-up or something"

i roll my eyes.

"if you're gonna insult me, please come up with something more original, cause i'm bored of the same jokes over and over again" i tell him.

i grab the kid from the arm and pull him away.

"c'mon, let's go" i tell the boy, leaving jet behind.

"thank you so much! i don't know why jet keeps behaving like that, i never did anything to him" the little guy says, looking up at me with his huge, sparkly eyes.

"he's just an asshole and you're an easy target"

he rapidly changes the subject.

"what's a twink?" he asks genuinely.

"uhm- it's a bad word, don't use it" i respond.

"mhm, okay, i'll ask my friends if you won't tell me"

i frankly don't care what he'll ask his friends.

"i'm aang, by the way!" he introduces himself a bit overjoyed and shakes my hand enthusiastically.

"zuko" i say briefly and then try to part ways with him, but he's apparently just like a leech.

"come sit with me and my friends at lunch! pleaseee" he suggests.

he basically runs after me on the hallway, my steps being way too long for him to keep up with just by walking.

"i'm good, so no thanks" i kindly refuse his offer.

"please, please, pretty pleaaaseee" he begs me, his hands pulling my sleeve.

"if i say yes, will you shut up?" i say a bit frustrated and annoyed.

"yes" he smiles from ear to ear, grabs my writs and starts pulling me to the group of friends he was talking about.

he's strong for a little guy, but UGH i don't need more human interraction!

i hate meeting new people, i'm not capable of making small talk and i'm always awkward around people i don't know.

spirits, why did i let myself be convinced by this kid aang? i could have just ran, but i guess i can't back down now.

"guys, guys, look who's here! eveyone, this is zuko" aang points at me. "he just saved me from jet!!"

"ugh, saved is a big word-" i say rubbing the back of my head and then i notice who is actually sitting at the table.

it's sokka. we hold eye contact for a moment, while aang interrupts me and starts talking.

"don't be modest, zuko! you were totally badass! here, let me introduce you to the gAang!"

i take my eyes off sokka, slightly blushing and i try to pay attention to the words coming out of aang's mouth.

"this is toph, she is blind, but she can see with her feet, cause she's an earthbender, isn't that like the coolest thing?" aang says pointing to a girl wearing a green crop top and black large pants.

"yes, it's very cool indeed" i approve.

"are you a bender, zuko?" aang asks me.

"uhm- yeah, a firebender"

aang starts clapping his hands happily.

"that's amazing, now we have people bending every element in our group! i'm an airbender myself" he says proudly and i nod in understanding.

"this is katara" he gestures to a girl dressed in a baby blue dress and she waves at me. "she's the best waterbender ever! you better not mess with her" he warns me, whispering the last sentence.

"i heard that, aang!" katara says trying to appear tough, but letting out a soft chuckle.

"anyways" aang continues, ignoring her intervention. "that is sokka, katara's brother and his girlfriend, suki"

"yeah, we actually met already, we have a few classes together" i inform him.

"wow, great! let's sit down now cause i'm starving!" aang says and we both occupy our chairs.

"how's your head doing?" sokka asks, giving me a warm smile.

"it doesn't hurt. i have a high tolerance for pain" i assure him and start crunching some of my fire flakes.

"i'm glad, but i still feel a bit guilty- hey! give me some of those!" he points to my fire flakes and i gladly give them to him, then he takes a mouthful.

"be careful, they're really-" i try to warn him, but it's too late.

"AAAHH, SO SPICY" sokka starts yelling and spitting everything into a napkin.

i chuckle and everyone bursts into laughter, including sokka.

"oh, snoozles, you should have listened to sparky" toph says, wiping a tear from the corner of her eye.

"sparky? you mean me?" i ask her confused.

"toph gives everyone nicknames" katara clarifies.

"oh- okay, i guess" i mumble.

"that means you're part of the gAang!" suki says cheerfully.

"but- i just met you guys"

"i'm good at reading people, sparky, so i think you'll fit in just fine" toph assures me and devours her french fries.

then, they go on talking, making jokes and laughing together. i feel kinds left out, but there's nothing to do about that, so i just focus on my food.

"so, babe, are you coming to my place tonight?" suki asks sokka and catches my attention with the question.

"suki, my *sister* is here" he tries to shush her. "we'll talk about it later"

i have no idea why their conversation makes me a little sad. it's not like i thought i had a shot with this gorgeous guy who is obviously straight. i don't even know him very well.

"sparky, why did your heart-" toph starts saying, but then she stops. "ugh- nevermind"

that was kinda weird, but i brush it off and don't think much of it.

"so, zuko, did you get the scar from a firebending accident?" suki asks me out of nowhere and takes me by surprise.

my body tenses up involuntarily and i clench my teeth.

"i don't think you should-" sokka starts saying, but i don't hear the rest, cause i get up from the table without saying a word and start walking away.

i feel a hand grabbing my wrist and i'm about to kick the person in the stomach, but i see that it's sokka and i supress that urge.

"zuko, please don't go, suki didn't mean to make you uncomfortable, she is very sorry, she didn't think it through-"

"i know, i know" i cut him of. "it's just- it's a sensitive topic for me and i- ugh- i'm not mad at her. i just think it's better if i keep my distance from everyone, at least for now"

i say that, then rotate to my left and walk away.

he doesn't follow me this time.

## Chapter 3

**zuko's POV**

it's a busy evening at the Jasmine Dragon and me, my uncle and jin can barely deal with all the customers.

jin is uncle iroh's only employee, besides me, of course, and while she's sitting at the counter, collecting the bills, i'm running back and forth to make sure everyone gets their tea on time.

uncle is preparing the drinks and my clumsy ass has to deliver them to each person.

i hate being a waiter, i have to put on a fake smile every time i open my mouth and try not to be rude to the annoying ass customers.

at least i've been working here for a while now, so the regulars have gotten used to my scar. i only get remarks about it from new clients, but it doesn't happen very often.

i almost drop the tray full of tea cups that i'm holding when i see who walks in the shop.

"seriously guys, why did we have to come here, you know how much i hate tea" sokka says while he and his friends sit down at the only available table.

i haven't talked to any of them for the past week or so, despite of sokka's numerous attempts to start a conversation. aang also asked me to join them for lunch a few times, but he gave up when he saw i kept refusing.

i'm about to go to the room behind the counter and beg my uncle to swich places with me and be the waiter until they leave, but it's too late. they've spotted me.

"zuko" aang calls to me and i have no escape.

"good evening, what can i get you?" i adress them as if they were some random customers.

i know, it's pretty pathetic, but i have no idea what else i'm supposed to do in this situation.

"sparky, cut the crap" toph says. "we know you're avoinding us so stop the bullshit and just be our friend"

her blutness shocks me and i don't know how to respond.

"what *toph means*" sokka adds, giving her a reproachfull look, even though she can't see him. "is that we really want you to accept us as your friends"

i take a deep breath because my lungs find it difficult to breathe while sokka's looking at me with those eyes.

"why the fuck do any of you care? you don't know me and i don't want you to. i just want to be left alone, for fucks sake" i burst and i feel my cheeks getting red, i don't know if it's because i'm angry, or because sokka is there, or maybe i'm embarrased cause every individual in this room is looking at me.

"jin, tell my uncle i'm taking a break" i throw my apron on the floor and walk out the door.

i start running as fast as i can, until my legs feel numb and i'm sure i'm going to collapse.

i crumble on the grass in the random park i find myself in. i catch my breath and watch the sky as it's getting darker and darker as the minutes pass.

what is wrong with me? i totally overreacted. those people were just trying to help me and i- i shut myself in, as i always do. they are probably sick of me and will never want to hear from me again.

why does it seem impossible for me to believe someone could genuinely care about me? why do i have to be such an awkward and insecure piece of shit?

i hug my knees to my chest, still lying on the grass, and i close my eyes, letting a tiny and warm tear fall on my cheek.

"you ok, buddy?" a voice startles me, but i recognise it immediately.

"yes, i'm fine sokka, you can go away"

he takes a seat next to me and i turn my back on him. i hate being vulnerable in front of other people.

"i'm not going anywhere" he states firmly.

we sit there, without saying a single word for a few minutes, but it's weirdly comforting.

"why?" i break the silence and he is taken by surprise by my question.

"huh-? why what?"

i sit up, a few inches away from him, wrapping my arms around my knees once again and burying my face in them.

"why do you want to be friends with me so bad? i'm just a freak with a hideous thing on my face and you're so popular and everyone loves you and-" my voice cracks a little.

"hey, hey, don't say that, what are you talking about?"

he breaks the already small distance bewteen us and puts an arm around my shoulders. he lifts my chin up with his index so i can look into his eyes.

"you're right. i don't know you. not as much as i'd like to anyways" he says. "but there are some things i do know. for example, how your golden eyes sparkle when you see a dog rolling in the grass, or how you play with your hair when you're anxious about something. or how you chip your nail polish - that i absolutely love, by the way - before going on the field in PE. or your clothes, dude, you really have to teach me how to wear something else besides hoodies" he chuckles and i do too.

"and what hideous thing? i don't see anything wrong with your face. for the record, i think you're really beautiful, zuko"

yup, i'm pretty sure my heart is going to explode, or no, my whole body is going to blow up and cover him entirely in blood and other gross stuff.

my insides are on fire and i can't force myself to stop staring into his eyes. it's like a fucking spell he has me under.

i then remember he has a girlfriend and i'm only hurting myself by thinking these things. i shift my attention on my shoes, which all of a sudden, seem very interesting.

"i- i don't even know what so say, sokka, i- um- thank you, nobody has ever said things like that to me"

"hey, you have nothing to thank me for, i just stated some facts" he stands up and gives me a hand so he could help me get up too, but i don't take it.

i don't need his touch to give me even more butterflies to dance in my belly than i already have.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

he doesn't take my hand, but i don't make a big deal out of it.

i wonder what has happened that made him so... withdrawn from everyone. and i won't even mention the scar. if it wasn't a firebending accident, does that mean it was purposely inflicted on him?

no, that would be too cruel, it must have been an accident, but i can't be sure of it, can i? he will open up when he feels like it, i'm sure. all i gotta do is wait.

even though i'm extremly impatient and naturally curious, i will respect his privacy.

"i think we should go back. my uncle can't deal with all those clients by himself" zuko says once he gets up.

"yeah, i think that's probably a good idea. he must be boiling" he stops for a second. "get it? like, cause he's boiling the tea?" i laugh at my own joke.

"yes, i get it" he chuckles.

he rarely does, but when it happens, for some reason, i feel like i fuffilled my duty. his voice is rough, but when he giggles, it sounds so pure and genuine it makes me wanna hug him.

in a friendly way, of course.

when we get back to the tea shop, zuko's uncle runs to him and pulls him into a bear hug.

"zuko, are you alright? i was so worried about you! don't run off like that ever again!" he tries to sound mad, but he fails greatly.

"i'm fine uncle, i just needed to get some air" zuko excuses himself.

"for 2 hours!?"

i can see on his pale face that he wasn't aware of how much time he was gone.

"sorry" zuko mumbles.

"anyway, let's get to work, the clients just won't stop coming. i guess we have to hire more people as soon as possible" his uncle says.

"i can do it" i offer, to everyone's surprise, myself included.

i don't know where that came from, but the idea of spending more time with zuko intrigues me. and i guess some extra money wouldn't hurt, as i'm living only with katara because our dad works abroad to send us money.

"perfect, you can start now" iroh says and hands me an apron.

suki and katara start laughing with tears.

"that apron fits you so well, babe" my girlfriend mocks me and high fives my sister.

i roll my eyes.

"ha ha, very funny, why don't you go home and make some dinner for when i'll be back?" i suggest katara.

"wait, snoozels in an apron? i wish my eyes worked just so i could see *that*!" toph bursts into laughter.

"i think he looks good" aang says innocently.

"yeah, yeah, i get it, now go, all of you"

they finally leave and zuko explains to me quickly what i have to do. it doesn't seem so hard.

i spill some tea, i break a cup, but nobody gets mad at me, they just brush it off, understanding that i've never done this before.

i don't get to talk much to zuko, but just knowing he's around here somewhere gives me some kind of comfort. and he does look kinda cute in that apron.

*wait, why did i just think that*?

anyways, his uncle seems to be the most caring and calm person in the world, and i think zuko's really lucky to have him.

i don't ask why he lives with his uncle, and not his parents. maybe they died? maybe they abandoned him? that could be an explaination for his major trust issues.

i now realise why he always smells like cinnamon and vanilla. it's because he comes here and is always surrounded by different flavours.

we work our asses off until midnight, when the shop finally closes, leaving us tired, but somehow pleased to see the customers happy.

"good job, boys! it was our busiest night, but we did it!" iroh pats us both on the back and puts on a big smile that could warm up even the coldest heart.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

as soon as i arrive home, i get in the shower and let the cold water hit my body, hoping it would make me wake up from this dream.

only it's not a dream. sokka did say all those things to me just a few hours ago and i can't stop myself from thinking about him.

i get out of the shower, i put on some shorts and a large white t-shirt and crumble on the bedroom floor, staring at the neon stars i have on my ceiling. i blast some music in my headphones so i don't bother uncle with the noise and i just sit there, trying not to think.

only it's utterly impossible. sokka's speech replays in my head over and over and over again and all i can see are his eyes, all i can feel is his arm around me and UGH, I HATE THAT.

if before tonight i was just a little attracted to him, now i think i'm completely hooked, but i'm so fucking dumb, because i KNOW he's straight. i just can't help it.

also, he took the job as a waiter in a split second? now how am i supposed to get over my feelings for him if i will see him every single day from now on?

why can't i just stop feeling? why can't someone rip my heart out of my chest? it would be less painful than knowing i could never be with him as more than a friend.

*he called me beautiful*.

does he really see me that way? or did he just say that so i'd feel better? i don't know, he seemed sincere.

i never thought of myself as beautiful, especially after my dad basically mutilated me.

can i be pretty in someone else's eyes, even if i don't see it? maybe? maybe not.

ugh, sometimes i just wish to kill my brain and stop it from overthinking every fucking insignificant thing. if i had trouble sleeping before, just because of my father, now i think i won't even be able to take a nap.

he'll be present in my thoughts, and that scares me more than anything.

## Chapter 4

**zuko's POV**

since sokka accepted to be a waiter at my uncle's tea shop a week ago, we've been spending loads of time together.

i've gotten closer to every member of the gAang and i'm also getting more comfortable around them. i now sit with them at lunch and sometimes they come by the tea shop to see me and sokka at work.

he makes this job a lot more bearable. he constantly jokes around and the clients love his outgoing personality, contradictory to mine.

&#45;-

i'm sitting in the back of chemistry class, staring at the boy with golden skin that is chilling in the opposite side of the room.

the teacher made him change his seat because we were talking too loud, but even from this distance, we can still communicate just by locking our eyes.

i can only see his back and a part of his profile. he is paying attention to the teacher, but i can only concentrate on him.

i grab my backpack from the floor, take out my sketchbook with colorful stickers on its cover and start drawing him. i sketch out his strong jawline, so sharp that i'm afraid it might cut my finger if i touch it, his broad shoulders, his strong, yet not bulky arms and his hair.

he never wears it any other way, but in that silly ponytail that he calls a 'warrior tail' and i wonder what he looks like with his hair down.

the bell rings, breaking me from my thoughts and i close my sketchbook quickly so nobody can see i was drawing my friend.

"spirits, this lesson seemed to last forever!" sokka complains as we're walking down the hallway. "do you want to do that stupid project together, or what?" he asks me.

i glance at him in utter confusion.

"what project?"

"in chemistry? the one the teacher just told us about? did you not pay attention at all?" he sniggers.

i pinch my earlobe and pull it lightly.

"um, no. i couldn't really focus" i explain.

"you're so cute when you're clueless" he says grinning.

i instantly blush. *he called me cute.*

it should be illegal for him to hold so much power over me.

"we have to do a project about some stupid metals for next week" he clarifies. "i guess i'm stuck with you, i can't let you fail this class just because you're head is in the clouds, can i?"

"well, it looks like you don't really have a choice. you can't seem to get rid of me" i smirk.

i elbow him and he returns the favour, pushing me a bit harder and we're chuckling together.

"hey, um-" he says. "i was wondering if you could possibly help me with my maths homework, cause i don't understand shit and you have good grades at everything, so..."

extra time with sokka? damn right i'll do it.

"of course, that's why we're friends, right?"

"oh, so we're officially friends now?" he taunts me.

we haven't known eachother for a long time, but you know, there are some people that come into your life and you just click instantly, as if somehow your souls were already connected in a past life. that's how it happened with sokka, it all came naturally, like it was meant to be.

"shut up" i bump my fist into his shoulder and try cover my smile with my hair, but he tucks it behind my ear.

"you're really pretty when you smile, you shouldn't hide it"

my body is extremly aware that his hand is still touching me behind the ear and i wish i could shoot the insects that permanently live in my stomach when he's around.

once again, i feel my insides melting under his gaze and i look away because i want to kiss him so bad, but i can't risk ruining the only real friend i've ever had.

"i gotta get to history" i say briefly and take off, leaving him standing in the hallway confused.

'*why does he compliment me so much? maybe he lik-'*

no, of course he doesn't, not in the way i like him anyway. i have to accept that he's just a kind person that likes to make compliments to people out of nowhere. platonically, obviously.

it's impossible for me to pay attention at the history teacher, so i get back to finishing my drawing of the boy that just refuses to leave my mind.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

he just let me stand in the hallway like an idiot and left. why is he acting so odd sometimes?

i mean, i know he's far from being ordinary, but he has some moments when he just randomly hides his face, acts strange or just leaves.

i wish i could understand him completely, but i feel like we're making progress with our friendship. i think he might be my first best friend.

i know many people in this highschool, but i can't really trust any of them with serious stuff. don't get me wrong, i would put my life in any of the gAang member's hands, but aang is the only boy in our group besides me and he's a few years younger than me and pretty immature. not that i'm mister maturity, but compared to him, i'm like a wise old man.

i never realised i needed a boy my age as my best friend, but it turns out i do, and i really want this friendship to work.

i enjoy zuko's presence very much and i feel like i'm getting to know him a little more every day and he's a lovely, but very private person.

the biology lesson starts and i can only hear mumbles coming from the teacher, but no words come together. all i think about is zuko and his precious smile-

"sokka? hey, sokka? earth to sokka!"

suki is waving her hand in front of my eyes, trying to catch my attention.

"what?" i sound a little meaner than i intend.

"where's your head at? i was asking you something and you completely ignored me"

oh boy, she is upset.

"yeah, sorry, i was a bit distracted, what were you saying?"

"can you take the day off at work? let's go on a fancy date and after that maybe we can do something more than cuddle this time" she smirks suggestively and traces lines on my leg with her fingers under the desk.

"sure, we can do that, i'll just text zuko and tell him i can't come to the tea shop"

"perfect" she claps enthusiastically and the teacher tells us to shush.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

my phone buzzes and i open it to see a text:

*ponytail guy:* *"is it ok if i skip work today? special plans with suki ;)"*

an invisible fist clenches around my heart and squeezes. today is friday, so it will be a busy night and he knows it.

or he is suppose to know it, not abandon me for that-

*zuko, that's his girlfriend, of course he will choose her over anything else.*

of course, why would he prefer to serve tea instead of hanging out with his beautiful girlfriend? i can only imagine how he'll be kissing her and doing... um- other things that couples do.

now i'm imagining how it would feel to have his lips on mine... no, that's completely out of the question.

why would he ever kiss me? i'm not even half as pretty as suki. i can tell you, she looks like a literal goddess AND I'M GAY. he would never choose me over her.

aaand, i'm overthinking again. he's not betraying me by wanting to spend time with his girlfriend and i have absolutely no right to be jealous. i respond to his text:

*me* *: "it's fine, don't worry. have fun :)"*

i force myself to add that smiley face in the end so he sees i'm ok with it.

even though i'm not.

&#45;-

the tea shop is swarming with people and i can barely cope with the orders. it would have been a lot easier if sokka-

*no. i can do it. i can so fucking do it, i don't need anyone to help me, i'm an independent bitch.*

some customers get mad because their orders take too long to be ready, but uncle calms everyone down by offering them cupcakes on the house.

i don't know how he does it. how he remains so chill and friendly, even when some clients are rude. i would spill the tea on their heads and kick them out if i were in his position.

but he's not like that. he doesn't have years of bottled up anger inside him like i do, all thanks to my amazing father.

we make it out alive at the end of the night, even without sokka's help and we go home really exhausted.

as i lay on the floor once again, i find myself checking my phone every minute, hoping to see a message from him, but it never comes. there is no specific reason for him to text me, i just wish he did.

i laugh at my own stupidity.

he's having fun with a girl, why on earth would he think of texting me? i'm such an idiot.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

i'm sitting on the edge of the bed, my legs hanging loosely without touching the floor. my face is buried in my hands and i can't even look at suki.

"what is it, sokka? did i do anything wrong?" the girl asks me from under the sheets.

"no, no, that's the problem" i say. "you did everything perfectly and yet... i- i don't know"

"then what's the problem? why are you upset?"

i sigh deeply and put my hair up again in my warrior tail just to keep my hands busy.

"i'm upset at myself. something's probably wrong with me cause i don't think that's how it was supposed to feel"

"you just said-"

"i don't mean physically, i mean emotionally"

she frowns in confusion.

"do you not love me anymore? is it because that thing with jet? because i told you it was a mistake, i was drunk and-"

"no, it's not that" i cut her off. "i'm extremly disoriented, so i don't know if i can explain it to you if i don't understand it myself"

"so what are you saying?" small tears are starting to form in her eyes and her voice is cracking. "you want to break up with me?"

"oh, baby, don't cry, please"

i pull her in a hug and she rests her head on my chest, crying. i run my fingers through her hair quietly.

"you know i still love you. we can just take a little break until i figure out what's not clicking, ok?" i try to comfort her.

she nods, even if she doesn't seem to agree very much with the idea.

"i'll go home now, okay baby?"

she nods once again, i get dressed and walk out the door into the chilly night.

## Chapter 5

**zuko's POV**

in exchange for helping him with his math homework, sokka insisted on teaching me how to play basketball. i wasn't very excited about the idea, but he was, so i couldn't spoil it for him and had to accept.

so here we are, in his back yard, where he has a little improvised basketball field, but with only one basketball hoop. he told me he practices here almost everyday after school and i love the way his face lights up when he talks about something he's passionate about.

"you gotta bend your knees like this and then straighten them when you throw the ball" he explains, giving me a demonstration.

it looks so easy when he does it, but even after multiple attempts, i still fail at introducing the ball in that stupid hole.

"no, no, no, you're doing it all wrong" he says a bit exasperated. "come here"

he places my hands on the ball, staying behind me. he retreats his hands from mine, only to position them on my waist and i feel like i'm dying.

i am very aware of the warmth of his body and i would like that single inch parting us to dissapear.

"ok, bend your knees, keep your hands just like that and now... shoot!"

he moves along with me and i finally score, but all i can concentrate on is his body so fucking close to mine.

"yeees, you did it!" he high fives me and gives me a quick hug.

*oh, how i wish i could sink in his arms for eternity*.

i banish that thought quickly.

"it only took me a thousand tries" i say dissapointed and roll my eyes.

"practice makes perfect, buddy. you can come here when you want and we can train together"

spending even more time with sokka... but doing something i absolutely hate...

well, i guess he's worth the sacrifice.

"sure, i'd like that"

"cool, now let's go eat something, i'm starving!" sokka suggests and starts walking towards the house.

"actually, uncle iroh asked me to paint something on the outside walls of the tea shop and i think i should go do that"

"wait, you paint? why am i learning about this just now?" he asks a bit pouty.

"um- yeah, i like to paint. and draw."

"that's so amazing! can you maybe paint me sometime? it would look great on the living room wall! if it's no bother, of course"

i don't tell him i already made drawings of him in my sketchbook. like, lots of them. it would sound kinda creepy if i think about it. but sokka posing for me willingly? it would take hours. hours of staring at him, analysing every little detail about his body...

"yep, no problem, i can do that"

"great! i'll go grab some sandwiches and we can go to the shop" he adds.

"wait, you're coming too? it will probably take a while, you might get bored" i warn him.

"nah, i can't get bored with you" he flutters his hand in the air and runs inside the house, leaving me standing there, with my cheeks flushed in a light shade of pink.

does he really have to make it so fucking hard not to like him?

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

i think watching zuko paint might be one of my favourite things in the world. we've been here for three hours now and we didn't talk much, because i wanted to let him focus on his work.

i feel priviledged to witness the way he sticks the tip of his tongue out when he's extremely concentrated, or how how he put half of his hair into a little bun. his hands are so steady and i can tell he puts his heart and soul in every strike he makes with the brush.

he painted a field of sunflowers at dawn, mixing the shades of yellow, orange and red perfectly, making it look so real.

"i think you missed a spot here" i tell him and he turns to face me with a confused frown on his face.

"where?"

"here" i say and smudge a bit of blue paint on the tip of his nose.

he clearly didn't notice when i dipped my finger in the bucket full of paint, because he is very surprised.

"oh, you didn't" he says and a subtle smirk appears on his face.

"oh, i did" i chuckle.

he immediately dips his hand in some red paint and touches my arm with the dirty palm, leaving a big stain on my t-shirt.

"you just declared war, sir" i announce him and sink my arms in the nearest bucket, that happenes to be filled with green paint.

i put both my hands on his chest and run them down until his shirt ends.

"oh, you're gonna regret this!" he threatens me.

after that, everything is a compilation of colours, stains and especially laughs. we laughed so hard that it's a miracle we didn't choke on the paint.

some the people passing by us got caught in the fight and shouted at us for covering them in paint, but that only made us laugh harder, until we had tears streaming down our faces.

"oh, i can't even remember the last time i laughed so hard" zuko says after we get tired and decide to make truce, throwing ourselves on the concrete to catch our breaths.

"yeah, me neither" i giggle.

and then it hits me.

"zuko... THE PAINTING"

i jump to my feet to look at it. different colours are spilled on the entire surface of the wall, like it rained with rainbows all over it.

"oh, spirits, it's all ruined because of me, i'm such an idiot, zuko, i'm so, so sorry" i almost start crying, but i look at him and he's smiling.

"sokka, it's not ruined" he shakes his head. "i think it's more special this way"

he looks at me and, as the sun is setting, his golden eyes look like two flames burning in the dark. time stops for a second, but then he breaks the spell.

"only one thing is missing" he adds.

"what's that?"

"dip your palm into the blue paint" he orders me and i comply.

he does the same thing, only with the red one.

"now put it right here" he points to where the sun in the painting is.

i stick my hand where he says and he puts his right next to mine, holding it still so the paint can transfer on the wall. we then remove our hands and admire the result.

"now it's perfect" he says proudly.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

"i love it" sokka states, glancing at the painting with admiration.

"me too" i say, but i look at him instead.

my clothes are soaked in paint and i'm absolutely sure the colours aren't going to wash out, but i don't even want them to.

while i was throwing paint at sokka, i felt something that i haven't experienced in a very long time. happiness. just pure, utter happiness.

i want to remember this moment forever and fortunately, we have the shop's wall as a token to remind us of it every time we come here.

we just sit there and stare at the masterpiece for a while, until a voice breaks our trance.

"sokka? what are you doing all covered in paint? and zuko? what the hell? i thought you would be a lot sadder after you broke up with me, but i see you're completely over it already" suki yells really upset.

wait, did she say they broke up? more specific, sokka broke up with her. maybe he did it because he figured out he liked me, too? am i a bad friend for wanting him to ruin his relationship for my sake?

"suki, wait-" sokka tries to say, but the girl interrupts him.

"you should have at least told me you broke up with me because you're gay"

*what?*

sokka likes boys? my heart fills with hope, even though right now, suki would kill me with her stare if she could.

"what the hell suki, i'm not gay!" sokka shouts and he sounds a bit insulted. "we were just hanging out and i told you we were only taking a break"

*of course he's not gay. spirits, why do i even let myself hope, only to be dissapointed in the end? i'm* *such a clown.*

"yeah, sure, we all know how that will end. you're just an asshole who waited to get in my pants and then leave me the next fucking second" she turns around and walks away.

*what? did they...?*

"suki, it wasn't like that-" he tries to say, but she already left, showing him the middle finger.

"dammit" he scoffs and starts walking around in small circles.

"um- what was that all about?" i try to sound detached when i ask him, but my heart is pounding in my chest.

he keeps cursing under his breath.

"ugh- we uhm- did um- you know..." he mumbles suggestively and i hope he doesn't mean what i think he means.

"did what?"

"we did *it,* you know" he lifts his eyebrows and it's crystal clear.

my stomach turns and i feel knots forming and climbing up to my throat. somehow the image of him with suki gives me the urge to puke.

"and then- zuko, are you ok?" he begins saying, but he sees my face and stops.

"excuse me for a sec-"

i find the nearest bush and throw up everything i ate today. sokka rushes and holds my hair up so i don't get vomit on it. ugh, this is so gross, why do i have to be like this?

"zuko, spirits, are you alright?"

"yes, yes, i'm fine" i wipe my mouth. "i probably drank some spoiled milk for breakfast and the effects are showing now"

this is so embarassing, i don't even know if he believes my shit story.

"right..." he says. "yeah, so after that i told her we should take a break cause it didn't feel right"

"ugh- that's rough, buddy" is all i can think of saying.

i'm shit at dealing with emotional stuff. i can't even handle my own feelings, how am i supposed to help others with theirs?

"yeah, whatever, we were growing apart before that anyways"

"how so?" i wonder.

"i'm not really supposed to tell anyone, cause i promised suki i wouldn't... but you're my best friend, right? i can trust you won't tell anyone"

*i'm his best friend.*

"of course you can" i say a bit overlyexcited despite the situation.

"ok, so she kinda cheated on me a while ago"

"wHaT?" i burst. "how can she cheat on *you?* who was the guy?*"*

"it was jet"

"jEt?!" i'm shocked. "how can she even look at that douchebag?"

"i know, right? but she got drunk at a party and i forgave her after a while, but i didn't forget. it hasn't really been the same since then, to be honest"

there are a few moments of silence because i need to take in the information.

"spirits, that's awful. i'm not really good at this dating shit, but, you know, if she cheated once, she might do it again. i just don't want you to get hurt"

and i mean it. i'm not saying it so he doesn't get back with suki. i only want him to be happy, even if it's not with me, and i will do my best to make sure of that. it's my duty as his best friend, isn't it?

"thank you, zuko, you're a really great friend. and i know, i was thinking about that too. how am i supposed to trust her after something like that?" sokka mumbles. "anyway, i think i should head home now. katara is probably wondering where i am" he says.

i'm a bit sad that he wants to leave when i could spend entire days with him, but i understand, i'm not absurd. he has his own shit to worry about.

"of course" i approve.

we say goodbye and then we part ways, each of us heading to their own house.

## Chapter 6

**zuko's POV**

"zuko, those girls were totally hitting on you!" sokka tells me excited after he pulls me in the back room of the tea shop.

today, uncle had to go and buy supplies for the shop, so he's not here and me and sokka are responsable for making the tea.

"what? what girls?" i ask confused.

"you just took their order! didn't you notice how they were looking at you all flirty?"

"no, they weren't flirting-"

i peek around the corner and see them waving at me and giggling. huh, maybe they were, after all.

"you really are clueless" he laughs. "this is your chance, man!"

"uhm- sokka..." i try to interrupt him, but i stand no chance.

"go ask for their numbers! out of two girls, maybe you'll get one!"

spirits, what the hell is he talking about?

"sokka, you are forgetting something crucial. i don't like girls"

he frowns at me, looking again at the girls sitting at table four.

"what? they are both so pretty, you can choose which one you want"

i rub my temples and take a deep breath.

"yes, sokka, they are very pretty, but i'm gay"

he pauses for a second, no expression in his face.

"i'm sorry, what?" he says.

i have no idea why he seems so shocked, i thought he knew.

"are you for real? you didn't know?" i genuinely ask.

"you never told me!" he tries to defend himself, raising his voice a little.

"i didn't think i had to, i assumed it was obvious!" i almost shout.

i wave my black nails at him and point to the subtle eyeliner i tried to make this morning, as well as the yellow flower resting in my hair.

"wha- ugh, i can't just assume someone's sexuality just because of that! you can be straight and paint your nails, it's not a rule!"

i mean, he's not wrong, but why does he seem slightly mad? is he homophobic? he did sound a bit insulted when suki accused him of being gay.

my legs turn into jelly and i have to lean against a table in order to support my own weight. what if our friendship is ruined now? i thought he knew, i thought he didn't care, and i thought he supported me regardless.

"ok, but i get called slurs almost every day at school, didn't you hear those? didn't you just... connect the dots?" i ask.

"why would i take into consideration what some idiots say? i couldn't assume they were right without you saying it!"

ok, if i'll stay here one more second, i think i'm going to cry.

"whatever" i shake my head and grab a trey with cups of tea on it.

"zuko, wait-" sokka grabs my arm, but i brush him off and proceed walking to serve the stupid tea.

he is mad. he is dissapointed. he will never look at me the same. he won't even want to be my friend again. my heart aches, but i have to put on a smile  
for the customers.

"here you go" i tell the girls and place the cups of tea on the table in front of them.

they giggle, giving eachother suggestive looks and then glaring at me.

"thank you" one of them says. "hey, could i maybe have your phone number?" she adds when i turn around and i'm about to leave.

"you seem like a great girl and i'm flattered, i truly am, it's just... i'm not really into girls" i scratch the back of my neck a little uncomfortable.

"oh, i see" she says. "that's totally cool, thank you for being honest. we just wanted you to know that we think you're really handsome"

my cheeks heat up a little and i can't help but give them half a smile. do they actually find me handsome? they are so sweet and they don't hate me for liking boys!

"you just made my day" i tell them and smile a little more.

"aw, you have dimples! be careful, if you show them too often, people might actually melt and you'll have to live with that on your conscience for the rest of your life" the other girl says and i blush even harder.

their words really touched me, but all i have in my mind is sokka's expression from a few minutes ago. my smile fades and i start zoning out.

"hey, hey, are you alright?" the first girl asks, looking at me with concern.

"yes- no..." i shake my head. "i'm sorry, you're really sweet and i apreciate it, but i don't feel well right now" i explain and then i exit the shop.

*don't cry, zuko. just don't cry.*

i sink my nails in my palm as hard as i can and try to focus on the physical pain, instead of the emotional one, but i would have to break a few bones to even compare the two.

my gaze accidentally falls on the wall me and sokka painted a few days ago, and i feel like someone just decided to cut my heart into pieces with some scissors and chew on every bit. slowly. and painfully.

i lose control over my body for a second and my fist somehow makes contact with the stupid painted wall.

"agh- FUCK" i shout in pain.

at least it wasn't my right hand thanks spirits, so i can still draw. blood starts dripping fast from my knuckles, covering my entire hand.

i'm angry. i'm just so angry. angry at sokka, angry at the pain coming from my hand, angry at myself!

"zuko, are you-" sokka comes out of the shop.

he stares at me and then he sees my hand.

"oh, spirits, zuko, what did you do? let's go to the hospit-"

i push him away.

"i'll go myself, stay at the shop" i say briefly and head to the nearest bus station.

he doesn't come after me.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

*stupid, stupid, stupid, stupid! i'm extremely, incredibly, absolutely fucking stupid!*

why on earth did i react like that when he came out to me? it's not like i have a problem with it, i was just shocked!

i should have put the pieces together, but i'm a complete idiot! and now he thinks i hate him, and i hate *myself* for that.

he was upset and i don't blame him. he punched the wall. there was blood. he got hurt.

*he got hurt because of* ***me.***

it's my fault, everything is my fault. i didn't even go after him, but i couldn't have left jin alone at the tea shop, could i?

UGH, i'm such an idiot!

&#45;-

when i get home, the first thing i do is grab my basketball and start banging it against the concrete. this is the best way i can clear my head and calm down.

"sokka, what's wrong?" katara comes to me concerned.

"nothing. i'm fine"

she doesn't believe me, of course, so she won't give up until i tell her what's on my mind. we sit down on the ground and i start talking.

"did you know zuko was gay?" i ask her.

"um, i thought everyone knew" she shrugs.

i slap my forehead a bit harder than necessary.

"how the hell did everyone figure it out except for me?" i say pouty.

"so this is about zuko" it's a statement, not a question. "did you guys have an argument or something?"

i stare at the ball i'm still holding in my hands. i'm ashamed of myself, but i have to tell 'tara. she's always there for me and, even though she's younger, she's way wiser than me.

"ok, so these girls hit on him at work today and i was encouraging him to talk to them then he told me he was gay and i reacted pretty bad"

"what do you mean? what did you do?"

"i raised my voice, but i didn't mean to. i was just surprised, cause i never even stopped to think if he liked boys or girls. he went outside and punched a wall and he was bleeding pretty bad. he did that because of me"

katata pats me on the back and i lean my head on her shoulder.

"and now he thinks you have a problem with him liking boys?" she asks and i nod. "did you try to explain to him what happened?"

"i tried, 'tara, but he won't talk to me. he didn't let me go to the hospital with him and now he's not even answering my texts"

"did you try calling him?"

"i did. a thousand times. he ignored all my calls. i texted him, asking about his hand, begging him to just hear me out. he didn't even look at the messages, but i know he was online"

"just give him some time, sokka. he'll come around"

i don't even realise i started crying, until katata wipes some tears off my cheeks.

"i don't wanna lose him, 'tara"

that mere thought terrifies me. i can't imagine what i would do without zuko. he's the thing i was always missing, without even realising it.

"you won't" katata assures me. "now, go get some rest. you can talk to him at school, on monday"

i follow her advice and go to my room, but there's no point in trying to fall asleep.

zuko is the only thing occupying my mind.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

i told my uncle everything that happened today. i didn't really have a choice, as he freaked out when he saw my bandaged hand, but i assured him he had nothing to worry about.

the injury on my heart is more serious than the one on my hand anyways.

"perhaps you misunderstood. i'm sure sokka meant no harm" uncle tells me after i finish narrating the events.

"you didn't see the look on his face" i contradict him.

he pours me a cup of tea, takes a sip and then adds:

"if he didn't want to be friends with you anymore, as you said, would your phone be buzzing without stopping for hours? he cares about you, nephew. you just have to let him do that"

uncle iroh is always right and i both hate and love that about him, but i'm not ready to face sokka yet. maybe i overreacted and made a fool out of myself. my abandonment issues are showing.

"i'll go to bed, uncle, thank you for your wisdom"

when i finally jump in bed, i decide to open my phone. i avoid sokka's texts cause i'm too much of a coward to check them, but instead, i read the ones from the gAang's group chat.

***hair loopies:*** *"tomorrow is GAME NIGHT and everyone must come to sokka and i's house"*

***happy bald guy:*** *"yeeey, i LOVE game night!!!!!!"*

***blind bandit:*** *"i hope at least this time i won't DIE of boredom"*

***hair loopies:*** *"toph, how on earth are you writing? you're blind. and game nights are always fun,* *what are you talking about?"*

***blind bandit:*** *"my parents hired someone so type for me. and, sugar queen, if for you making* *flower crowns is fun then count me out of game night"*

***happy bald guy:*** *"woow, toph, your parents have people for everything!"*

***hair loopies:*** *"we will play other games, more exciting ones, i promise"*

***hair loopies:*** *"aang, you always forget toph's parents are stupid rich"*

***bad at giving nicknames:*** *"guys, i don't think i'll come"*

***happy bald guy:*** *"noooooo :((((("*

***hair loopies:*** *"attendance is MANDATORY"*

i think it should be best to announce them i won't be coming either. it would be too awkward.

***sifu hotman:*** *"i can't come either, sorry"*

***blind bandit:*** *"oh no, sugar queen will explode in 3... 2... 1..."*

***hair loopies:*** *"if any of you dosn't show up tomorrow, i'm gonna make you wish you were never* *born"*

um, that's kinda harsh.

***happy bald guy:*** *"if i were you, i would take her seriously"*

i've seen katara waterbend and i really think she can make us miserable in dozen of ways if she puts her mind to it.

***bad at giving nicknames:*** *"ok, i will come, but i won't stay long"*

i guess i don't really have a choice. i have to face my problems at some point anyway. i can't possibly avoid sokka forever so it's best to get this over with as soon as possible.

***sifu hotman:*** *"fine, i'll be there"*

i turn off my phone, but i don't try to sleep. i still have nightmares each time i do, so i've learned to function with only a few hours of rest every night. that's why i'm contantly tired and the dark circles under my eyes live on my face rent free.

i spend the next few hours just staring blankly at the ceiling, thinking of him.

## Chapter 7

**zuko's POV**

"finally! i thought you'd never come and i'd have to hunt you down" katara sighs as soon as she opens the door for me.

she puts on an innocent smile, contradictory to her words and my eyes widen (well, my healthy eye, cause the other one can't move very much). she *can* be scary sometimes.

"sorry, i missed the bus and i had to walk all the way over here" i excuse myself as i'm stepping inside their house.

"ok, ok, whatever, let's go in the living room, everyone's waiting for you"

i comply and as soon as we walk in, my glare meets sokka's eyes. what the hell, i didn't even have to look purposely for him, it just... happened, like i am naturally drawn to him.

"hey guys" i mumble, taking my eyes off sokka cause i can't handle the tension.

they are all sitting on the floor, in a circle. suki is next to sokka, then aang and toph. i take a seat next to the blind girl and she punches me in the arm as soon as i do that. that's how she shows affection, so i have admit i don't mind it.

"finally, sparky! if you came 5 minutes later, i think sugar queen would have killed someone" toph jokes and katara gets very offended, but brushes it off quickly and takes a seat between me and suki.

i ocasionally lay my eyes on sokka and every single time, he's staring back at me, so i withdraw instantly.

"can we talk, zuko?" he asks me while katara is explaining the rules to whatever game she wants us to play.

"shut up, you can talk after this" his sister snaps at him.

she really is invesed in this game night, she isn't just joking around.

"ok, so the person spinning the bottle will ask a question, or give a dare to the person it lands on. as simple as that" 'tara tells us. "aang, you're first"

aang spins the bottle and it lands on suki. she chooses truth.

"is it true that you still have feelings for sokka?"

both her and sokka blush and avoid each other's glare.

"no" she says briefly.

"that's a lie" toph says and smirks.

sometimes i forget she can feel hartbeats and tell if someone is being honest or not.

"screw you, toph!" suki shouts at her.

"hey, the game is called TRUTH or dare, so i'm here to make sure everyone tells the truth" she shruggs and puffs, casting her bangs away from her eyes.

suki rolls here eyes and hugs her knees embarrased.

"your turn to spin it, toph" katara mentions and toph obeys.

"aang. truth or dare?"

"truth" aang says.

"why does nobody choose dare?" toph scoffs. "do you have a crush on katara?"

aang turns so red, even his ears heat up and he starts mumbling something undecipherable.

"c'mon twinkletoes, just say it" toph presses him.

"ugh- um- i- maybe- a little" aang stutters.

karata tries to hide her flushed cheeks by pulling her hair over her face, but it's not working very well.

"he's telling the truth" toph says. "and i think sugar queen has a thing for twinkletoes, too"

"how about you mind your own business, toph?" katara defends herself.

toph raises her arms in defeat.

"hey, i'm just trying to play cupid cause it's obvious you like eachother and you're idiots for not being together"

"whatever, suki, you're next" katara changes the subject, to aang's dissapointment and passes the bottle to suki.

sokka has been awfully quiet the whole time, which is unlike him. usually, he would constantly make jokes about anything, but not today apparently.

suki spins the bottle and it lands on me.

"truth or dare, zuko?" she asks me.

"dare"

she thinks about it for a moment.

"i dare you to tell us how you got that scar"

"what the hell, suki?" sokka yells at her and it's the first thing he said tonight.

i feel my already pale skin losing every bit of colour it had and my ears start buzzing.

"excuse me" i say and get up and rush to the bathroom.

i lock the door and press my back against it, letting myself slide until i reach the cold floor.

dark memories are clouding my mind and i realise they will never see past my stupid scar. when they look at me, that's all they see, and i can't blame them.

gosh, i wish i could dissapear into thin air so i wouldn't have to deal with anyone ever again.

i suddenly hear some knocks on the door and the noise breaks me from my thoughts.

"zuko! zuko, please open up!"

it's sokka. he hasn't talked to me utill now and despite that, here he is, trying to pick up the pieces of my scattered heart.

"go away, sokka" i almost whisper and i'm not sure he heard me.

"no, zuko, i'm not going anywhere so please open the door"

should i? should i not? i can't stay locked up in here forever. i unlock the door and he enters, closing it back as soon as he does.

he doesn't say anything, he just sits next to me on the floor and pulls me into a hug. at first, i don't return the hug because i'm taken by surprise, but after a few seconds, i let myself sink into his arms, enjoying his warmth and scent.

we just sit like that for what feels like forever and, at the same time, just a few seconds. time passes weirdly when i'm around sokka.

"i'm so sorry for everything" he says at last and breaks the embrace.

"you don't have to-" i try to cut in, but he doesn't let me.

"just shut up for once and listen to me" he interrupts me firmly. "i think you got the wrong idea at the tea shop. i have absolutely nothing against you liking boys, you're still my best friend, no matter what. i support you and i was an idiot, i didn't see that coming, even if i should have, and that's why i reacted the way i did. i hope you can forgive me"

i play with my fingers and chip some of my nail polish nervously.

"i'm sorry too, sokka. i totally overreacted. i should have listened to what you had to say. i should have answered your texts and calls, but i was too scared because i didn't want you to see me differently just because i'm gay. after what happened with my father-"

i stop. this is the first time i've ever mentioned my father and sokka knows it too. i can see it imprinted on his face.

"what happened with your father?" he asks coldly.

i shake my head.

"it doesn't matter" i brush it off and he doesn't insist. "the point is, i'm terrified of people leaving me out of nowhere, so i try to push them away first, to avoid being dissapointed. that's why i act the way that i do sometimes and i'm trying to work on it, but i don't know if i can-"

"zuko, i would never, ever leave you. you could literally kill a person in front of me and i would help you hide the body without a second thought"

i've been staring into his eyes since we started talking and i don't think i'll be able to break the eye contact soon.

"i know you wouldn't hurt me. at least not on purpose, but i find it hard to fully put my trust in someone and even if i do, i constantly have a voice in my head telling me that i'm gonna be abandoned and nobody actually cares about me"

i have no idea where this wave of sincerity came from. i've never exposed my feelings like this to anybody. i've never been so weak in front of another person.

"oh, zuko..." sokka murmurs and pulls me closer to him.

i wrap my arms around his waist and he rests his chin on my head, placing a small kiss on it first. it sends shivers down my whole body. i feel safe in his arms, like nothing bad can happen to me while he's here.

"zuko, you know what i'm terrified of?" he asks me after we sit in silence for a while.

i shake my head, not letting go of him yet. i want to hold onto this moment for a little longer.

"i'm terrified of losing you" his words make my skin tingle and my heart ache in a good way. "when you didn't reply to any of my texts, i thought i lost you forever, and that drove me crazy. you're my best friend and i can't imagine what i'd do if you weren't"

*best friend.*

*friend.*

do you kiss your friends on the head? do you hug your friends on the bathroom floor for so long? i have no idea, i've never actually had a friend before, so i couldn't tell.

"i was afraid of losing you too. i was petrified when i assumed you would never want to talk to me again" i confess.

he sighs.

"so, does this mean we're good?" he asks.

"yeah, we're good" i nod and we distance ourselves from one another.

i have gotten used to his body against mine, so now that we broke the embrace, every cell in my body longs for his touch. why is he doing this to me?

"let's get back, i think people might think we drowned in the toilet or something" sokka suggests and we both chuckle.

when we enter the living room, the chattering stops abruptly.

"if you two lovebirds are done smootching, can we go on playing something? i'm bored as hell" toph states.

*lovebirds?*

"we weren't smooching, toph" sokka rolls his eyes and we go back to our seats, across from eachother.

"whatever you say, snoozles"

"ok, let's continue" katara says enthusiastically.

"if my first question bothered you, can i ask another, zuko?" suki asks and i nod.

what can be worse than questions about my past?

"ok, do you have feelings for sokka?" she raises an eyebrow, not taking her eyes of me.

apparently, there *are* worse questions. ok zuko, just try to play it cool, pretend you don't know what she's talking about.

"um, he's my friend, so of course i care about him" i say.

"you know that's not what i mean" she cuts me off.

i feel my heart in my throat and i think that if i try to say something right now, it will come out as a squeak.

"no, i don't like him like *that,* suki" i gather all my strenght to sound convincing.

i am totally aware that toph can feel my heart pounding so hard, it's almost jumping out of my chest, so when i think she's about to call me a liar, i squeeze her arm just a little, to give her a hint. fortunately for me, she gets it.

"he's telling the truth" toph covers for me and i'll be forever grateful for that.

"no, you're both lying!" suki bursts. "i saw the way you look at sokka, i'm not blind!"

"ouch" toph says, but she doesn't seem offended by her remark.

"what the hell, suki? why do you always have to be such a bitch?" sokka asks her.

her jaw drops, she wasn't expecting him to say something like that to her.

"me? why do you always have to defend that freak? what, you've known him for like a month or something and now he's more important to you than i am? don't you find it weird that he never told us anything about him? we literally have no idea why he came to our highschool, why he doesn't live with his parents or how the fuck he got that *thing* on his face!"

"get out" sokka says calmly, to everybody's surprise.

i can tell he is trying hard not to let out his anger.

"i was leaving anyways" suki rolls her eyes and bursts out the door.

an awkward silence has settled in the room and nobody knows what to say.

"she's right, guys. you know nothing about me" i say at last.

"no, she's not. don't feel pressured to tell us anything you're not ready to" katara says and looks at me with understsnding.

"just ignore her, she's frustrated that i broke up with her" sokka mumbles angrily.
