"we don't need to know about your past, sparky. we know you from the present and that's all that matters to us" toph tries to encourage me.

"yeah, and if you ever feel like sharing anything with us, we will always be here for you" aang adds.

i'm overwhelmed with emotions. i never thought i'd find people to accept me and be patient with me. i feel blessed right now.

"what did i do to deserve you guys?" my voice cracks.

i cover my face with my hands so i can stop the tears that are trying to escape. i'm even shaking a little, but i won't let them see it.

"group hug!!!!" aang shouts full of excitement and before i have time to protest, i feel many hands wrapping around me, forming the warmest hug i've ever had.

## Chapter 8

**zuko's POV**

"why did nobody think of telling me *game night* means sleepover? i didn't bring my pyjamas" i say little irritated.

we're at the dining table right now, eating something katara and aang cooked together.

"we kinda forgot this is your first game night" aang mumbles.

i roll my eyes, but i'm not mad at them, it would be stupid if i was.

"it's no problem, zuko, you can just take some of sokka's clothes" katara suggests.

"of course, i'll give them to you after dinner" sokka approves with his mouth full of meat.

if anyone else would have talked with their mouth stuffed like that, i would have found it annoying, but it's sokka, so it's just cute.

"thanks, buddy" i say and take a bite of my stake.

after we all finish eating, we decide to play monopoly and, even if i'm not really into this particular game, i don't hate it.

it was really fun to watch katara be so invested in winning, toph mocking her for it and stealing all her properties, aang trying to stop them from arguing and sokka bragging about the amount of money he made during the game.

i just sat there and analysed each one of them, unable to believe that for the first time in my life, i'm surrounded by real friends, doing normal activities that teenagers do.

"ha! i win! i'm the best! i kicked your ass, sugar queen!" toph shouts excitedly.

katara pouts and starts collecting all the pieces from the game and putting them back in their box.

"yeah, yeah, you were just lucky" she rolls her eyes.

"at least we all had fun" aang shruggs and katara loosens up a little.

"you're right" she agrees.

"oh, guys, i forgot something" sokka adds.

we all look simultaneously at him, curious to find out what he is talking about.

"dad's bedroom is still in renovation so two of us will have to share a room. if it's ok, i'll share with zu-" he mentions.

only the idea of sleeping in the same bed as him drives me crazy.

"i'll sleep with sparky!" toph interrupts him and grabs my arm.

*what the hell?*

katara widens her eyes and looks at her, even if the blind girl can't see her stare.

"uhm, are you sure?" sokka asks a little confused.

she nods her head.

"don't worry, if he tries anything, i'll beat the crap out of him" toph assures everyone.

"toph, what could i even try? i'm gay and you're 15" i ask a bit offended.

"yeah, yeah, that's what they all say" she says.

"ok then, it's pretty late, so i'll show you your rooms" katara says and we all scatter around the house.

toph and i's room is right across from sokka's, which is somehow both comforting and intimidating at the same time.

"here are some pants" sokka says and hands me a pair of grey sweats. "do you sleep shirtless or do you want a t-shirt too?"

"t-shirt, please"

i don't feel comfortable sleeping naked, even if it's really hot, especially if others are around.

"right, right, i forgot. toph. it would be awkward and inapropriate, even if she can't actually see you"

i didn't even think about that, but he's totally right. he hands me a dark blue t-shirt.

"you could have slept here, i don't know what got over toph. she must really like you, cause she never shares a room with anyone" sokka adds.

i have no idea why toph did that either, but in the next few seconds, i am thankful that she did.

"i think i should hit the sack or otherwise my eyes will fall off from their sockets" sokka states while he takes his shirt off.

of course he sleeps shirtless. with a body like that, it's understandable.

*is it really hot in here or am i just having a gay panic?*

i think both options are correct.

*don't stare, don't stare, don't make it awkward zuko, friends often change in front of eachother,* *don't make a big deal out of it.*

"sparky! come here!" toph yells from our room and i realise i was holding my breath so i let all the air out of my lungs.

"i- uhm- i guess i sohould- ugh- i'll just go" i mumble like an idiot.

"okay, if you need anything, you know where to find me" sokka smiles at me and i'm certain that if i don't get out of here soon, my legs will turn into jelly completely.

i nod, letting him know i understand, because i'm unable to form any word.

"good night, zuko"

"you too" i manage to spit out and i rush outside.

i shut the door behind me once i enter my room and i sustain myself by leaning my back on the door.

"spirits, sparky, you seriously have no control over your body" toph remarks.

"*really?* i didn't notice!" i say sarcastically and roll my eyes.

"you should thank me, so don't be a dick. if i didn't offer to share a room with your crusty ass, you would have died of a heart attack in his bed"

she's right. she always is, and it's annoying.

"yeah, thanks for that" i scratch my head.

"do you have any idea how fast your heart is beating? i'm surprised it hasn't exploded yet"

i take a sit on the bed, next to her.

"yes, toph. i am very aware of that, you don't have to rub it in my face"

i sigh and then change into sokka's clothes. i don't have to hide my body from toph because obviously, she's blind.

the pants are slightly too short, but not enough to bother me and his t-shirt fits me pretty well. i pull the material up to my nose and i take a deep breath. it smells like him, mint, lemon, and a faded scent of rain.

it feels like having him right here, next to me-

"are you done sniffing his clothes like a weirdo, or are you gonna do that all night?" toph breaks the silence that i didn't mind.

"i wasn't-"

"shut up" she cuts me off. "when will you tell him?"

"tell him what?" i frown, putting some hair behind my ear.

"that you're mad in love with him, dumbass"

"oh, i won't tell him" i say briefly.

toph slaps her own forehead a little too hard, and then punches me.

"ouch, why did you-"

"cause you're an idiot. you're lucky snoozles is clueless, because you're not very good at hiding your feelings"

i pout and keep rubbing my arm in the place toph hit me.

"i'm trying, ok? and what the hell do you want me to do?"

"uhm, i don't know? be honest with him, maybe?"

i shake my head, and then i remember she can't see me.

"it would ruin our friendship, toph"

"you don't know that for sure" she states.

"yes, i do. he likes *girls.* do i look like a girl to you?"

"i can't see, you idiot" she interrupts me.

"oh, yeah, sorry" i apologise embarrased by my stupid mistake, but she doesn't seem to be offended by it.

"how are you so sure he likes *only* girls?" toph asks.

i stop to think about it for a second. i don't *know* he only likes girls, but wouldn't he have told me if he was bi or pan or anything else?

"there never was any hint of him liking boys"

"maybe he doesn't even realise it himself" she says, lifting her shoulders.

i scoff.

"i can't put our friendship at risk just for the slim chance of him *not* being straight!"

"would you shut up about the ruined friendship crap? if your bond is strong enough, this surely won't break it. and who the hell is straight anymore?"

"yes it would destroy it, toph. it would make it extremly awkward for both of us" i say firmly.

"well, i think you're just too scared of being rejected" she raises an eyebrow.

maybe i am. is that unusual? no, i don't think so.

"ok, so what? if i know for sure he doesn't feel the same way, i won't be able to mentain the same attitude when i'm around him, even if he wouldn't have a problem with it. what i don't know, won't kill me, right?"

*right?*

she shuts her eyes in annoyance and starts rubbing her temples.

"and for how long do you think you can keep your shit together? if you continue to blush and stutter whenever you talk to him, he'll notice something's off. snoozles's not *that* dumb"

dammit, she does have a point.

"ugh, why is this so *hard?*" i groan and cover my face with a pillow.

"look, sparky, i can't force you to do anything, and i won't tell anyone until you're ready, but i think you should give it a try. if he doesn't like you like that, you can at least move on"

she is right once again. i would have to get over him, but in order to do that, i'd have to cut off every contact with him.

and i'd rather have him as my friend than as a stranger, even if it hurts. even if that means i have to see him in a relationship with someone else.

"i'll think about it, toph" i tell her. "who would have thought you'd give me relationship advice and discuss feelings?"

"if you tell anyone about this, i'll break you in half"

she sounds very serious. she really wants to keep her badass apparence, huh?

"fair enough" i shrugg. "you should get some sleep, it's really late"

i turn off the light, i take my pillow, get out of bed, and lay on the floor.

"what the hell are you doing?" toph asks me.

"you should have the bed, i don't mind sleeping on the floor"

it wouldn't be the first time, but i don't tell her that.

"and i thought you can't be a bigger idiot. get on the bed and quit fooling around" she orders me.

"i just wanted to be a gentleman, spirits" i say and then comply.

i get under the blanket, and so does toph. she moves around until she finds the right position.

"good night, sparky" she whispers. "and don't touch me unless you want to have your arms separated from the rest of your body"

does she really have to threaten me every two minutes?

"you know i won't, toph. good night"

after a while, i can tell toph fell asleep, as she's snoring a little, but i'm not bothered. my mind is crawled with millions of thoughts anyway, so it's not like i can sleep.

i analyse closely every outcome that my confession to sokka could have.

what if he *does* feel the same? i know i'm hurting myself, but my imagination doesn't give a shit about that.

i see an image of me, telling him i like him as more than as a friend. he smiles in relief, also confessing his love to me. he takes my hand, i feel shivers all over my body. we hug, both of us crying, wishing we would have done this sooner.

i imagine him kissing me, but i force myself to stop, because it physically hurts that it won't be able to come true.

a cold tear appears in the corner of my eye, and soon crosses my heated cheek.

&#45;-

"sparky! hey, sparky!" a voice wakes me from my sleep.

it takes me a moment to understand where i am and what is happening around me.

"mhm, what, toph?" i ask with a sleepy voice. "what time is it?"

a speck of light is breaching through the curtain and i rub my eyes.

"i don't know, but you were twitching and mumbing something about your father"

"oh, sorry, that was just because of my nightmares"

this is so embarassing. i grab my phone from the nightstand and turn it on. it's 6:24. i slept around 3 hours. it's enough, i guess.

"do you have them often?" she asks me.

"don't worry about it, go back to sleep, it's extremly early" i say and get out of bed.

toph groans and i can tell she's already half asleep, so she doesn't ask where i am going.

i head to the bathroom, wash my face with some cold water to banish the tiredness and then stare at myself in the mirror.

i'm not really used to look at myself, because at home i don't have a mirror in my bathroom anymore.

i smashed it when i had a mental breakdown a while ago. i just couldn't stand seeing the person that right now is staring back at me.

the dark circles under my eyes make me look like i just rose from the dead, my hair is tangled and i'm as pale as a ghost.

i won't even mention the wrinkly red skin covering almost half of my face.

spirits, i'm such a clown for even *thinking* about the possibility of sokka liking me when i look like this.

i rush out of the bathroom and go downstairs. i gotta keep myself busy for a few hours, until everyone wakes up.

## Chapter 9

**sokka's POV**

when i enter the kitchen, i am surprised to lay my eyes on zuko, as it's only 8 am.

"morning, sunshine" i mumble with a raspy voice, as i woke up 5 minutes ago.

he clearly didn't hear me walk in, because he flinches and some flour jumps in the air, covering him in it. what is he doing with flour?

"ugh- hey, sokka" he says and rubs his t-shirt (well, my t-shirt) in attempt to get rid of the flour. "i didn't see you there"

*damn, he looks really good in my clothes...*

um, where did that thought come from? whatever, i will choose to ignore it for now.

"why are you up so early?" zuko asks me while i pour myself a glass of orange juice.

i shrug and take a sip.

"i was thirsty. what are you doing there?" i point at the bowls scattered around the table, the eggs, sugar and other groceries.

"i wanted to make you guys some pancakes" he starts twirling a lock of hair around his index.

"you don't have to do that. since when are you up?"

"6" he says briefly.

"6!? why? didn't you sleep well?" i ask curiously.

"uhm- i don't usually sleep much, so..."

i scowl and think about how much i enjoy sleeping. how does he even function?

"it's not good for you, you should get proper rest"

he grins a little.

"don't worry about it, sokka" he brushes it off. "so, are you gonna help me make these stupid pancakes or what?"

he has the talent of changing the subject radically when it comes to talking about himself. damn, is he ever gonna open up to me?

"fine" i sigh. "i'll help you cause clearly, you have no idea what you're doing. you can't mix the flour and the oil first, what were you thinking?" i point at the yellow bowl that's in front of him.

i grab it, pour some milk and start mixing everything up.

"i've never made pancakes before, ok? spirits, why do you have to be so judgy?" he rolls his eyes dramatically and tries to mentain a straight face.

he can't do that for more than 3 seconds and soon, we both burst into laughter.

"ok, break those eggs and pour them in here" i instruct him and he complies.

he cracks them so tactless, that some pieces of the egg shells fall into the white and yolk that he was supposed to separate.

"um, zuko- i know egg shells have calcium, but i don't think we should eat them"

he sighs in annoyance.

"dammit, i can't even do this simple task!" he drops the entire egg shell into the bowl and crumbles on the floor.

his hands are smeared with yolk, but he drags them over his face anyway. i take a seat next to him and put my arm around him.

"hey, buddy, it's fine. the first time i cooked anything, i almost burned down the house. i was lucky katara was around and was able to waterbend the fire fast enough"

he puffs, but i can see a faint smile on his face. when i look at him, i start chuckling.

"what? why are you laughing?" zuko asks me and his only brow frowns in confusion.

"you covered your face with yolk, dummy" i inform him and he giggles a little too.

i get up and drag him along with me, leaving him with no choice but to stand up. i sink my hand in the bag of flour and put some on his face.

"here, now we can turn your face into a pancake!"

i think this wasn't the best moment to do that, because his face is emotionless and i'm not sure what he's going to do next. he was already frustrated and instead of helping him, i-

my thoughts are interrupted by some flying flour hitting my chest.

"are you sure you want to start this war once again?" he asks with a devilish smirk.

my mind travels to our last "fight", when he was painting the tea shop's wall. do i want to repeat that? do i want to see zuko being playful and full of life once again? hell yeah.

"haha, yes, i am sure, but are *you* ready to get your ass kicked again?" i raise an eyebrow, not breaking eye contact with him.

i see a barely perceptible spark in his gloden eyes and that fills me with a weird wave of energy that i decide not to think much of.

"we'll see about that" he grins.

we rush to opposites sides of the table, ducking and throwing flour when we think the other isn't paying attention.

after a while, zuko runs for his life and hides behind the couch in the middle of the chamber, as the kitchen is linked to the living room.

"you know you can't get away that easily!" i warn him and follow his lead.

i don't bother to watch where i'm stepping and when i'm about to throw flour on his pretty little face, i slip.

somehow, zuko reaches me before i touch the ground, but he can't stop me from falling, so somehow i end up on top of him.

we're both panting, covered in flour from head to toe, but for whatever reason, none of us moves. we just stare at eachother without letting out a single word.

our faces are only a few inches apart, and i could just-

*what the hell, sokka, where is this coming from? stop with these intrusive thoughts, zuko is your* *friend, you can't just randomly kiss him!*

spirits, why am i still thinking about this?!

"uhm- sorry-" i manage to say and get off him.

why do i feel my mouth so dry and my heart pounding really fast all of a sudden?

he stares into space for a few seconds, but then he gets up too and dusts himself.

"we should probably clean this mess before the others wake up" zuko suggests and i look around me for the first time.

it's a disaster. why didn't i think this through? the flour isn't gonna wash out very easily from the couch and it probably even got under the furniture.

"what the fuck, sokka?" 'tara appears in the doorstep, shouting angrily.

me and zuko look at eachother, then we both move our stare to katara.

"spirits, why are you idiots yelling so loud? you interrupted my beauty sleep" toph enters the room as well.

"zuko and sokka spread flour all over the room! what are you, 5? clean up immediately!" katara orderes.

"yeah, yeah, we will, mom" i attempt to make a joke.

i only realise that i shouldn't have said that when katara turns around and leaves the room with watering eyes.

"shit- 'tara, i'm sorry, you know i didn't-" i shout so she can hear me, but she doesn't come back.

i slap my forehead. i'm such an idiot, dammit.

"i'll go after her, snoozles, you clean this shit up" toph says and exists the room.

i grab a mop and start wiping the floor nervously.

"what was that about?" zuko asks.

i assume i should tell him, but it's still hard to talk about it, even though many years have passed since it happened.

"mom died a few years ago" i state.

zuko stiffens and his eyes become sadder.

"i'm so, so sorry to hear that, sokka... um-"

i can tell he wants to ask how she died, but he's not sure if it is appropriate or not.

"cancer" i tell him and he nods in understanding. "katara was there when she... passed. it affected her a lot and it's still a sensitive topic for both of us" i clarify.

we keep on cleaning in silence for a while and it's odd how only minutes ago we were laughing our asses off like there was no tomorrow.

"i know how you feel" zuko breaks the silence. "my mom died too"

my heart skips a beat. this is the first serious thing he's ever told me about his family.

"can i ask how?"

i know i'm forcing my hand, but i don't know when i'll get him to open up again so i'm trying to get as much as i can from him while i have the chance.

he pauses for what seems like forever and i can see his fists clenching, as well as his jaw.

"suicide" the pain in his voice is astronomical.

she killed herself. what in the world could have possibly pushed her to do something so drastic? to leave her child alone in the world?

"i- i found her- in- in the attic" zuko literally chokes on the words and it breaks my heart in half to see him like this.

i think he's about to crumble on the floor, but i rush to him and pull him into a hug. he soon wraps his arms around my waist and buries his face in the crook of my shoulder, letting out a small squeak.

i rest my chin on his head and inhale his familiar scent. even if he's dressed with my clothes, there's still a faint trace of cinnamon and vanilla that i just love.

he radiates warmth, maybe because he's a firebender, or maybe i'm just imagining it, but it makes me not want to ever let him go.

"it's ok, it's gonna be ok" i whisper.

this is the only thing that comes in my mind and it's pathetic. i know it's not ok and he knows it too, but he doesn't push me away. instead, he pulls me even closer and i can't complain about it.

"we should finish cleaning" he mumbles and his words get lost in my shirt.

"yeah, we should" i agree, but none of us seems eager to break the embrace.

finally, we both pull away and he brushes his hair with one hand and sniffles.

"thanks, sokka. i really needed that" he smiles shyly.

i needed it too. i feel like i'm recharged with energy after i hugged him. he doesn't look like it, but he gives some of the best hugs i've ever had.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

we made those pancakes after all the mess we made, in order to make katara feel a little better. chocolate really is the cure for everything.

we're all sitting on the floor, stuffing our mouths with pancakes and joking around like we usually do.

i'm still thinking about the hug i shared with sokka an hour ago. i can't seem to get it out of my head. if i close my eyes, i can still feel his touch and his scent. i literally melted in his arms, ok?

i don't ever let myself be vulnerable in front of others, but... it just happened. i can't believe his mother is dead too. i never stopped to think about it.

i know his father works in a different country, i just assumed... i don't know... that his mom is there too? i didn't pay much attention to this aspect, to be honest.

i felt bad for sharing my experience when he was talking about his. i didn't want him to think i am self centered or that i'm trying to overshadow his trauma, i just wanted him to feel understood.

"hey, zu, you have something-" sokka gestures to the corner of his mouth.

i imitate him and wipe the left corner of mine.

"no, no" he shakes his head. "here, let me"

when his thumb makes contact with my bottom lip, my lungs stop functioning once again and, even worse, his finger lingers there and he forgets to remove it.

here i am, sitting with my mouth half open, my eyes glued to his and my heart growing rapidly in my chest.

he's gonna put me in the hospital one day, i swear.

i see his jaw clenching and his eyes wander off on my lips for a split second, then they return, restoring the eye contact.

i swallow, as if i'm trying to push the heart that's stuck in my throat back to my chest.

"ok, you're good now" he clears his throat and moves away his stare and his finger.

i'm too frozen to speak and i stare into nothing, just like when he fell on top of me earlier this morning.

i glance at toph and i can see her smirking behind her long bangs.

## Chapter 10

**zuko's POV**

i thought history class was never going to end, but the bell finally rang, awaking me from daydreaming.

i head straight to the bathroom and i find it empty, to my content. i can enjoy some moments of privacy, while i splash some cold water on my face.

i can barely keep my eyes open, as in the last few days, my thoughts have been completely occupied by sokka.

he's the first thing that comes into my mind when i wake up, and i think only of him when i'm at school, unaware of what the teachers are saying.

besides, i struggle to fall asleep because i'm imagining unrealistic scenarios with me and him as soon as i lay my head on the pillow.

every. single. day.

it's really exhausting. i wish i could just stop having a crush on him, but i can't help it. i often think of the chance of him liking me as more than a friend, but that only happens in movies. in real life, the feelings are almost always one sided, so there's no point in hoping for a different outcome.

suddenly, my reflection in the mirror is not the only one staring back at me. i turn around when i realise i'm not alone anymore. jet is here too.

he doesn't say a word, he just fixes me with his eyes and i don't know what i'm supposed to do.

when i'm about to leave this extremely awkward situation, he pushes himself against me, pinning me to the sink and...

he tries to... kiss me?

i use all my strenght to get him off me right before he gets to touch my lips with his.

"what... the... fuck!?" i stutter both angry and confused. "what the hell are you doing? is this some kind of joke?!"

he starts walking around in circles, scraching the top of his head nervously.

"i think- i-... i think you were right" he stops pacing around and just stares at me.

"i was right? about what?"

"i think i like boys and i might have a little crush on you" jet confesses.

ok, i did *not* expect that.

"jet, if this is a prank to mock me again, please just cut it off, i'm not in the mood"

he shakes his head frantically.

"no, i'm serios, you idiot. i don't know what to do" he states.

"uhm- about what?"

"about liking you, dumbass. how the fuck did that happen, you're an idiot and i'm supposed to be straight!"

he covers his face with his palms and sits on the floor. i don't take a seat next to him because that floor is dirty as fuck, i don't even want to step on it with my shoes.

"look, we both know you're an asshole, but it's ok if you're gay, who even gives a shit?" i shrugg. "but dude, haven't you heard of *consent*? you can't just kiss people randomly without asking!"

jet looks at me embarassed, tapping his foot relentlessly.

"yeah, sorry about that... i- i don't know what took over me..." he mumbles. "what am i supposed to do now? how will i tell my friends, my family? what if they don't want to have anything to do with me anymore?"

ugh, so many questions and i'm shit at comforting others. i gotta think about what uncle iroh would say and how he would handle the situation.

"fuck them" i say bluntly.

ok, uncle iroh would definetely *not* say that, but i can't compare his wisdom with my stupid ass.

jet furrows his brows, waiting for a further explanation.

"yeah, just screw them. if they don't accept you, just dump their asses in the trash can" i add.

he smiles a little and a few moments of silence pass.

"how are you so openly gay?" he asks me all of a sudden.

i lift my shoulders and inspect the room, as if i could find my answer written on the walls of this filthy bathroom.

"i just know i can't change anything about it, so why fight it? because some stuck up jerks say it's wrong? hell no, just do what makes you happy, you can't live your entire life trying to live up to other people's expectations" i say.

jet looks at his hands for a moment, like he's taking in all my words.

"you know, you were supposed to make me stop liking you, not to make me do so even more"

i blush a little and start playing with my shirt nervously. nobody ever told me they had a crush on me and it's somehow overwhelming. sadly, he's not the person i wanted to hear this from.

"look, jet, i bet that underneath that asshole, there is a good person hiding, but i kinda like someone at the moment..." i try to not make it sound rude.

*not me being loyal to someone i'm not and probably won't ever be in a relationship with...* *&#42;internal screaming&#42;*

he gets up and nods, biting his bottom lip.

"oh- i- of course- i- i understand" he mumbles. "and... thank you for not ignoring me, even though i was horrible to you since you came to this school"

"it's ok, jet, don't worry about it" i flutter my hand in the air to banish the idea. "if you ever need someone to talk to, i'm here for you"

he thanks me with a faded smile.

i know, i know, he was a total jerk to me, but when i first realised i was gay, i had nobody to talk to about it and i struggled. like, a lot, so i'm not gonna let him go through the same thing that i did all alone.

"wait-" he calls me when i'm about to get out of the bathroom. "how did your parents react when you told them? i'm terrified to tell them, i'm sure they will freak out"

my heart starts pounding in my chest, as my memories make their way to the surface. i don't know why i decide to be honest with him.

"my father burned my face and then kicked me out a few years later"

his eyes widen and his jaw drops, leaving his mouth half open.

"oh, spirits, i didn't know... i'm so sorry i ever made fun of you for..."

"yeah, well, i hope you won't go through the same thing" i say. "oh, and please, don't tell anyone, i don't even know why i told you"

"sure, sure, my lips are sealed"

he imitates zipping his mouth and locking it with an invisible key.

"thank you"

i leave the bathroom and head to lunch.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

why can't i stop thinking about him? why has he been on my mind 24/7 since Game Night? what the hell is wrong with me? i wonder what he's doing right now... why isn't he here yet?

"sokka! are you even listening to me?" katara snaps her fingers in front of my eyes, bringing me back to reality.

"yeah, yeah, sorry" i mumble.

we're at lunch and katara started talking a few minutes ago, but i haven't got a clue about what. everyone but zuko is here. well, and suki, who hasn't talked to any of us since that night and is now eating with the cheerleaders.

"and then i asked what was wrong with my outfit. she said my skirt was too short and it was *too* *distracting* for the boys" katara says frustrated.

"what the hell? that's so stupid" zuko appears out of nowhere and takes a seat next to me, throwing his backpack on the ground carelessly.

i don't know why, but i instantly smile when i see him. he grins back at me, but then moves his eyes on 'tara.

"that's really unfair!" aang adds. "a boy in my class often wears tank tops and you can easily see his pecs. nobody ever says anything about it"

"i think you should come in your underwear tomorrow just to spite them" toph suggests.

"no, that would probably get her expelled" zuko says and he seems to be searching for solutions.

he has his elbows on the table, his fingers interwined and his chin rested on his hands. i notice how every time he thinks profoundly, a little wrinkle appears in the corner of his mouth.

"i know!" he suddenly exclaims, slapping the table in excitement and i realise i was staring at him all this time.

everyone turns their heads towards him, cause they have been talking about spirits know what for the last minutes, while he still thought about katara's issue.

"we get as many boys as we can to come to school wearing skirts. they won't tell them it's too distracting, so they will see what they're doing is just purely misogynistic!" zuko explains his idea.

katara's eyes widen, toph smirks and aang smiles from ear to ear.

"you're a genius" i tell him and our eyes meet for a second, sending me shivers all over my body.

*why does he give me shivers? he shouldn't do that.*

"zuko, i swear i could kiss you right now, i love the idea!" 'tara squeaks and starts jumping around.

an image of katara and zuko kissing pops up into my mind and for some reason, my stomach starts tying itself into knots.

"ayy, i'm so excited!" aang says. "i've never worn a skirt, but i think i would rock it!"

we all giggle at his enthusiasm.

"you totally would, buddy" i assure him. "i'll text every boy i know and make an announcement on the highschool group chat. we can plan everything and put it in action in a few days"

"that's perfect. we can go thrifting to get skirts before work" zuko suggests.

"great, it's settled then" i say.

i see aang gets a little pouty, so i ask him what's wrong.

"i can't come today, you guys can go without me. i'll just get one tomorrow" aang lets us know.

me and zuko going shopping alone? why does that thought excite me? don't get me wrong, aang is like my little brother and i love him, but i can't say no to some extra zuko time.

"oh, alright" zuko nods.

the bell rings and we have to go back to class. another few hours of wondering what zuko is doing when we don't share a class. spirits, i gotta stop thinking about him.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

"wow, this place is huge" sokka remarks when we enter the thrifting store i usually go to.

his big blue eyes wander around, taking everything in and analysing closely every inch of the place.

"yeah, it is. there are tons of cool stuff here, we just have to be patient and dig deep through all the junk" i instruct him.

"can't wait" sokka says and we start looking for anything worth our attention.

after almost 2 hours of picking some nice clothes and piling them up on our forearms, i find something i never thought i'd find in a place like this.

"sokka" i call for him, visibly excited.

"what is it?" he lifts his head from a mountain of clothes and a few stray hairs are coming out of his ponytail.

i flutter what i found in front of him.

"what's that?" he asks.

"how do you not know?" i ask a bit indignated. "i've always wanted a maid outfit, like i saw boys wear on tiktok, but i never knew where i could find one"

he looks for a second at the outfit and then back at me.

"wait, you get boys in dresses on your tiktok? i only saw stupid people dancing on shitty songs so i uninstalled it" sokka says and i burst into laughter.

"oh, no, you're on straight tiktok..." i snort, but then i remember he *is* straight. "sorry, but that's the worst side of tiktok you can be on. i'll fix it for you another time"

he brings his bottom lip out and pretends to pout.

"ok, the *maid outfit* looks cool, but isn't it a little too much for school?" sokka asks.

"yeah, it is, but i won't wear it to school. i'll just buy it in case i ever need it"

he nods, gives me a thumbs up and a smile. i wonder how he would look like in it. well, he would look hot even with a potato sack on him, so the question is rethorical.

"zuko, look! cat ears! they would look so cool with that black skirt i got, don't you think?"

"definetely! you gotta get them!" i encourage him and i can see he really is content with what he found.

gay panic is *real*.

sokka in a maid outfit, wearing cat ears, will permanently occupy my mind from now on.

## Chapter 11

**zuko's POV**

"today was a blast, man!" sokka says excitedly as we're on our way to the tea shop, walking down the street.

"yeah, i didn't expect so many boys to wear skirts willingly" i reply with a smile on my face.

i'm really glad every teacher got the point we were trying to make and i was surprised to see everyone's enthusiasm for this idea.

boys should wear skirts and dresses more often because damn, each and every single boy looked amazing today.

"i think i could get used to this" sokka moves around to make his skirt flutter.

we haven't changed our clothes because we love them too much. of course, we got weird stares from almost every person that we passed by, especially old ones.

we also were cheered by groups of girls, or even boys, so that made up for all the criticism that we didn't take into consideration anyway.

sokka matched his tie with the black skirt, and wore a white shirt and long socks. he also added the cat ears, which made him incredibly cute.

i'm wearing a dark crimson skirt, a beige sweater over it and some rainbow, also long socks. besides, today i made a flower crown and even got compliments on it!

"i couldn't believe when i saw jet wearing a skirt! i never thought i'd witness that asshole doing something like that" sokka says.

i shrugg. i was glad to see jet expressing himself in a way he never could before.

"well, he's actually not that big of an asshole" i mention.

sokka gives me a confused look.

"what are you talking about? he bullied you and many others"

"hmm, i will tell you something, but you have to promise me you won't tell anyone else" i gaze at him.

"i won't, come on, spill the tea, you made me curious!" sokka begs like a little kid.

i roll my eyes and smile. i narrate the bathroom incident, but exclude the part where i tell him i fancy someone else. he would ask additional questions that i'm not ready to answer.

"no freaking way" his eyes widen and his mouth opens in shock.

he takes a few moments to process the events. meanwhile we're still walking, our shoulders touching ocasionally and our hands...

well, i am very aware of the insignificant distance between our fingers, only a few milimeters apart. would it be weird if i just...

*yes, it definetely would, why are you even questioning that? stop thinking about it!*

i have to repress the urge to grab his hand, to feel his soft and warm skin...

*shit, i'm doing it again.*

"zuko, he has a crush on you!" he exclaims after a few minutes.

"so?" i ask frowning.

"so, if you say he's not so bad, maybe i don't know, you can try something out? get to know eachother better?" he suggests.

and, just like that, every hope i had about him having feelings for me is destroyed. he wouldn't propose something like this if he liked me.

i know he's just being a good friend, but why do i feel like the ground is about to swallow me whole right now?

"um- no, i don't think so" i mumble with my heart sinking bit by bit.

"why? do you like someone else?" he asks me calmly.

*panic. panic. panic*.

"ugh- um- kinda" i stutter.

why. the. fuck. did. i. say. that?

"what? why didn't you tell me? who is he? do i know him?"

*it's you, dumbass. it's always been you and that's crushing my soul.*

"nope, you don't know him" i answer quickly.

i hate lying, especially to him, but i can't think of any other way of escaping this situation.

"oh..." he sighs.

"he's from my old school, but we don't talk much" i add.

why would i even build up this lie? i didn't have any friends at that school, it was filled with spoiled brats with stupid rich parents.

"will i ever meet him?" sokka asks and i face palm myself mentally.

"i don't think so... let's change the subject, please"

he nods and he doesn't insist. we sit in silence, but not for long because we finally reach the tea shop.

&#45;-

uncle has hired more people, so it's easy to handle the customers now and i get to talk more with sokka. we immediately got over the slight awkwardness that followed the conversation about my fake crush, so now it's just like nothing happened.

"hey, sokka, can i talk to you for a sec?" jin approaches us. "alone" she looks at me and smiles a little.

"yea, sure" i remove myself, but i don't go too far.

i would lie if i said i'm not trying to eavesdrop. i know i shouldn't, but what the hell does jin want to tell sokka that i can't hear? i pretend to wipe a table that's at a reasonable distance from them.

"so, i was wondering, maybe- um- you'd want to- to go out sometime?" the girl asks him and i can't help but look at him for a second.

will he accept? is he over suki? does he like someone else? will he look at me before he gives her an answer?

*no, why would he do that? he doesn't need your permission for anything.*

"oh- um, okay, sure" he mumbles and jin's smile grows.

"great! is tomorrow ok?"

"yeah, tomorrow works" sokka approves and then goes to take a customer's order.

*stop, zuko. ignore the emotions now, wait until you get home. wait till you're alone, then you can* *let yourself feel everything.*

"hi, zuko" a voice startles me and i turn around to face jet.

what is he doing here? i keep on wiping the table nervously for the millionth time because i think i'll be shaking if i don't keep my hands busy.

"what do you want?" i ask without looking at him.

"you said i could talk to you if i had a problem, but i don't have your phone number" he says. "i asked around and found out you work here so, here i am" he shruggs.

i throw away the rag and dust my hands before taking his phone.

"sure, i'll give it to you" i start typing, but my hands are a bit shaky from frustration.

"are you ok?" jet asks me.

i lift my eyes from the phone and they unintentionally land on sokka, who is in the opposite part of the room. he's looking at me and jet, but he's not smiling like i expected him to. instead, he has a straight face on, that i can't read properly. he looks away when he sees me staring back.

"i've been better" i manage to say. "how about you, do you have something on your chest that you wanted to tell me right now?"

he shakes his head, chewing on the toothpick that's never missing from his mouth.

"nope. i was kinda bored and i thought maybe you wanted to hang out or something" jet takes me by surprise. "as friends, of course" he adds when he sees my facial expression.

i should forget about sokka, and jet seems to be in the right place at the right time.

"oh, ok. my shift ends in half an hour, you can wait if you want and we can go somewhere after"

am i a bad person for wanting sokka to be jealous? am i playing with jet's feelings if i do this? i don't want that, but he said we would only hang out as friends, so i guess it's fine.

i know sokka didn't hear our conversation, because he was way too far and this place is noisy, so he doesn't need to know if we're spending time as friends or not.

spirits, why am i trying to spite a person that doesn't even like me to begin with? it makes absolutely no sense.

&#45;-

"you know you still have a skirt on, right?" jet asks me once we leave the shop and i look down.

to be honest, i forgot about it, but this is the last concern i have right now.

"i don't really care" i say.

nothing seems important right now, because sokka is the only one in my mind, even though i am trying hard not to think about him.

we walk until we reach a park so we sit down on the closest bench. my heart aches when i realise this is the park where sokka called me beautiful as we were lying on the grass, after i ran off from the shop.

"so, you have a crush on that guy, sokka, right?" jet says out of nowhere.

i sigh and bury my face in my palms.

"it's that obvious, huh?" i groan.

"a little bit, yes" he chuckles, but there's a speck of sorrow in his voice. "i saw the way you were looking for him constantly while you were serving tea. and how your shoulders would drop when you saw him talk to the other waitress, the one with the messy hair"

he's talking about jin. i should be more careful about the emotions i show in public.

"i'm stupid for falling for a straight guy" i mumble and starts scraping some dirt with my shoes.

"i don't know, man, he was also searching for you while you weren't paying attention, so maybe he likes you back" jet adds.

what? that's not possible.

"no, he doesn't. he's going out with jin tomorrow and he encouraged me to start dating someone else. clearly, he does *not* like me back"

jet pouts and runs his fingers through his hair.

"that sucks, i'm sorry"

he seems honest and i'm grateful that he's trying to help me.

"yeah..." i scoff. "i'm actually trying not to think about him, so can talk about something else?" he agrees with a soft nod.

"i came out to my friend, longshot. he was very surprised and i was scared of his reaction, but he was very accepting" jet says.

that makes me smile. i know how important it is to have your friends' support and i'm genuinely happy for him.

"that's great, dude!" i pat him on his back and he grins.

"yeah, it really felt liberating, like i was carrying this huge boulder on my shoulders and i finally got rid of it"

i know exactly what he means.

"when are you gonna tell your parents?" i ask him.

he takes a deep breath and bites the inside of his cheek.

"i'm trying to find the right moment, but i always come up with reasons to postpone it whenever i gather up the courage to tell them" he admits.

"you can't wait for the right moment to arrive, you gotta create it yourself. but if you think that their reaction might be extremely bad, like if they might hurt you or kick you out, it's better not to tell them" i advise him.

i know from personal experience. he contemplates a little at the possibilities.

"i don't think they'd be that harsh" jet says after a few moments.

"that's good. then, just get it off your chest and you'll be relieved. they'll have to accept it, it's not like they can change anything"

he nods.

"i think i will. thank you"

i smile in response and we sit in silence for a while, until the sun dissapears completely and some stars make their appearence on the sky.

"i think we should probably head home. it's getting pretty late" i suggest, even though i don't mind his presence.

"yeah, you're right" he agrees. "you want me to walk you home or something?"

that sounds a lot like something to say on a date, which it isn't the case.

"i'm good, i know how to get there" i joke and he understands.

we wish eachother good night and we part ways. the streets are mostly empty already and there are only a few cars passing by occasionally.

i enjoy the chilly air of the night and now that i'm alone with my brain, guess who's popping up in my mind? my best friend, of course!

i can't help but wonder if he would like me if i were a girl. would he take me out on dates, cuddle with me and give me his sweatshirt to wear every time he's not next to me? probably.

my head is in the clouds, and i don't notice a car has pulled over close to where i am.

"hey, you there!" i hear a deep voice behind me.

i pray that he's not talking to me and i start walking a little faster, my heart running crazily in my chest and my knees feeling weak.

he keeps calling for me and i can hear many footstept heading towards me, so he's not alone.

i realise i should have changed my clothes. walking on the streets in a skirt, as a boy, especially at night, can be dangerous. why didn't i think about that?

"why are you ignoring me, twink?" he pops up in front of me, forcing me to stop walking.

other 3 guys stand around me and i can tell they're a few years older than me. i could easily take one of them out, maybe two, 3 with a little luck and skill. but there's four of them so even trying anything would doom me.

"i don't want any problems, guys, please just let me go home" i manage to say, even if my voice is almost cracking.

he takes the flower crown that i forgot i even had on my head, rips it and throws it on the concrete, stepping multiple times on it. why does he feel the need to do that?

"did you hear that?" he addreses his friends. "he wants us to let him pass, isn't that sweet?"

they all start laughing and my pulse rises even more.

"sure, go" one of them says and he moves out of the way, inviting me with his hand extended.

i should have seen it coming when he let me go so easily, because once i step forward, one of them pushes me from behind, making me fall on the ground.

when i try to get up, i feel a strong kick in my ribs that makes all the air leave my lungs. i curl up and groan, struggling to breathe properly.

"we can't just let you mock the male gender by wearing girls' clothes, can we?" one asks rethorically and kicks me once more.

i know it it an odd thing to think about while being beaten up, but i realise the pain takes my mind of sokka, even for a little while.

"go on, punch me" i say and i am finally able to stand up.

they look at me confused and then they exchange glances.

"are you a masochist or something?" one jokes and frowns.

i shrug, trying to stand up straight, but my legs are trembling. i know i'm not being smart about this, but i honestly don't care whag happens to me anymore.

suddenly, sharp pain overwhelms my jaw and i fall back on the ground, spitting a dark and warm liquid out of my mouth.

"let's go, it's not as fun if he's enjoying it"

even if my vision is slightly blurred, i can tell they got back in the car and started driving away, leaving me on the concrete.

i roll on one side so i don't choke on my own blood and close my eyes, focusing on the physical pain for a while.

## Chapter 12

**zuko's POV**

apparently, living with an abusive father for the first 18 years of my life has its advantages. i can tolerate pain *really* well.

those random guys did nothing that i haven't felt before thanks to my father, so i manage to get home after spirits know how much time.

when uncle iroh sees me, he almost has a heart attack and insists on rushing to the hospital, but all i wanna do is go upstairs and cry.

i know he only wants what's best for me, but i can't deal with anyone right now, so i shut the door to my room, lock it and let myself fall on the floor.

uncle tries to talk me into letting him inside to help me clean up my wounds, but i put on my headphones and turn up the volume until my eardrums hurt, so i can't hear any of his words.

i lift up my sweatshirt and examine my bruised ribs. let's just hope they aren't broken, cause they sure hurt like hell and the purple-ish colour covering them isn't comforting at all.

i know i have dry blood all over my face and clothes, so, once again, i am glad i don't have a mirror in my bathroom. if i saw myself right now, i would probably break down completely.

i somehow find some strenght to take off my clothes and get in the bathtub. i let the burning hot water pour all over me as i hug my knees and lean my head on them, staring into a blank spot.

i feel nothing and, at the same time, i feel everyhing.

how is that even possible? i know it doesn't make any sense, but i can't find better words to describe it. i want to cry, but i can't even do that, it's like i'm empty inside.

my skin is burning, but i start rubbing the blood off me, trying to ignore the excruciating pain i feel whenever i touch certain parts of my body. the water slowly turns red and i think i'm gonna puke.

when i feel like i got rid of every trace of tonight's events, i put on a hoodie that is twice my size and some sweats, then get under a blanket.

i hug myself and i realise i'm shaking badly, even if i just got out of the hot shower.

i hear my phone buzzing and i can barely lift it from the nightstand. i see 20 messages from sokka, 13 missed calls, some from him, some from my uncle and also some texts from jet.

i can't really focus on sokka's messages, but he seems worried that i didn't answer until now. i look at the time and see that it's 2 in the morning. when did it get so late?

i force myself to type "i'm fine" and i send it to him, then turn my wi-fi off because i can't stand to talk to anyone at the moment.

he's going out with jin tomorrow and i have no right to be jealous, but i can't say that it doesn't hurt, because it does. it hurts. really, really bad.

spirits, i'm not even good enough for him and i could never be. even if he did like boys, he deserves *so* much better than me.

what do i even have to offer? childhood trauma? abandonment and trust issues? nobody wants to deal with that crap and i wouldn't want to burden sokka with it.

my eyes start to sting and warm tears slowly find their way to the surface. i might as well drown in them, because i'm certain they won't stop streaming down my face very soon.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

*"i'm fine"*? *"i'm fine"!?!*

is that his only answer after i was worried sick that nobody knew where he was and he wasn't answering his phone?

i texted him like crazy and he responded with *that*? what the hell?

i wanna say i'm mad at him for running off with jet without a word and then dissapearing for hours, but i admit i was beyond relieved to know he's alive and well.

even after he answered, i texted him some more, but he was disconnected, so i called him. no answer.

what is happening to him? is he alright? should i go check up on him?

*it's 2 am, sokka, you cannot do that.*

right. i'll just talk to him tomorrow, at school.

&#45;- **&#42;time skip&#42;**

it's been 2 days since i haven't heard anything from zuko. he didn't show up to school, or at work, he didn't reply to any of my texts and when i asked his uncle if i could go see him, he said zuko wouldn't want that.

what's *that* supposed to mean? why wouldn't he want to see me? i'm his best friend, dammit and i can't stand not knowing what's wrong with him.

after work, i go home to take a shower and change my clothes, then i jump in the car and head to the nearest supermarket i can find.

i grab a bunch of zuko's favourite snacks and sweets, some soda and a stuffed turtleduck, because i know how much he loves them.

i then drive to his place and his uncle opens the door for me. at first, he doesn't want to let me in because of zuko's request, but i don't give up until he surrenders.

i've been here once before, when we had to do a chemistry project together, so i know where his room is.

"zuko?" i call for him while knocking on his door.

no answer. i knock once again, but still no response so i press the doorknob and open it.

there's barely any light in his room, but i can distinguish most of the objects. some plasic wraps are scattered on the floor, as well as a pile of clothes in the corner of the room. everything is a mess, but there's no sign of him.

"zuko?" i say once again but the only sound i can hear is some water running.

he's in the bathroom.

"hey, zuko, are you in there?" i ask and knock on the door.

when he doesn't answer, i start to panick and my vision is blurred by the worst scenarios possible. i can only think of him laying in there, blood dripping from his wrists on the cold floor... i have to go inside.

i bust the door open and i am ready to see the horrific image that i pictured earlier, but i sigh in relief when i realise it's not the case.

he's in the bathtub... fully clothed.

"zuko, are you ok?" i reach for him and when i come in contact with the water running all over him, i notice it's really cold.

"spirits, it's freezing, get out of there" i say alarmed.

he barely looks at me for less that a second, but i can see that his face is covered in bruises.

"what the hell happened to you?" i ask.

he hides his face in his knees.

"i'm fine, sokka, you can go away" he says in a weak voice.

"you are far from fine, come on, let's warm you up" i grab his arm, but he refuses to move. "ok, as you wish"

i sigh and get in the bathtub. i tremble a little because of the thermal shock, but i force myself to sit down, facing him.

he looks at me confused and i can tell he's been crying, because his eyes are red and puffy.

"what are you doing? you're gonna catch a cold, get out" he says concerned.

his wet hair is glued to his face, as well as his clothes to his body.

"i'll get out only when you do" i state firmly.

he clearly did not expect that and he looks annoyed.

"you're an idiot" he mumbles and finally gets up.

thank spirits, i don't know how he can resist in this cold water, because i'm already trembling.

he climbs out of the bathtub and so do i, then he takes a towel and hands me one too and we scrub the water, trying to get dry.

"why were you taking a bath with your clothes on?" i presume to ask him.

"i couldn't take them off. it hurts" he says and i look at him confused.

what hurts?

"you have to change into some dry clothes, you can't stay like this" i state. "come on, i'll help you"

he stares at me hesitantly and i am aware that he is not very comfortable with that. however, after a few seconds, he nods his head, giving me permission to undress him.

"can you lift your arms, please?" i request calmly and he does as i ask.

he grunts when he moves, but i can understand the reason why only after i lift his sweater and uncover his abdomen, seeing that it's filled with bruises.

"shit, zuko, how did this happen?"

he remains silent. i take his sweater off completely and grab the towel to dry his skin. he doesn't oppose, but i notice he tenses up when i go over some spots that are red-ish, or even lighter than the rest of his skin.

he has multiple scars all over his chest, arms and back, but i think it might be better not to mention anything about them. he knows i can see them, which is why i assume he was hesitant in the first place.

as i'm patting his chest softly with the towel, his eyes meet mine and the pain that radiates from them crushes my soul completely.

i only realise i stopped moving when he breaks the eye contact and gets out of the bathroom to bring some clean clothes for both of us.

"i brought you some stuff" i say after we change and sit on the bed.

zuko's clothes are a little too big for me, but they are super soft and smell really nice. just like him.

"i'm not hungry" he mumbles when i show him the snacks.

he wraps himself up in a blanket and looks anywhere but at me.

"oh- okay, i also got you this" i say and hand him the stuffed turtleduck.

he looks at it for a second and then he moves his eyes to fix mine.

"why are you here, sokka?"

his words hurt. he's trying to push me away again, but i won't let him do that. he is stubborn, but i know he needs comfort, just like everyone else.

he doesn't even take the stupid turtleduck.

"that's a stupid question and you know it" i cut him off. "i was so worried about you, zuko! i couldn't even sleep at night and it killed me that you didn't bother to give any sign that you were alive"

"how was your date with jin?" he ignores what i say completely.

*what? jin?*

"what does she have to do with anything?" i frown.

"can you just answer the damn question?"

i am very confused. why bring jin into the discussion?

"it was ok, zuko, nothing special. i only agreed to go out with her cause i didn't want to seem like a jerk. the whole thing was pretty awkward and we decided to stay friends"

"oh..." he says.

"now can you explain what in the world happened to you? tell me who did this to you so i can hunt them down and kill them" i try to make a joke.

he doesn't even smile, he just keeps a straight face on.

"some random guys saw i was wearing that stupid skirt a few days ago and i guess you can figure out what happened next"

i picture zuko being hit and all i can feel is pure rage. if i had been there with him...

"wait, weren't you hanging out with jet?" i try not to sound jealous, because i am not! "if that prick did this to you i swear..."

"no, he didn't, sokka" he interrupts me. "it happened after he left, but i don't want to talk about it"

i drag my palms across my face. i can't believe shit like this still happens. we sit in silence for a few moments, avoiding eachother's glare.

"so... how did it go with jet?" i ask out of nowhere.

i'm not jealous, ok?! i'm not! i'm just- curious...

"it was fine"he says. "it wasn't like that anyway, we're just friends" he adds after he pauses for a second.

why did he feel the need to explain? and why did i feel... relieved when he said that? this is so weird.

"i see..." i mumble. "so, you wanna watch a movie or something? this junk won't eat itself" i gesture to the bag full of snacks.

"sure" he nods and turns on his tv.

&#45;-

i frankly have no idea what movie we're watching and it's been almost an hour. i don't even know what the plot is about.

what i *do* know is that zuko is extremely near me and he's holding the turtleduck i bought him, which fills my heart with joy.

he is slowly getting closer and closer to me, until his head is leaning completely on my shoulder.

i realise he fell asleep, so i assume he didn't do it on purpose, but why is my heartbeat rising at an alarming rate anyway? why do i feel like my whole body is on fire and my skin is tingling like i have millions of ants walking all over me?

i would feel bad about waking him, but he's not sitting comfortably and it's obvious he's extremely tired. i move him a little bit, until he gets in a proper position and i bring the blanket up to his chin so he doesn't get cold.

i turn the tv off and when i get up to go home so i can let him rest, i feel a warm hand grabbing my wrist.

"stay" he whispers with his eyes still closed and i can tell he's half asleep.

for some reason, his requests makes my heart jump like it's crazy and i immediately get back in bed.

## Chapter 13

**zuko's POV**

i slowly wake up when a ray of sunshine breaks through the curtains and hits my face. i want to rub my eyes, but i realise my arms are wrapped around something warm that is moving regularly.

it's sokka.

i'm hugging his waist, gripping on it like i'm adrift in the ocean and he's the only thing keeping me from drowning. my head is laying on his chest and i can hear his heart pumping blood in his veins.

my left leg is crossing sokka's body, being interwined with one of his legs, while his left arm is resting lazily on my back, bringing me closer to him.

i can't ignore the butterflies in my stomach and the fact that my heart literally hurts from how much i want this moment to last forever. i should probably move away, but honestly, it's the last thing i wanna do.

*how the hell did this happen?*

i mentally recap last night's events and i may or may not have asked him to stay in my bed, and... he did.

no, no, no, this is wrong. how on earth am i supposed to get rid of my feelings for him if things like *this* happen? if he continues to be so painfully sweet every time i try to push him away and distance myself?

he bought me a freaking *turtleduck,* how am i supposed to *not* fall in love with him? i know he genuinely cares about me, but just... not in the way that i want him to.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

when i gain consciousness, i feel some weight on my chest and it takes me a few seconds to realise that it's zuko. how did we even end up like this? and is it wrong that i don't want him to let me go?

his dark hair is falling in every direction all over my chest and the way the sunshine strikes his messy locks makes it impossible to ignore the urge to touch them.

i give in and run my hand softly through his hair, careful not to make any sudden moves so i don't wake him up. it is so soft and it smells so nice that i can't resist and i place a little peck on his head. he looks like an angel when he's asleep.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

*sokka's playing wih my hair, he kissed my head and my body is about to disintegrate.*

he probably thinks i am asleep, because he doesn't say anything, he just twirls my hair around his fingers in slow motions. he didn't push me away when he realised we are cuddling.

it pleases me that he isn't bothered by it, but as much as i want to extend this moment, it's a school day and, even if i'm not going, sokka has to.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

zuko starts stretching a little, which means that he is awake. he then distances himself quickly when he sees he's almost on top of me.

"shit, sokka, sorry, i didn't mean to-"

his voice was already deep, but apparently it's even raspier in the morning, which makes my ears tickle in content.

"it's ok" i assure him. "i didn't dislike it..." i smirk lightly.

was it weird to say that? yeah, maybe a little, but i can tell he's trying hard to restrain his smile.

"i didn't dislike it either" he says and he hides his face with his hair, but it's obvious his cheeks are flushed.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

it took me a second to realise that...

he... he has his hair down.

sokka's leaning on his elbows now and his hair is framing his face flawlessly, bringing out his bone structure.

*he looks like a fucking greek god.* it makes sense now that he always ties it in a ponytail. if he kept it like this on a daily basis, nobody would be able to breathe around him.

"what time is it?" i ask when i come back to earth and try to stop thinking about how illegaly hot he is.

sokka reaches for his phone and glances at it quickly.

"11 pm" he says nonchalantly.

"*what?* i haven't slept so much since... i can't even remember" i state with surprised look on my face.

now that i think about it, i didn't dream about my father like i usually do, which is weird. could it be because of... him?

"well, that's good, right?" sokka asks.

i stare at him for a second with my eyes bigger than normal and i bet i look like a lunatic.

"i- i didn't have nightmares last night, sokka" i say as if he's supposed to know what i'm talking about.

i'm just- i'm too delighted about it to care that i just shared something personal. i was able to sleep peacefully for the first time since forever.

"you often have nightmares?" sokka asks me with concern printed on his face.

i nod and start playing with the bedsheet nerously. there's no backing out now.

"yeah..." i sigh. "like- every night"

he brings his eyebrows closer together and a little wrinkle appears between them.

"is that why you said you don't usually sleep much?"

i nod once again.

"what are they about?" he wonders and he is now leaning with his back against the wall.

i lift my eyes in order to meet his deep blue ones and it's masmerising how easily i can get lost in them.

"uhm- about my- my father" i answer at last.

"oh..." he looks away, then back at me. "do you want to talk about it?"

it's obvious he wants to know more about it, but i don't know if i should tell him...

"not really" i answer briefly. "he was a *really* bad person"

he nods in understanding and stops a moment to think about it.

"i'm sorry to hear that" he says and i try to smile to let him know that it's ok, but it doesn't really work out.

without a word, he pulls me in a hug that we hold on to for a while. he somehow eases the pressure that takes over my heart whenever i think about my father.

"shouldn't you go to school?" i break the silence, but not the embrace.

i'm way too comfortable, as he's slowly moving his fingers up and down my arm, melting every single spot he touches.

"it's too late already, i'll just skip today" he says. "let's go somewhere" he suggests after a small pause.

to be honest, i'd follow him to the end of the world.

"what do you have in mind?" i ask intrigued.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

"c'mon, sokka, don't be a bitch and just tell me where you're taking me" zuko whines. "and why did we have to bring bread with us?"

we've been driving for half an hour and he doesn't know where we're heading because i want to surprise him. also, i have to admit, i like hearing him beg.

"are you 5? just stop complaining, we're almost there" i shut him up.

he rolls his eyes, crosses his arms and pretends to pout, then turns up the radio.

"damn, i love this song!" i exclaim and i start singing along.

zuko looks at me weirdly, but i can see the corners of his mouth slowly rising.

"wow, you really can't sing" he chuckles and shakes his head.

"oh, like you can do it better" i mock him. "and it's not about having a singing voice, it's about this thing called... *having fun.* have you ever heard of it?"

he punches me lightly in the arm.

"ha ha, you're *so* funny. i *can* have fun" he assures me.

"oh yeah? then prove it. singing battle. right here, right now" i raise an eyebrow at him and smirk.

he takes a second to think about it.

"fine, but prepare to lose" he says at last.

"oh, i will *not* lose"

we both start singing, taking it easy at first, but then trying to cover eachother until we're basically screaming. of course, we both suck at singing, but at the end of the song, we gasp for air because of the real commitment we put in it.

at this point, we're both panting and laughing. i remember the state zuko was in yesterday, completely opposite from how he is now and the fact that i could lift his spirits up makes my heart fill up with joy.

"ok, we're here" i say and stop the car, then we get out of it.

"you drove us to another town just to go to a *park*?" zuko questions.

"can you just shut up and trust me?"

he approves and we start walking through the huge park, filled with kids yelling and playing with eachother or with their pets, until we reach our target.

"no way" zuko says.

his face lights up and he looks at me with those silly puppy eyes that make my heart skip a bit. or multiple beats.

"see? i knew you would like it" i grin.

"i love it, sokka" he adds with excitement. "so that's what the bread is for" he chuckles.

he then sits down on the edge of the turtleduck pond and starts ripping the bread in little pieces, feeding them one by one to the tiny animals swimming around.

i join him, but i choose to give him my piece of bread. watching him giggle when the turtleducks are all coming to him, fighting for the crumbs of bread is more than enough for me.

i can't get enough of his laugh and i might get addicted to it if he keeps doing it.

from the way the sunshine is falling on his skin, he seems to belong in a fairytale, and surely not from this world.

why am i thinking all these things about him? all i know is that i can't stop it.

&#45;-

"let's get ice cream" zuko suggests after we leave the park and of course i accept.

we decide to leave the car where it is and just walk around the unfamiliar streets of this town. we talk about random things until we come across a stand of ice cream.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

sokka gets a mango ice cream and i choose one with mint and chocolate chips, because mint reminds me of him.

"lemme have a taste from yours" sokka says while we're walking aimlessly down the street.

"sure, here you go" i hand him my ice cream and he does the same with his.

we taste from eachother's desert and i immediately think about how sokka's lips, as well as his tongue have touched the same ice cream as i have. does that count as kissing? i don't think it does, but it's the closest thing to it that i'll get.

the sun is starting to set, painting the sky in shades of orange, yellow and purple.

"we should probably head home" sokka states and i agree with him.

i'm a little dissapointed that this day has come to an end, but i'm beyond pleased about everything that has happened.

i woke up next to sokka, a thing i didn't even think was possible, and then we spent the whole day together, just the two of us.

he skipped school just to take me to see the turtleducks, because he knows they're my favourites and he wanted to make me feel better...

and he did. he makes me really happy.

## Chapter 14

**sokka's POV**

"... and then we got ice cream and talked about all kinds of stuff and we walked around that town without knowing where we would end up and it was so amazing" i tell katara everything as soon as i arrive home.

she was making dinner when i got here and it's almost ready now, since i helped her finish it faster.

"i'm really happy you had a good time and that zuko's feeling better. i was worried about him, but it seems like you were the light at the end of the tunnel for him"

i put some rice and chicken on katara's plate, then i do the same for myself.

"yeah... he was in a really bad shape" i sigh.

i replay in my head the moment when i found him in the bathtub, but i banish it immediately. i don't want to remember how i felt when i saw him so wretched. it really crushed me.

"are you alright?" katara asks me concerned when she sees my facial expressions.

"yeah, i was just-" i start to explain, but something else catches my attention.

my phone buzzes, so i reach for it and i see that i got a text from zuko, asking me if i want to go ice-skating tomorrow after school. obviously, i accept and i'm also glad to hear he's coming back to school.

"is that from zuko?" 'tara asks and i lift my eyes from the screen.

"how did you know?" i squeeze my eyebrows together.

she smirks and shruggs, proud that her guess was correct.

"your face lights up instantly when you talk to or about him"

she puts an elbow on the table and rests her chin in her palm. my lips part a little and for a second i am speechless.

"no, it does not!" i defend myself and my voice is weirdly high pitched.

"why are you blushing?" she chuckles. "is there something going on between you two?"

my heart starts racing and i have the impression that this room is rapidly getting warmer.

"uh- um- nothing's between us, wh- why would you think that?"

why am i stuttering? it really isn't anything going on, except for our friendship.

"why are you panicking, sokka? i just assumed that because you spend loads of time together and you always talk about him like he's the most important thing in the world"

"i- i do that?" i mumble.

well, i mean, zuko *is* extremely important for me, but that's only because he's my best friend.

right?

"uhm- yeah, you do, and you always mention him, regardless of topic" katara adds.

"that's not true!" i burst. "ugh, forget it" i roll my eyes.

she raises her arms in defense.

"woah, someone's touchy" 'tara takes a bite from her dinner.

we sit in silence until we finish the food and then we wash our plates.

"what do you wanna eat tomorrow?" my sister asks me as we're chilling on the couch.

"oh, maybe we can make some lasagna, zuko said he loves it and-" i stop when i see the look on her face.

she raises one of her eyebrows and stares at me with a smirk on her face.

"shit, i do talk about him a lot" i realise and rub my forehead.

"no way! i didn't notice!" katara exclaims sarcastically.

i bury my face in my hands and sigh. is it possible that i might like him as... more than a friend?

"'tara, how can i tell if i have a crush on someone?" i ask her genuinely.

she almost starts laughing, but then she looks into my eyes and sees that i'm dead serious. what if i do like him like that? what will i do then?

"wait, you really have no idea what your feelings about him are?"

i slowly shake my head while staring at a fixed spot.

"wow, ok, we will figure it out together" she pats my back softly.

"how?" i wonder.

she bites the inside of her cheek and stops to think for a second.

"i got it!" her eyes light up and she crosses her legs on the couch, facing me. "i'll ask you some questions and you have to answer with the first thing that comes to your mind, ok?"

i nod and agree with her plan, but i'm kinda nervous to be honest.

"how would you feel if zuko came to you tomorrow and told you that he wants to stop being friends with you? or that he has to move to the other side of the world and you'll never see him again?"

only thinking about that tears my heart to pieces.

"i'd be destroyed, 'tara. i'd feel empty without him. completely crushed" i answer, staring right into her eyes.

she doesn't comment on my response, she goes on with the questions instead.

"what would you do if someone broke zuko's heart?"

i don't even need time to think about it.

"i'd kill them, step on them, then feed them to the turtleducks because zuko loves them so much"

katara's eyes widen and she squeezes ger lips together to form a thin line.

"kinda too much, don't you think?" she smiles weirdly.

"no, i was serious" i state with no expression on my face.

"uhm, ok... um- next question! what is your first thought when you randomly see something funny or cute?"

"text zuko and tell him" i respond instantly.

"a new, really cool movie appears, who do you want to watch it with?"

"zuko..."

"what do you look forward to when you go to school?"

i pause and gulp.

"...seeing zuko"

my answers genuinely concern me and my breathing starts to get quicker.

"what would you do if you were in an locked empty room, just you, zuko and a gun with only one bullet and only one of you could get out alive?"

"what the hell is this katara? a crime movie?" i protest throwing my arms in the air.

"just answer the fucking question, sokka!" she says annoyed.

"fine, it's easy, i'd just shoot myself" i roll my eyes and run my fingers through my hair.

she smiles, but doesn't say a word. she just plays with her t-shirt.

"what's your favourite thing about zuko?" she continues after a short break.

it's absolutely impossible to name only one. i chuckle just thinking about it.

"i love how his skin glows in the sun, i love his laugh and the way his eyes sparkle when he's happy. oh, his eyes... those golden eyes, with specks of orange and, if you look closely, even some faint green, make my heart stop every tim-" i choke on my words.

and it hits me.

i turn my head towords katara and see her wide smile, but i feel like i can't breathe.

"katara, i think i-" i start saying, but i can't finish the sentence.

"oh, i think you do" she nods her head.

i jump to my feet and start walking around the living room desoriented.

"but i- how can i- i like girls, katara!"

she comes to me and slaps me on the cheek.

"get yourself together, dammit! so what if you like a boy? it's not a big deal, so stop panicking, you're unsettling me"

"you're right, it's not a big deal that he's a boy, but..."

but he's my *best friend.* how can i have a crush on my best friend? it's not right. i need to be alone.

"i'll just go to my room, good night, katara"

i rush upstairs and lean my back against the door. i let myself slide slowly until i reach the floor and i sit there for a while.

i'm so stupid, how didn't i realise earlier that i have this type of feelings for him? i swear to spirits, sometimes i'm blinder than toph.

everything makes sense now. this is why i was jealous when jet started talking to him at the tea shop and i felt so angry when they left together without telling me.

this is why i considered kissing him when i slipped and fell on top of him and why i enjoyed cuddling with him.

even when he's not physically beside me, he's permanently present in my mind and i can't do anything about it. i'm always wondering whether or not he's ok, what he's doing and where he is. i never thought much of it, but now that i do... everything is crystal clear.

what am i supposed to do and how am i supposed to act when i'm around him from now on?

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

after i tell my uncle every little detail of today's events, i go upstairs to take a shower, because i'm really tired. i struggle to take my clothes off, as my wounds still hurt like a bitch, but then my mind flies to sokka and i get overwhelmed with a wave of energy that empowers me.

i didn't realise i kept smiling since i came home, until uncle iroh pointed it out while we were having dinner. i've fallen so hard for sokka, that the mere thought of him makes me giggle like a kid and smile uncontrollably.

i know i said i shouldn't get my hopes up, but after today... maybe he actually does like me like that. or if he doesn't yet... maybe i can make him fall for me eventually. is that too much to desire?

with all these thoughts running through my head, i struggle to fall asleep, but when i actually do... it makes me wish i hadn't.

*i'm in the same dream i've had a million times before, the one where my father burns my face, but* *this time, it's somehow different.*

*sokka is sitting between me and my father, keeping me behind him, to protect me. when father tries* *to pull me to the fire, sokka pushes me away and takes my place* *without hesitation.*

*i try to cast my father away from him, i pull him by the arm, i hit him multiple times, but it's like i'm* *a ghots, only a spectator, unable to do anything.*

*i watch him hold sokka above the hungry flames and i scream so loud that i think my vocal chords* *will tear themselves to shreds, but i don't give a shit.*

*i try to move, but my legs are glued to the floor and they seem to be sinking quickly, taking away* *my ability to take any step forward.*

*i scream and shout and cry until i choke on the tears, but his suffering doesn't stop and i'm* *completely worthless in this situation. i can do absolutely nothing to help him and that kills me. i* *beg my father to take me instead, to burn me, not him.*

*i offer to let him kill me, torture me however he wants, anything just to let him go, but every attempt* *to convince him fails greatly.*

*--*
