i wake up screaming in the middle of the night, covered in cold sweat from head to toe and i desperately gasp for air. i bring my palm to my face and the surface is wet and sticky. the tears even got into my mouth and i their salty taste make me nauseos.

i rush to the toilet and i get there just before i vomit my guts out until i feel like i'm completely empty. the last time i puked, sokka held my hair, but now he's not here to do that, so it got on my face and now i have to wash it.

i take a quick cold shower to wake up entirely from the worst nightmare i've ever had in my life.

getting hurt myself can't compare to the pain i felt when i saw sokka suffering. if this is what i'll dream about from now on, i'll just never sleep again. i can't bear it.

i look at the time and it's 3am. i can't let myself get back to sleep, even if i can barely keep my eyes open. i go to the kitchen and make myself a coffee to help me stay up.

i need to keep my hands busy or i'll go crazy. i take my sketchbook and start drawing frantically, like it's the only thing keeping me breathing.

it's really helpful, because after a while, i slow down the pace and i become more calm. i stare at the drawing of me shielding sokka from my father that is shooting flames from his fists. i'm on fire, but i smile because sokka is alright and my father's blasts can't touch him because i'm there.

i close my sketchbook, go to my closed and grab a brown wooden box from the top shelf. i have an idea.

## Chapter 15

sokka**'s POV**

"why did you want to go ice skating if you've never done it before?" i ask zuko confused.

he's gripping on the fence surrounding the ice with both of his hands and he's moving slower than a snail.

"you once mentioned that you like it, so i thought you'd enjoy doing it with me" he answers.

*i'd also enjoy doing other stuff with you...*

what the hell, where did that come from? shit, i gotta learn how to control my intrusive thoughts.

i don't even remember when i told him that i like ice skating, but the fact that he remembered this insignificant detail about me makes my heart melt.

"how sweet of you. now will you speed up a little? even gran-gran skates faster than you" i taunt him and he rolls his eyes.

"you just want me to fall so you can laugh your ass off" he states.

"huh, that *would* be fun to watch, *if* you weren't already hurt, but you are and i don't want you to aggravate the injuries"

he stares into my eyes for a moment, but then he looks away and starts moving forward. he slips and almost falls, but i react quickly and help him regain balance.

my hands linger on his body for awhile and he's gripping on my clothes, afraid he might slip again.

"i hate this" he mumbles, taking his hands off me and placing them back on the fence.

"you hate everything" i say and chuckle softly.

he scratches the back of his neck and then gets some hair out of his face.

"yeah..." he approves. "i don't hate you though"

he gives me a deep look that makes me feel a wave of heat taking over my body and quickly reaching my cheeks. we mentain the eye contact until a loud thud makes us turn around to see a kid laying on the ice and starting to cry. his mother picks him up and tries to comfort him.

"ok, i don't want to fall like that too, let's go home" zuko says and wants to head to the exit.

"i'm not leaving until you can skate at least a few meters by yourself" i state and block his way.

he frowns and pouts.

"well, now i hate you too" he sighs.

i giggle.

"no, you don't. come on, grab my arm and i'll help you move faster than an old lady"

he puffs, but then obeys. i start skating slowly, leading zuko without him even needing to move his feet. he's squeezing my arm as if his life depends on it and i'm not gonna lie, i kinda like it.

"now, try pushing your feet forward, one by one. take your time" i guide him and he does as i say. "see? you can do it"

we skate around the place a few times, until zuko is starting to get the hang of it.

"you're getting it! ok, now let me go and try to skate by yourself" i tell him and try to pull away from his grip.

"no!" he exclaims, clutching me even harder. "don't let me go"

he looks at me with those puppy eyes that i can's fucking resist.

"fine, spirits, you're such a baby" i say. "let's try something else" i propose and he raises an eyebrow.

i place my hands on his waist, from behind and start pushing him lightly.

"sokka, i'm gonna fall" he sounds a little panicked.

"no, you won't" i assure him. "i'm here and i won't let you slip"

he sighs deeply and stops protesting. he begins moving his feet cautiously, while i'm holding onto him and speeding little by little. we're so close right now, it's making me nervous.

"see? it wasn't that ha-" i don't get to finish because suddenly he loses his balance.

i try to avoid letting him fall, but with no success. in a matter of seconds, he goes from standing up to laying on the cold ice.

"shit, zuko!"

he grunts in pain. fuck, i told him i wouldn't let him fall, but i couldn't keep that promise, and he was already hurt badly, he didn't need any more suffering.

"i'm so sorry, zuko" i say and extend my hand so he can use it to get up.

he grabs it, but instead of helping himself up, he pulls me down and i end up on top of him. once again. i stare at his lips and then move quickly to his eyes.

"agh, fuck" he groans. "bad idea, my ribs"

"dammit, you idiot, are you ok?" i ask and get off him as fast as i can so i don't cause him any more pain.

"you sure have a way with words" he says sarcastically while i'm lifting him up.

we get off the ice and sit on the closest bench we see.

"does it hurt badly?" i question him with tons of concern in my voice.

"i can handle it" he says, but i can tell from the tone of his voice that he's in pain.

i take my skates off and he tries to do the same, but when he reaches for his feet, he quickly goes back up, holding his abdomen.

"spirits, stop trying to act like a tough guy and learn how to accept help" i roll my eyes and kneel down to take his skates off as well.

"you don't have to do that, i can-" he attempts to say.

"shut the fuck up, you're hurt" i cut him off and put on his sneakers, then tie his shoelaces.

he thanks me and i lift my eyes in order to meet his. aaaghhhh my heart is going nuts, but i can't even move my stare from him.

"do you think you can walk to the car or do you want me to carry you?" i ask him.

"i can fucking walk, i'm not invalid" he assures me.

"fine, spirits, i was just trying to make sure you're ok"

"ugh- i know..." he coveres his face with both of his hands. "i'm sorry, you're being sweet and i'm an asshole, please forgive me"

how can i hold grudge against those pretty eyes that make my whole body melt in less than a second?

"it's ok, zuko, i can't be mad at you, regardless of what you do"

he bites the inside of his cheek and gives me a faded smile while staring right into my eyes.

we soon reach the car and get inside. i should drive him home, but i don't want to part ways with him just yet.

"hey, wanna go to my place?" i ask him. "there's this new movie that i heard is really worth watching that we can-"

"sure" he accepts before i can even finish what i wanted to say.

i nervously drum my fingers on the steering wheel and start the car.

"cool" i say briefly and then put on some music that we listen to and sometimes sing along, until we reach my house.

katara is currently on a date with aang - finally -, so that means me and zuko can enjoy some time alone.

"are you hungry?" i ask zuko as soon as we sit down on the couch in the living room.

"not really" he shruggs and grabs the remote control to turn on the tv.

i get up and go to the fridge and come back with two plates full of food.

"i said i wasn-" zuko starts protesting, but then he sees what i brought him. "wow, lasagna!"

i can't help but smile when i see that he's excited.

"i know you haven't eaten anything all day so if i don't see the plate empty, i'll kick your ass" i threaten him.

he raises his arms in defeat, then grabs the plate and takes a bite.

"aye, aye, captain" he mumbles with his mouth stuffed. "mmm, this is so good"

i chuckle at this image of him, sitting with his legs crossed on the couch and enjoying his favourite meal.

"me and katara made it because i know you really like it" i add.

he shifts his attention from the plate to me. some sauce is dripping from the corner of his mouth, but i don't mention it.

"*you* made this? spirits, it's amazing, i could eat this everyday for the rest of my life"

*i could cook it for you every day for the rest of your life.*

wait what?

"i'm glad you like it. now, let's start that movie, shall we?"

he agrees, but before we begin the film, i take the empty plates to the sink, wash them and then grab some snacks.

"why do you have fire flakes? you hate them, cause they're spicy" zuko asks me when i take a seat on the couch and hand him the snacks.

"i keep them in case you come over" i shrugg like it was obvious.

he nods, raising an eyebrow and he opens the wrapping. although he tries to hide his face behind his hair, i can see his cheeks are painted in a light shade of pink.

"oh, i forgot something!" he jumps and hurries to his jacket that is on the hanger in the hallway.

i follow him with my eyes as he grabs something from his pocket and then returns. when he sits down, he groans softly and i assume his bruises still hurt.

"here" he says and extends his arm, then opens his fist.

he reveals a thin bracelet that is made of 3 colours: the first one is light blue, followed by a warm shade of purple and then bright red.

"you made this?" i ask and my eyes meet his.

"yes, and i have one that is exactly the same" he says. "we can wear them as a sign of our friendship"

*friendship.*

"i love it, zuko"

i could look at the way he smiles for eternity and i wouldn't get bored of it.

"see, blue it's for you, red for me and purple is a mix of those two" he explains and i see sparkles in his eyes.

this is so sweet that i think i'll fucking cry.

"they're perfect" i state and give him my wrist so he can tie the bracelet there.

his warm fingers on my skin remain imprinted there even when he's not touching me anymore. i get over this feeling and help him get his bracelet on as well.

"wait, when did you have time to make them? yesterday we got home pretty late" i bring my eyebrows closer together.

he shruggs and avoids my glance while playing with the edge of his sweatshirt.

"please don't tell me you stayed up all night to make them" i pray with my eyes.

he's still not looking at me, so the answer us obvious.

"spirits, zuko, i told you that you need proper rest or your body will shut down eventually"

"i couldn't sleep, ok? i prefered to do something productive instead of staring helplessly at the ceiling" he explaines.

there is a moment of silence and i look down at my wrist, then start playing with my bracelet.

"it was because of your nightmares, right?" i ask him.

i know he is reserved with this kind of personal stuff, so i don't know if he'll answer or just choose to ignore me.

"yes" he nods. "it was the worst one i've ever had"

i pinch my ear lobe and consider if i should insist or not.

"do you want to talk about it?" i wonder.

i really want him to open up to me and surprisingly, he doesn't change the subject like he usually does.

"this time, my father wasn't hurting me, he was hurting..." he pauses for a second. "you"

he glues his eyes on mine and i can read the pain imprinted in them. he said something about his father hurting him and it made me question if that happened often in his childhood.

"zuko... did your father... hit you?" i ask hesitantly.

he focuses on his hands and doesn't say a word for what seems like forever, but then he nods, without even looking at me.

"shit..." i whisper. "if you want to tell me about it, i'd be more than happy to hear you out"

he runs his fingers through his hair and sighs.

"he's the one who did this to me, actually" he gestures to the scar on his face.

my heart drops instantly.

"wh- what?" i stutter.

no, that is not possible. how can a human being do that to another person on purpose, let alone his own *child?*

"yeah" he chuckles nervously. "he did it when i was 15, after he found out i was gay"

ok, i'm gonna kill his father. i've never even met this person, but i've never hated anyone in my entire life, as much as i do right now.

i don't even know what to say, how to react, how to comfort him.

"i assume that if i told you this, i should tell you the rest too" he says.

"only if you're ok with it"

of course i'm dying to know, but i don't want him to feel pressured to tell me.

"you're my best friend, so you have to know everything about me" he glances at me.

i nod and place my hand on his when i see how he's clutching on the material of his pants. he eases up a little and then starts narrating.

"my father was - well, he still is - very hot-tempered and used to take out his anger, mostly on my mother, but sometimes on me too"

he pauses for a second and i rub his back slowly to let him know i'm here for him.

"he would find literally any small reason to hit her. the chicken was overcooked? *slap.* she forgot to wash his work uniform? *hit.* i tried to stop him from hurting her a few times, because she was crying so loud-..."

he covers his ears with his palms as if he can still hear the screams.

"-but, when i did that, he would hit me too and obviously, mom begged him to let me go and take her instead. usually, he did, so, in some way, because i interfered, he would inflict even more pain on her-"

"no, no, don't you dare blame yourself for the actions of that *monster"* i say disgusted.

my eyes are starting to sting and i'm pretty sure tears will start coming soon.

"-anyway, i assume at some point, she couldn't take it anymore, so she... hung herself"

his voice cracks and he brings one hand to his eyes. he turns his back on me to hide the fact that he's crying, but i turn him around to face me.

he keeps his head down so his hair falls on his face. i lift his chin up with my index and i use both of my hands to get that hair out of his face. i then wipe his tears with my thumb, while staring right into his eyes. our faces are only a few inches apart.

"it's ok to be vulnerable, zuko. you don't have to hide from me" i say with a weak voice while i'm also choking on tears.

he nods and then he goes on.

"after she... did that, i was- i was the only one left that he could let out his anger on. when i dared to cry in front of him he would hit me even harder, saying that men don't cry and he's doing it for my own good"

even if it's barely noticeable, i know he's shaking from all the memories that he's reliving.

"-when he found out i liked boys... it was like i unleashed hell on earth. he thought that, by scarring my face, he would teach me a lesson and it would make me '*normal' "*

he sniffles and wipes some tears off his cheeks.

"it didn't work, of course, and when he saw that, he kicked me out of the house and disowned me. i lived on the streets for a few days, because i was too ashamed to ask anyone for help-"

that sounds like something zuko would do.

"-i knew uncle iroh would help me, but i didn't want to burden him with my presence. at some point, he found out what had happened and made me come live with him. i don't know where i would have ended up if it wasn't for him"

he's crying his eyes out at the moment, and so am i. i'm so angry, at his father for hurting my zuko, at the universe for letting that happen to him... i'm angry at everything.

i pull him into a hug and he buries his face in my chest, wrapping his arms around my waist and squeezing tightly.

i lay down with him in my arms, not letting go and letting hin soak my shirt with salty tears. i feel like my heart is being stabbed repeatedly because he's been through *so* much and he literally deserves the world.

at some point, his grip softens and i realise he's asleep. my arms and legs have gone numb from his weight on me, but nothing could convince me to move him when he looks so peaceful.

he deserves to rest.

## Chapter 16

**zuko's POV**

i gradually wake up and when i open my eyes with a groan, i realise i'm literally on top of sokka. spirits, i must have fallen asleep and... i didn't dream terrible things.

"shit, sokka, i'm crushing you, why didn't you wake me up? what time is it?" i look around me disoriented, trying to remember what happened before i fell asleep.

oh, right, i cried my eyes out like a baby while telling sokka all about my shitty childhood, in a sudden moment of vulnerability. how am i supposed to behave around him, now that he knows my trauma? will he act like i'm made of glass because he pities me?

"it's 11 pm" sokka says casually.

*11?!*

"why did you let me sleep *5 hours*? uncle iroh must be so worried that i'm not home" i quickly jump to my feet.

i start looking around for my keys, my phone, my jacket, but sokka comes to me and puts his hand on my shoulder.

"chill, i texted him and told him you'll stay here tonight" he says and stares at me with those baby blue eyes.

*stop beating so damn fast, stupid heart!*

"it's fine, sokka, i can go home, i can't bother you any more than i already have" i state and head to the front door.

he hurries and places himself between me and the exit, blocking me.

"you think i'll let you walk alone on the street so late?" he raises an eyebrow.

"um- since when do i need your permission to do something?" i ask sarcastically.

he grabs my forearm and lightly drags me back to the living room.

"remember what happened the last time you were alone at night? i don't want that to happen again so you're staying. it's not up for debate" he states firmly.

*is is weird that i kinda like him bossing me around?*

ugh, the bottom in me is showing.

"fine, whatever" i scratch the back of my neck. "imma go take a piss"

i head to the bathroom and when it's time to wash my hands, i am bound to stare at myself in the mirror that's hanging above the sink.

my face is all puffy and stained with tears, my eyes are red and my hair is a total mess. sokka is forced to see *this* whenever he looks at me?

my stomach is rapidly turning into a tight knot that climbs up my body until it reaches my throat.

*don't cry, don't cry, stop with the drama, you're annoying already!*

i can't help it. i'm so fucking weak, but i can't change the fact that i hate myself and i feel like puking every single time i see my reflection.

i draw my hand to my mouth, trying not to spill my guts, but with no success. i get on my knees, i bend over the toilet and there goes the lasagna i ate earlier today.

"spirits, zuko!" sokka burst into the bathroom.

i assume he was drawn by the lovely noises i'm making involuntarily.

"get out, sokka" i manage to say before vomiting some more.

of course, he doesn't obey, but instead, he pulls my hair back and ties it. he then runs his hand up and down my back slowly.

"what's wrong? are you sick?" he asks me concerned.

*yes, sokka, i am mentally ill, but i know that's not what you mean.*

i finish puking, but i don't get up just yet. i can't look at him.

"i'm fine, sokka, you can go" i mumble. "please"

"stop shutting me out!" he exclaims. "you are *clearly* not ok"

he grabs my shoulders and turns me so that i can face him. i turn my back on him as fast as i can.

"sokka- please don't look at me" i almost whisper and i bury my head in my knees.

i'm sure he can tell my voice is cracking, so he doesn't try to turn me around again.

"talk to me, zuko" he begs me. "please. it crushes me to see you like this and i want to help you, but i can't do that if you don't communicate with me"

we sit in silence for a moment and i can't even believe how he hasn't lost his patience with me yet.

he probably thinks i'm acting like a brat on purpose, to get attention or pity or i don't fucking know. i mean, i do act like that, but i can't do anything about it.

i can't control my feelings sometimes, i can't schedule the moments in which i just want to cease to exist or at least dissapear for a while. if i could, i'd program them when i'm alone so nobody has to deal with this crap.

but i can't. and i feel like shit because sokka has to witness my mental breakdown.

"how can you even look at me?" i break the silence. "i can never do it without feeling sick to my stomach. it sounds like i'm begging for compliments, but you wanted the truth, so here it is"

i turn and face him now and i see that he doesn't have a ponytail anymore. he probably used his hair tie to prevent my hair from getting dirty.

"i know you've told me before that i'm beautiful and shit like that, but *i can't see it*. and trust me, i've tried. i just-" my voice breaks.

i use my palm to wipe a tear that i didn't even sense until it crossed my cheek. i'm pathetic.

sokka fixes me with his big eyes that are filled with pure saddness. they're starting to glow and i don't want him to shed another tear for my stupid ass. he already did that enough while i was pouring my heart out a few hours ago.

"oh, baby-" he says and i almost flinch at the sound of that word. "if only you could see yourself through my eyes"

he puts some stray hairs away from my face and cups my jaw with both of his hands.

*what is he doing?*

he's dangerously close to me and i am trying hard not to kiss him right here and right now. wait, what the hell? i just puked, that would be gross.

"i think words are useless right now, because i can't force you to love yourself-"

*but you can love me.*

"-but i really apperciate that you told me how you feel" he says.

he lets go of my face and i start breathing properly again.

"i'm sorry you're the one that happens to be there whenever i have a breakdown. it must be exhausting for you." i sigh. "i'm just *so broken* and you shouldn't be burdened with this"

my voice trembles and some tears roll down my face so i let my head down. sokka lifts my chin so i have no choice but to get lost in his ocean eyes.

"zuko, don't say that you're a burden *ever again.* don't even *think* it. and i don't see you as broken, just hurt. but if that's how you see yourself-" he shrugs. "-then i will help you pick up all the pieces and glue them back together"

i chuckle nervously and i wipe my face with both of my shaking palms.

"what in the world did i do to deserve you?" i whisper.

he smiles.

"huh- weird... that's what i ask myself every day when i think of you"

all my blood has gathered in my cheeks and i don't even have to look at myself to know that they are extremely red.

"ok, how about you take a shower, i do the same in the other bathroom and then we'll do something that i think you'll enjoy?" sokka suggests after a few seconds of silence.

i nod and he soon jumps to his feet, offering me his hand to help me get up. i accept it and, even after i remain alone in the bathroom, i can still feel his skin imprinted on mine.

&#45;-

i return to the living room 15 minutes later, wearing sokka's clothes and his hair tie around my wrist. he told me i can keep it, so now i'll never take it off. i see that sokka's sister is sitting on the couch next to him.

"hey, 'tara" i say when i enter the room. "how was your date with aang?"

"hi, zuko. it was great actually, we're going out again in a few days"

she sounds really excited and i'm genuinely happy for her. she and aang have been making sweet eyes to eachother since i met them and they're *finally* considering getting in a relationship.

"i'm glad" i smile softly and take a seat next to them.

"ok, i'm gonna go to sleep now, i'll leave you guys alone. good night" she waves her hand and heads to her room.

sokka is wearing a black tanktop that highlights his impeccable body and i get shivers just by looking at him. i notice his hair is tied again in a ponytail.

"let's go to my room" he says bluntly and i stiffen.

"ugh- i'm not tired, i just woke up a while ago" i mumble.

he flutters his hand in the air.

"oh, don't worry, we won't be sleeping, come on" he grabs my wrist and leads me upstairs.

*we won't be sleeping.*

how can i not worry? what the hell does he mean by that? i'm seriously starting to panick.

"let's paint our nails!" he exclaims and plops on the bed, shooting sparkles from his eyes. "i've never done it before so i thought maybe you could help me"

i don't know whether i should feel relieved or dissapointed that he didn't mean what i thought he did. if he *had* referred to... *that,* at least it would have meant he liked me. even though i don't think i'm ready for something like that.

"sure, i'll paint your nails, but... don't you think people will assume that you're... you know, gay?" i wonder and sit on his bed.

"pfft, i don't care" he scoffs. "let them believe what they want"

i nod, but my toungue tingles because i want to ask him whether he actually is or not. well, he surely isn't gay, but if he was bi, maybe...

aaaand i'm getting my hopes up again. idiot.

"what colour do you want them?" i gesture to the pile of nail polish that i assume belongs to katara.

he bites the inside of his bottom lip while he's considering it and i want to kiss him so bad...

"can i make one hand black and the other yellow?" he raises his eyes at me and some wrinkles form on his forehead.

he's so cute that i'd literally cry if i had any tears left.

"you can do whatever you want, they're your nails" i smile at him and he returns the favour, melting my heart completely.

"awsome!" he squeaks.

i love his enthusiasm, it's contagious. i begin with the black nail polish and i feel privileged to touch each one of his fingers. while i use the yellow one, he flutters his other hand so the paint can dry faster.

"thank you, i love it!" he says after i finish, not being able to take his eyes off his nails.

next, i paint my own nails black, replacing the old layer that was extremely chipped with a fresh one.

"can you do my eyeliner now?" sokka suggests.

i glance at the clock and see that it's almost 2 am.

"it's kind of late, don't you think? we have school tomorrow" i point out.

"we can skip the first classes, please?"

i can't possibly resist those huge puppy eyes and i mentally slap myself for it.

"fine, i'll do your eyeliner quickly, then you have to rest"

he agrees and goes to sit on the edge of the bed, letting his feet touch the floor. i stand up in front of him, between his legs so i can reach his eyes.

it's still very uncomfortable, because i'm too tall and i have to lean in too much. sokka notices that i'm struggling so he puts his hands on the back on my knees. he pulls them in a way that i end up sitting on his lap, facing him, my legs on each side of his body.

*this is bad, this is bad, this is bad.*

"wh- what are you d- doing?" i stutter like an idiot and he just stares at me innocently.

"i don't want you to hurt your back, so now we're on the same level and you can do it easier"

uhm, no, i can't do it easier, because now i'm struggling to breathe normally, my hands are shaking like i drank 10 energy drinks and my cheeks are burning like crazy.

i have to erase the make-up a few times, because it turns out wavy because of the unstoppable shaking, which i pray to spirits he doesn't notice.

honestly, he would have to be blind (sorry toph) not to realise the effect that he has on me, but a guy can hope, right?

once i'm done wih the eyeliner, he hurries to the mirror and his jaw drops.

"i look so fucking hot!" he makes different poses and jumps around like a kid. "you might have to do this more often, because i look absoutely amazing"

i can't help but smile. of course he looks amazing, he could never be anything but gorgeous.

"i'm glad you like it, but now you have to sleep. you'll be tired tomorrow" i tell him.

"ok, fine, you sound like my dad" he rolls his eyes.

*i mean, you* ***could*** *call me daddy...*

WHERE THE HELL DID THAT COME FROM?

"i assume i'll go to the room i slept in with toph" i say and i get up from the bed.

"uhm- um- yeah... do that" sokka mumbles.

he sounds a bit hesitant, did he want me to sleep in his room? no, he would have said so if he did, right?

"good night" i say and open the door.

"you too" he grins.

-**- (a few hours later)**

i wake up gasping in the middle of the night after another horrible dream. how come i don't have nightmares when i sleep next to sokka? i mean, i only did that twice and it was the same every time. no nightmares.

before i can realise what i'm doing, i find myself standing in front of sokka's door. would it be wrong if i asked him to let me sleep in his room? maybe, but surprisingly, i don't really care at this point. i open the door with a squeak and enter the room.

"pst- sokka!" i whisper, not to startle him, but he doesn't respond. "sokka" i call him once again.

he had his back facing the door, but now he turns around, grunting.

"zuko?" he mumbles with a raspy voice that makes my legs feel like jelly.

he rubs his eyes and tries to adapt them to the dark room.

"i had a nightmare, would it be ok if i slept here? i mean, it's totally fine if you don't wan-"

"shut up and come here" he interrupts me.

he scoots over to the right side of the bed, making room for me. i lay down next to him and he tucks me in the blanket.

he then presses his chest agains my back and wraps his arms around my waist, pulling me even closer to him.

i'm starting to question my life choices, because i think i'll desintegrate. he's so warm and smells so nice and... &#42;*gay panic&#42;* homies are allowed to cuddle, right? *anyways...*

i feel safe in his arms. like nothing bad could possibly happen to me, as long as he's beside me.

and i was right. once again, the nightmares don't touch me when i'm with him.

## Chapter 17

**zuko's POV**

"sokka" i whisper. "sokka, wake up!"

i shake him lightly and he lets out a deep groan, but he doesn't move.

"c'mon, we gotta go to school" i explain and i try to escape his embrace.

he doesn't let me get up, but instead he pulls me back, squeezing me even tighter and burying his face in my back.

"why did you wake up *so* early?" he whines. "did you have nightmares again?"

i didn't. he seems to be the only remedy i have against them, so how am i supposed to sleep from now on? i can't ask him to hold me every night, that would be innapropriate.

"it's not early, sokka! it's 10, so we already missed the first classes and no, i didn't have nightmares" i assure him. "now come on, i can't skip any more lessons, or uncle iroh will murder me"

"ugh- fine, i'll get up, spirits" he mumbles, not pleased at all that he has to leave the warm bed.

&#45;-

we arrive at school at lunch break, because apparenly, sokka takes forever to get ready in the morning. we head straight to the gAang's table and we find everyone laughing at some joke toph just said.

"hey guys" me and sokka greet them at the same time and occupy our usual spots.

"what a surprise, how come you two lovebirds have decided to join us today?" katara jokes.

*lovebirds.*

"why didn't you wake us up earlier?" sokka asks her.

he didn't correct her and neither did i, but we both know our relationship is strictly platonic.

"you were way too cute, all snuggled up, i just couldn't ruin it!" she defends herself.

sokka punches her lightly in the arm.

"shut up" he mumbles and then makes himself busy with a sandwich.

i can tell that he's blushing, and so am i. i start fidgeting with my fingers under the table so i can diminish the panic that is slowly taking over me.

"why are both of your hearts beating so fast? did you do the dirty?" toph smirks and raises her eyebrows.

"what does *'doing the dirty'* mean?" aang asks innocently, pulling toph's sleeve.

when he sees that she doesn't answer, he looks at each of us, but nobody tells him, so he pouts.

"what the hell, toph?!" me and sokka yell at the same time.

"what?" toph squeaks in defense, raising her arms. "somebody had to ask"

sokka rubs his temples, visibly annoyed.

"it's innapropriate, toph! and no, we didn't do anything, we're just friends" he clarifies and i approve.

he's not lying, we *are* just friends, but that doesn't make it hurt any less.

"mhm, keep telling yourselves that" toph mumbles and stuffs her mouth with a burger.

luckily, our conversation dies when somebody pulls a chair next to mine and sits down, slapping the table with their palm. it's jet.

"hey, zuko" he adresses me, acting like every person at the table was invisible.

he's uncomfortably close to me and i don't get to say anything, because someone else does.

"what the fuck do you want?" sokka shoots lasers through his eyes.

"sokka, language!" aang intervenes, but he gets ignored.

jet shifts his stare to sokka after he sighs and drums his fingers on the table nervously.

"i wasn't talking to you, was i?" jet tells him and then goes back to fixing me with his eyes. "a friend of mine is having a party this weekend. you should come"

he rests one of his hands on my leg, but i move it away gently.

"uhm- i don't know, i'm not really into parties" i mumble.

"oh, come on, please? i'd be really happy if you were there" jet whines.

i look at sokka and it's obvious he can't stand this guy, because his teeth are clenched and he looks like his fists ready to punch him, waiting for the right occasion.

"can my friends come?" i ask, not being able to imagine how i would go anywhere without them.

jet looks at each one of them, carefully analysing. he rolls his eyes, and then he gives me an answer.

"yeah, whatever, just be there" he says and tucks some hair behind my ear before getting up to leave. "oh, and by the way, you look really hot today"

he winks at me, then turns around and walks away. what the hell was that?

"i hate that guy" sokka states, glancing at jet, who is now sitting at a table with his friends.

"i think you're jealous cause he was hitting on zuko" toph shruggs and then smirks.

she enjoys mocking people *so* much.

"wh- what? i'm not jealous! why would i be jealous of that prick?" sokka exclaims, throwing his hands in the air.

"do i have to remind you that i can tell when you lie?" toph raises an eyebrow.

sokka puts his hands on his head and pulls his hair in frustration. his cheeks are bright and i can't help but smile at the thought of him being jealous. maybe he does like me after all...

"zuko, does jet have a cush on you?" aang asks me.

"yeah, kinda. i was hoping he'd get over it, but apparently not" i say, playing with my food.

"don't you like him back?" the bald kid insists.

i don't get to answer, because toph starts talking before i even think of responding.

"he likes someone else" she grins.

ugh, she can be really nosy sometimes and it's annoying.

"toph, seriously, can't you just mind your business?" i roll my eyes and try to cover my face with my hair.

"oh, right, it's that guy from your old school, right?" sokka fixes me with his eyes.

shit, i forgot i told him that stupid lie and now everyone knows about it.

"uhm- i- um- yeah, that guy" i mumble and rub the back of my neck.

toph can barely repress her laughter, she seems to be struggling not to roll on the ground.

"oh, this is fun" she wipes a tear from the corner of her eye.

fortunately, the bell rings and this shitty conversation is finally over. spirits, this was the worst lunch break ever.

&#45;-

when i get home, uncle iroh greets me with a hot cup of my favourite tea.

"how was the sleepover?" he takes a sip from his mug, but i can tell he's smiling.

i sit on the couch next to him and cross my legs under me.

"it was... intense" i pretty much sum up everything with that word.

"that's it? that's all i get? i want details, nephew!" he says indignated.

i replay the emotional conversation i had with sokka in my head. i remember the way i melted completely in his arms and i smile without even realising it.

"i think i love him, uncle" i say briefly.

uncle iroh gives me a smile that warms my heart and then rubs my back with his palm. then, i tell him everything that has happened yesterday.

"i never imagined that someone other than you could be there for me no matter what and genuinely care about what happens to me" i add and rest my head on uncle's shoulder.

he pats me on the head gently, and then i continue talking.

"i didn't even think i deserved someone like sokka and i still don't find myself worthy of him" i confess.

"if there's anything you deserve, it's happiness and peace" uncle iroh says and stares right into my eyes. "don't ever think that you are less than a person with a pure heart that needs love and affection. does he make you happy?"

*yes. yes, he does. more than anything in the world.*

"*so* happy" i whisper and some tears start rolling down my cheeks.

"then why are you crying, zuko?" uncle worries and pulls me into a bear hug.

i hate crying in front of others and for some reason i seem to keep doing it more often, which is not right. but this is uncle, so i let it all out.

"because i can't be with him" i say and my words get lost in uncle's shirt.

"and why is that? from what you told me, he seems to fancy you as well"

i shake my head and get out of the embrace, then wipe my tears.

"i can't know that for sure" i state.

"and how do you think you can find out if you don't tell him?"

i shrugg. i've seen dozens of movies in which a best friend falls for the other and, miraculously, the feelings are mutual. in every one of them. but that can't happen, because real life sucks and feelings are usually one-sided.

"i don't know what to do, uncle" i whine and cover my face with my palms.

"there's only two options. confess your love to him, or stay unhappy forever. which one is it gonna be?" uncle questions me.

i let myself slide off the couch until i reach the floor and lie there, facing the ceiling.

"feelings are complicated" i mumble.

"only if you make them that way" uncle contradicts me and pours himself some more tea. "just follow your heart and happiness will find its way to you"

i contemplate at his words while staring at the chandelier that's casting light in the entire room.

"i think i'll go to my room now. thank you, uncle" i say and get up.

he does the same and then forces me into another hug.

"ugh, that's enough affection for one day" i try to escape, but there's no point in trying.

"shut up and let me love you" uncle iroh orders me.

and i let him.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV** (the next morning)

today is katara's turn to make breakfast, so when i get out of the bathroom, i can already smell the delicious food.

"hey 'tara" i salute her and sit down at the table.

" 'morning" she mumbles, not taking her eyes off her phone.

i bet she's talking to aang, because she's smiling and even blushing a little. i'm about to say something to taunt her on this topic, but i hear a few knocks on the door.

"who can it be?" katara asks me confused.

i shrugg, as disoriented as her. who could visit us on a school day, so early i the morning? i walk up to the door and open it, only to find zuko panting and holding something.

"what are you doing here?" i ask him.

he lets himself in and takes off his shoes, still breathing heavily.

"good morning to you, too" he says, rolling his eyes. "i brought you something"

he hands me the carton box that he's holding in his hands and we head to the kitchen. zuko and katara greet eachother and my sister gives me a suggestive look, raising an eyebrow. i ignore her.

"you hungry, zuko?" 'tara asks him.

"thank you, but i just ate and i hurried here before school could start" he tells us.

i open the box and there is a cake inside. he came here to give me a *cake?*

"i made it last night" zuko explains.

"is it edible? did you poison it?" i joke, but he puffs.

"if i wanted to kill you, i'd come up with something more interesting" he assures me. "uncle iroh helped me, actually, so i think it's pretty decent, even if it doesn't look promising"

he's right, it's a little smushed to the left and it looks like it's about to faint, but the mere thought that it was made by zuko's hands makes me want to devour it on the spot.

"come on, taste it" he encourages me.

he looks at me with his face resting on his palms and his elbows on the table.

"on an empty stomach?" i pretend to have never eaten sweets before a meal.

"yes, sokka! life is short, eat cake for breakfast" zuko rolls his sparkly eyes once again and of course, i can't refuse him.

i take a bite and it's actually not bad at all. it's a chocolate cake and who doesn't enjoy chocolate? but most of all, i love it because *he* made it.

"mm- it's amazing!" katara exclaims and eats some more.

she usually is against eating chocolate before breakfast, but apparently, she's making an exception today. zuko is still fixing me, waiting for a reaction. those golden eyes are cutting me into pieces, but i am willing to accept it without complaining.

"it's perfect, zuko" i tell him and his smile widens. "what's the occasion though? are we celebrating anything?"

"can't i make a cake for my best friend without any reason?"

"of course you can" i mumble and take another bite.

he randomly thought of baking me a cake and came here the first thing in the morning, probably on foot, giving the fact that he was panting when he arrived.

ok, i'm falling for him even harder than i thought was possible.

"did you taste it?" i ask him and he shakes his head.

"i couldn't have brought it here after i had already eaten from it, dummy" he giggles.

"well, i think you should" i say and fill a spoon with cake.

i lean over the table to let him eat from my spoon and he doesn't turn it down, without breaking the eye contact. his lips linger on the spoon for a few seconds and i forget how to move.

"ehm-" katara clears her throat and breaks the spell he had me under.

*i wonder if he felt it too.*

"we should hurry if we want to get to school on time" my sister states and she's right.

"yh- yeah, of course" i stutter.

he holds way too much power over me.

## Chapter 18

**sokka** **'s POV**

i usually love parties, but i can't believe we're actually going to one that *jet* has invited us to. well, to be more precise, he invited zuko, and the rest of us are just coming along.

i hated when he was flirting with zuko and all i wanted was to wipe that stupid smile off his face-

"here goes nothing" i sigh and stop the car in front of the house where the party is taking place.

the whole gAang gets out and surprisingly, they are pretty excited. i can't even remember the last party we went to, so i adopt some of their enthusiasm unintentionally.

"just pretend we're not here because of jet and try to have some fun" katara pats my shoulder as we're heading towards the house.

"yeah, i'll do that" i assure her and she gives me a smile.

to be honest, all i want is to enjoy some time with my friends - especially zuko - and i hope i won't have to see jet's face too much.

"if the food is shit, i'm out of here" toph announces us when we get on the porch and aang giggles.

zuko takes a step forward and knocks on the door a few times, but i don't know if anyone can hear it because the music is really loud.

apparently, the door opens relatively fast and, guess who greets us?

"zuko! you're finally here" jet shouts and i can tell he's already a little tipsy.

he's holding a red plastic cup in one hand and with the other, he grabs zuko's hand to pull him inside.

he's already got his filthy claws on him and i wanna scream. we all get inside and this house is *really* huge, so there's plenty of space for all the guests.

there are dozens of people dancing together while colourful lights agressively hit every object in their way. some are drinking, laughing or making out in different places, getting maybe a little too comfortable, giving the circumstances.

"sokka, you're here!" some boys from the basketball team shout and rush over to me.

"hey guys" i say, but my eyes are searching for zuko through the crowd.

i can't see him, which means jet took him spirits know where.

"come on, captain, play with us" mason leads me to the other side of the enormous room.

i can't really protest, so i find myself playing beer pong with my team and some other people i don't recognise. however, i am incapable of focusing on anything they say or do, because i'm constantly looking for zuko.

not even once do i see him and it drives me mad. instead, i notice that aang and katara are dancing together, holding hands occasionally, while toph seems to have found some food, because she's busy stuffing her face. i'm glad the appear to be enjoying themselves, but *where the hell is zuko?*

"i'm going to the bathroom" i let someone know just so i don't seem rude for leaving.

i don't actually have to go, i just needed an excuse to get away from them. i make my way through the crowd, glancing at every face i encounter, but i'm getting more and more frustrated with every minute that passes.

after a while, i finally lay my eyes on him. he's leaning agains the wall, playing with a plasic cup and *jet* is saying something in his ear.

jet has one hand stuck to the wall, above zuko's head and they are *way* to close to eachother. it makes my blood boil so i stop at a reasonable distance.

zuko is telling jet something, but as soon as he locks eyes with me, his lips stop moving. neither of us breaks the eye contact for what seems like an eternity and i want to run towards him and kiss him, but i can't seem to move.

he shifts his stare from me only when jet's greasy palms start touching him all over. zuko moves away his hands, visibly bothered, but jet doesn't take the hint, so he reaches under zuko's t-shirt.

ok, now i'm gonna separate his hands from his body with my teeth. i rush over to them, but before i can do anything, zuko pushes jet off him pretty hard and heads my way.

"leave him alone, he's drunk" zuko stops me by pressing his hand on my chest.

"oh, come one, just one punch!" i roll my eyes.

he puffs and smiles, shaking his head. i'm suddenly filled with energy while i'm in his presence and i force myself to respect zuko's wish to not kick that asshole's ass.

"ok, fine" i give up. "let's dance!"

i grab his wrist and drag him to the middle of the jumping crowd, but he doesn't seem too eager to join them.

"i don't know how to dance, sokka" zuko yells in my ear in order to cover the noise.

"it doesn't matter! just let your body feel the music!" i encourage him and for a second i think he's gonna ditch me.

he doesn't. instead, he begins moving his legs and arms awkwardly, absolutely not on the rhythm of the music, but it's better than nothing.

after a while, he starts to get the hang of it and his movements are not so stiff anymore. i'm glad that he got to lighten up a little and enjoy dancing around.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

sokka brings out a part of me that i didn't even know i had. because of him i danced for the first time in front of so many people and the weird thing is... i didn't even care.

i didn't give a shit if anyone was looking at me and my embarassing dance moves. all that mattered was that i was there with sokka, and he made all my inhibitions vanish.

now, we're resting after dancing so much and we unintentionally got caught up in a game that some people are playing.

i'm sitting in an armchair because there wasn't enough space for me next to sokka, on the couch.

"hey guys, what are you playing?" suki appears out of nowhere and asks.

"spin the bottle" someone answers her briefly.

"great, i wanna play!" she exclaims.

suki examins the area and when she doesn't see any empty space for her to sit, she turns to sokka.

"can i sit on you?" she asks him, but doesn't even wait for him to respond.

"uhm-" he murmurs, but he's too respectful to get her off.

the game has started apparently, because some people are already making out in the middle of the group, but i can't pay attention to anything.

all i can see is suki being all over sokka, with her arms around his neck and playing with his hair.

"so, baby, when are we getting back together? is the break over?" she questions him.

she's obviously *not* sober because she's mispronouncing almost every word that's coming out of her mouth.

"ugh- i don't know, suki" sokka responds to her.

he glances at me, but as soon as he does, i look away, forcing myself to focus on the game.

"suki, it's your turn" a blonde girl announces her and hands her the bottle, but suki turns it down.

"there's just one person here that i want to kiss" suki mumbles and i look at her without thinking.

she grabs sokka's face between her palms and leans in to kiss him. he doesn't pull away immediately and this image makes me sick to my stomach.

i basically jump from my seat and walk away from the scene. i'm aware that it was probably obvious that i did it because of their kiss, but whatever.

i elbow my way to the exit, but while walking to the door i see toph making out with a girl in the corner of the room. it takes me a second to figure out that it's june, a goth girl from my biology class and even if my heart is in pieces right now, i'm happy for them.

i burst outside and apparently, it has started pouring, but i couldn't care less. i begin walking down the street, stumbling in the dark chilly night and after a few minutes, i can't make myself take another step, so i lay my back on the concrete.

i let the sharp raindrops pierce through my entire body, shutting my eyes closed and my arms spreaded on the ground.

honestly, i would rather have someone stab me right through the heart than to keep feeling like this. i can't stand seeing him with someone else and i don't think i ever will be able to accept that he's not mine.

"zuko!" a familiar voice breaks me from my thoughts.

it seems to come from a considerable distance.

"zuko!" sokka shouts again, and now he seems to be closer, so i get up.

i see him running, but he stops at a safe distance, a few meters away. i think he can read the pain in my eyes, because he brings his eyebrows closer together. he doesn't say anything, he just fixes me with those criminal blue eyes.

"what, sokka? get back inside or you'll get soaked" i say, but he doesn't move an inch.

"why did you rush outside, zuko?" he asks me calmly.

i smile nervously and i thank spirits for the rain, because he can't tell that i have tears rolling down my face.

"because i can't take it anymore" i sigh and rub my eyes because my vision is blurred.

"you can't take what anymore?" he wonders.

my heart is stuck in my throat and my hand is twitching anxiously against my leg.

"loving you!" i shout and i can't believe i actually said it.

he doesn't say anything, but his eyes become bigger.

"i'm so tired, sokka" i add and i shake my head. "i'm tired of trying to hide my feelings for you every goddamn day, and you don't make it easy at all. you're just so sweet and i-" i hide my face in my palms.

i take a deep breath and restore the eye contact, even if it's hard to look at him at the moment.

"-i can't pretend like i only consider you my best friend or like i'm not *so* fucking jealous when i see you with someone else. i know that by telling you all this, i might ruin our friendship, but it physically hurts me to keep it in me any more"

right now, i'm even jealous of the rain touching sokka's skin in a way that i cannot.

"if you want to stop being my friend, or something, i understand, but i think i would have exploded if i hadn't told you this" i continue. "i love you, sokka and i can't fucking change it"

i take a step backwards, then turn around and start walking away.

"zuko-" sokka says, but i interrupt him.

"don't say anything, i know you only see me as a friend-" i say while continuing to distance myself.

i suddenly feel a hand gripping my forearm and turning me around.

"i feel the same way, you idiot" he states and i freeze.

my mouth opens by itself from surprise, but i close it back quick. what if he's making fun of me? what if he's joking?

he diminishes the distance between us, bringing our forheads together and placing his hand on the back of my neck.

"zuko..." he says breathing heavily. "can i kiss you?"

i gulp and the only thing i can do is nod, because i'm unable to form any words.

he hesitantely removes the already insifnificant distance between us and... he softly touches my lips with his.

i've never kissed anyone before, but this- this feels- there are no proper words for describing it.

we break the kiss and depart a few inches, only to stare into each other's eyes and we're both panting, soaked to the bone. sokka looks at me as if i were a drop of water and he's been stuck in the desert for weeks.

he cups my face and slides his fingers down until he reaches my lips. he shifts his glance from my eyes to my mouth and back.

he presses his lips into mine once again, not as shy as the first time and i'm one hundread percent sure i'll desintegrate right now.

he moves one hand to my waist in order to help me support my own weight, because i'm literally melting and i can't even stand by myself. my heart is beating faster than i ever thought was possible and it's sending exploding fireworks all over my body.

"you have no idea how much i've been wanting to do this" sokka says when we pause to breathe.

"tell me about it" i gasp.

we let our forheads rest against each other and we interwine our fingers. our hands seem to have been created to fit perfectly together and i know that the shivers i'm getting are not from the freezing rain falling over us.

&#45;------------------

## Chapter 19

**sokka's POV**

when i wake up, the first thing that comes to my mind is the boy with golden eyes that stole my heart. i smile instantly and i would have believed that last night was all a dream, if i didn't still feel his lips on mine.

kissing zuko is hallucinating, it makes me feel like we are the only people left on earth and that nothing else matters. it's even better than cactus juice, and oh boy, do i love cactus juice.

"sokka, you're radiating, did i miss anything?" katara asks me when i meet her in the kitchen for breakfast.

oh, right, we didn't tell anyone about the kiss. i look at her with a smirk printed on my face and her eyes get so big that they might as well jump from their sockets.

"did- did you and- did something happen between you and zuko?!"

"mm, maybe" i grin and look down at my plate.

she squeaks and starts jumping around, fluttering her hands.

"TELL ME EVERYTHING" she shouts, overly excited.

it's an order, not a request, so i have no choice but to spit everything out. i tell her how zuko ran outside after suki kissed me, then i went after him and we confessed our love for each other - more detailed, of course.

"that is soo romantic" her eyes sparkle, but a second later, her brows furrow. "why didn't you tell me last night?!"

she punches me in the arm and tries to look angry.

"i- well- it was a bit of a shock for both of us, ok? it still sounds too good to be true"

oh, i forgot to mention that after we kissed, we found a slightly more private place that wasn't in the pouring rain and we talked for a while.

he told me about how he started liking me since the first day he came to school and then, gradually, he started falling for me harder and harder. he said he was blown away by the fact that i was the first person that showed kindness to him and tried to get to know him.

i literally did the bare minimum and i got blessed with this dark haired angel having a crush on me? i sure am a lucky guy.

i then proceeded to tell him about how unaware i was of my feelings for him. how i was clearly drawn to him from the very beginnig, but i didn't realise exactly why. i thought it was just my desire to have a best friend, but it seems to have been a lot more than that...

"we gotta leave earlier today, we're gonna pick zuko up" i say while i finish my sandwich and start putting my shoes on.

"aww, you're driving to the opposite side of town just to get your boyfriend" katara squeals, clapping her hands.

"yeah, yeah, just hurry" i roll my eyes, but i can't help but smile.

*boyfriend.*

i mean, we haven't talked about labels yet, but i assume that naturally, we *are* together. or at least we will be very soon.

&#45;-

once i pull up to his house, i tell katara to wait in the car while i make my way to his front door. after a few knocks, i expect him to be the one who opens up, but instead, my eyes meet his uncle's.

"good morning" i give him a smile and he returns the favour. "is zuko ready?"

"hello, sokka, come inside, he'll be down in a bit" iroh invites me in the house by extending one arm behind him.

"um- sure, thank you" i say and step inside.

he leads me to the couch in the living room and starts pouring me some tea. i'm not really fond of it, but i cannot refuse him. he leaves the cups on a table and sits down next to me, staring into my eyes for a moment.

he then suddenly pulls me into a hug and at first, i am taken by surprise, but then i pat him on the back, too.

"thank you" he whispers and then withdraws.

"what for?" i ask him confused and start scratching the back of my neck.

"for making my nephew happy" he puts a hand on my shoulder. "i haven't seen him like this since-well, to be honest, i've never seen him truly happy... until last night when he came home"

hearing that fills my heart with joy. just the thought of zuko smiling makes me want to offer him my heart on a platter.

"you should see him when he talks about you..." iroh chuckles briefly. "his eyes start glowing like you're the only thing bringing light into his life and he can't hold his smile"

zuko's uncle sighs and wipes a little tear from the corner of his eye, then he goes on.

"witnessing everything that zuko's been through so far was extremly painful for me, and now that he's finally found peace... i'm asking you to be careful with his heart. he's pretty much convinced that he's not worthy of you and maybe at some point he'll try to cast you away because he thinks you deserve so much more"

how can he possibly think that? i am the one that doesn't deserve him, with that pure heart and killer smile...

"i know he has the tendency to push people away, but i would never, *ever* leave him. i promise" i state and iroh nods, smiling.

"zuko's been like a son to me ever since my only child passed away-"

dammit, i had no idea that he had to bury his own son... another proof that horrible things happen to the kindest people.

"-and now... i'm more than elated to say that i have two sons" iroh adds and rubs my back for a second.

his affirmation completely melted my heart and i don't even know how to respond to such a thing.

"i am truly honoured" i bow my head a little and he pats it, in a fatherly way.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

yes, i eavesdropped on the whole conversation uncle had with sokka, from the top of the stairs. no, i don't regret hearing the two of them bond together.

i begin walking down the stairs, pretending i don't know they've been talking about me for the past few minutes.

"mornin' " i say once i'm downstairs. "let's go, i don't want us to be late because of me"

"hey zuko" sokka adds and doesn't take his eyes off me even for a second.

we say goodbye to uncle and hurry to get in the car, where katara greets me from the back seat.

"agh, zuko, you're like... my brother now! isn't that cool?" she bumps her fist into my arm while we're on our way to school.

i giggle and glare at sokka, who is focusing on the road.

"yeah, it really is" i agree with her.

i'm flattered that she is so excited for me and sokka and that she supports us. whatever *us* means at the moment.

&#45;-

my throat started feeling sore since the first lesson, but now i am sitting in literature class, with sokka on my left and my head is pounding.

i let my pencil down and rub my temples in circular motions, but it makes no difference. i begin moving my head around and the images are getting slightly blurry.

"hey, zuko, are you ok? you look really pale" sokka asks me and i can tell he's worried.

my first instinct is to say that i'm fine, but i know he genuiny cares about me so i decide to be honest.

"i'm feeling a little dizzy. i probably caught a cold yesterday, from the rain" i inform him.

he reaches to touch my forhead with the back of his hand and he squeezes his lips in concern.

"you're burning" he states. "we should get you to a hospital"

"sokka, it's just a stupid flu-" i cough a few times.

he sighs and shakes his head, then he starts tapping his foot nervously.

"excuse me" he adresses the teacher. "zuko is sick, can i take him home?"

spirits, now everybody turned their heads around to stare at us. sokka doesn't seem to have a problem with that, but i'm very uncomfortable.

"of course, just get back as fast as you can" she tells him and we gather our stuff so we can leave.

&#45;-

"sokka, don't you think you're overreacting a little bit?" i question as he's tucking me under a second blanket (he said that one wasn't enough). "i look like a caterpillar"

"you're literally shivering, so no, i don't think i'm overreacting" he states. "and hey, at least you're a cute caterpillar!"

i sneeze and then sokka hurries to bring me some hot tea with honey and lemon. i take a sip and i can feel the warm liquid tingling my throat.

"are you comfortable? do you want me to bring you anything else?" he asks me.

spirits, he's so sweet when he's caring and panicking over nothing.

"i'm ok, sokka, you can go back to school"

"but i don't want to" he whines.

he climbs on the bed and snuggles up with me, hugging me waist.

"you'll get sick too if you stay so close to me" i try to gently move him, but he's really stubborn, so he grips me tighter.

"good, then i'd be able to skip school and stay with you all day"

he starts placing little pecks all over my face, starting with my forehead, nose and cheeks, making me giggle. i try to push him away and pretend i don't enjoy it, but he pins my hands with his on each side of my head.

after a thousand little kisses, he stops with his face a few inches above mine and just stares at me. then at my lips. i involuntarily set my eyes on his as well and we sit like this, none of us moving, for a few seconds that actually feel like hours.

i can't resist the tension anymore, so i lift my head in order to unite our lips and i don't known if i'm burning because of the fever or because of him.

kissing sokka feels like stepping on fluffy clouds, floating relentlessly and getting carried away by the breeze, while being drowned in soft flower petals. honestly, i could do this all day.

"mmh-" sokka grunts. "as much as i *love* doing this, i should probably go"

he arranges some of my hair behind my ear while analysing me. he gives me one last quick kiss and then he gets up.

"after school i gotta go to work, but i'll come by tomorrow" sokka assures me and i nod in understanding.

how am i supposed to survive without his kisses until tomorrow?

**-- (the next day)**

when i hear another knock on my door, i close the book that i was just reading.

"uncle, i swear, if you bring me any more tea-"

but the door opens and a bunch of colourful balloons are being pushed from outside into my room. after a few seconds, sokka succeeds in getting all of them inside, but almost falls over from the pressure of pushing them.

"what the-" i mumble.

"hey! hi! how are you feeling? do you still have a headache? is your fever gone? how's your throat?"

i giggle at the sight of a dozen helium balloons in rainbow colours floating around in my room.

"i'm alright, sokka, you don't have to worry"

"well, if you had answered my last text, i wouldn't be worrying!" he rolls his eyes and takes a seat next to me.

"we literally talked half an hour ago and i told you i was feeling ok. besides, i was reading, so i didn't check my phone"

since he left yesterday, he texted me like, every 10 minutes, to make sure i was alive, eating and staying hydrated.

"yeah, yeah, excuses" he says and pulls me into a hug. "i missed you" his words muffle into my hair and he kisses my head.

"i missed you too" i smile and look up at him.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

i lean in and kiss him breifly. damn, this is all i've been thinking of the entire day.

"read to me" i gesture at the book that he placed on his nightstand when i came in.

"it's the middle of the book, i don't think you'll underst-"

"please" i give him my puppy eyes and raise my eyebrows suggestively.

i know he can't resist them, so he accepts. i make myself comfortable, burying my face in his chest, taking in his familiar scent and i hugging him tight.

he starts reading, but the words that reach out to me are tangled and i can't focus on *what* he is saying.

all i know is that i'm bewitched by his voice and the way he pronounces every word that comes out of that pretty little mouth of his. i can't stop staring at him and how his lips are moving harmoniously.

"zuko, what are we?" i interrupt him and he moves his gaze from the pages of the book, to me.

"what? um- i don-" he begins talking.

"can i be your boyfriend?" i cut him off once again.

his cheeks instantly get red and a slight smile appears on his face.

"do you really have to ask?" he chuckles.

i change my position so i can sit straight and look better at him.

"i just want to make it official" i say. "if you do too, of course" i add.

he raises an eyebrow, then puts the book away. he grabs my hand and pulls me closer to him, crashing his lips into mine with passion.

"of course i do, dummy"

## Chapter 20

zuko's POV

for the last few days, sokka has refused to leave my side until i felt better, except for when he had to go to class or work. today, i can finally return to school and i feel better than ever, after almost a week of sleeping peacefully next to sokka.

the first classes pass painfully slow, because all i think about is him and how much i can't wait to see him at lunch, as we don't have common lessons today.

once my biology class is over and it's time to head to lunch, i walk over to my locker in order to leave there the books i no longer need. my face instantly lights up when i notice sokka leaning on my locker with a soft smile painted on his face.

"i missed you" he says while carefully brushing my hair with his hand.

"it's only been a few hours since you last saw me" i chuckle.

"oh, don't act like you weren't thinking about me the whole time" he raises an eyebrow.

he stares at me with those sparkly blue eyes that warm my heart in a second.

"ok, ok, i missed you too" i admit, rolling my eyes.

he smirks victoriously and pulls me closer to him by my belt. he presses his forehead against mine and sighs, moving his fingers all over my chest.

"i want to kiss you, zuko. really, really bad"

i gulp, then slowly interwine my fingers with his and i start finding it difficult to breathe.

"we shouldn't-" i mumble. "people will see and it would be inappropriate..."

i can't believe i'm saying this, but i'm starting to understand those couples that always make out in public. it's really hard to stay away from the person you love, even for a few hours.

"yeah, it really would" jet appears out of nowhere in front of us.

we distance ourselves and sokka is visibly annoyed as hell.

"spirits, just fuck off, can't you see you're interrupting something?" sokka spits out angrily.

jet raises his arms in defense.

"damn, dude, chill. i come in peace" jet assures us.

"what do you want?" i ask him.

he shifts his weight from one leg to another and keeps chewing on his toothpick.

"i wanted to apologise for how i acted at the party when i was drunk"

"yeah, you better be" sokka says at the same time as i say "it's fine"

jet rubs the back of his neck awkwardly and moves his eyes from me to sokka and so on.

"right..." he mumbles after a few seconds. "i also wanted to tell you that i'm happy for you guys and that i started talking to this boy, haru, and he's really nice, so i won't be bothering you anymore"

i feel that sokka is about to say something, probably that we don't give a fuck about who he's talking to, but i squeeze his hand to make him shut up.

"that's really great, jet. i hope it works out for you" i smile a little and he returns the gesture.

"yeah... thank you. and sorry again. i'll leave you guys to it, see ya" he waves his hand and walks away.

after he leaves, me and sokka start heading twords the lunch area.

"why are you being so nice to that asshole?" sokka questions me, furrowing his brows.

i lift my shoulders and scratch my temple for a second.

"i don't know, it doesn't bring me any satisfaction to act like a jerk"

sokka puts one hand on my waist and brings me closer to him, also reducing the distance between his lips and my ear.

"you're so kind it's annoying" he whispers and then pauses. "but it's also really hot"

my cheeks start burning and i can feel him smirking, while his warm breath on my skin is giving me goosebumps. i gently push him away.

"stop, sokka, we're in public" i remind him.

"what do you mean? i'm not doing anything" he says innocently, fixing me with his big eyes.

oh, he knows damn well what he's doing.

&#45;-

"zuko!!!" aang shouts when he sees me. "are you feeling better?"

me and sokka occupy our seats at the lunch table. i haven't seen my friends in the last few days and i really missed them.

"yes, aang, i'm great" i grin.

"of course he's feeling good, twinkle toes. he had snoozles to cuddle with and kiss him where it hurt" toph mocks us.

we both start blushing and staring at each other.

"leave them alone, toph, they are so cute together" katara squeacks, squishing her own face in awe.

she really is excited about our relationship. what a supportive queen.

"i'm just joking, sugar queen" toph sighs and then adresses us. "besides, i'm relieved that you two idiots finally decided to get together. as much as i was enjoying feeling you both get all flustered around each other, it was starting to get frustrating"

"wait, you knew he liked me back this whole time?" i ask her indignated.

she smirks and shrugs with her eyebrows lifted.

"of course i knew. i just wanted to see you embarrass yourself in front of him until you couldn't hold it in anymore"

i face palm myself. she is evil.

"wait, toph, you knew zuko liked me? and you didn't tell me?" sokka intervenes.

"it wasn't my secret to tell"

sokka slaps his forehead with his palm and drags it all the way to his chin.

"you mean we could have been together earlier, but we were too dumb" he concludes.

"exactly" toph approves and continues eating her food.

&#45;- (after lunch)

i'm sitting in economy class, staring at the window because literally anything is more interesting that whatever the teacher is saying, when i get a text from sokka.

ponytail guy: tell the teacher you gotta go to the bathroom and meet me in the hallway.

what in the world does he want? to be honest, i don't even care, as long as i get to see him and escape this boring class.

i get permission to go to the bathroom, and as soon as i step out the door, i feel a hand grabbing my arm. without saying a word, sokka pulls me to a room i've never entered before.

"what's in there?" i ask him while he's unlocking the door.

"my friend jerry gave me the key to his closet" he says as if i'm supposed to know who that person is.

he finishes unlocking and opens the door.

"who the fuck is jerry?" i wonder confused.

he pulls me inside the small, dark room by the wrist and shuts the door.

"the janitor" he clarifies.

right, then the buckets and all the cleaning stuff that are everywhere around this room make sense.

**⚠**️warning - it's getting spicy**⚠**️

he quickly pushes me against the wall and starts kissing me like he hasn't seen me in years. i don't oppose, because i've been longing for this all day, just like him.

"wh- what if someone comes in?" i manage to say when he pulls away in order to catch his breath.

"i asked jerry to keep watch" sokka explains. "from a distance, so he can't hear anything" he adds with a grin on his face.

he brings my hips closer to his without breaking our lips apart. he buries one hand in my hair, pulling it lightly so my head would slightly tilt back, and with the other one, he makes his way from my abdomen, to my chest and then my neck.

the hand that was in my hair is now placed on my lower back, making sure i'm not moving away, while he leaves the other on my neck, squeezing just a little bit. i involuntarily let out a soft moan in his mouth and he smirks instantly.

"you like that, huh?" he taunts me.

i'm a bottom, what the hell did he expect? he'll probably make fun of me later, but i couldn't care less.

i place my hands on his hips and turn him around so that now he's the one with his back against the wall. i cup his jaw with both of my hands and continue kissing him.

"what do you think you're doing?" he chuckles softly and immediately flips me so we get back to our original position.

he pins both of my wrists above my head with only one hand and uses the other one to trace different shapes on my back, sending shivers all over my body.

he starts making his way with his lips from my mouth, to my chin, jawline and then my neck, leaving a small red mark on my sweet spot as soon as he finds it. spirits, this should be illegal 'cause it's killing me softly.

"s-sokka..." i gulp. "everyone will be able to see that" i say breathing heavily.

he stops what he's doing and stares right into my eyes, making me forget how breathing works.

"good" he states. "let them know you're mine"

**⚠**️end of warning**⚠**️

he winks at me and smirks, then runs his fingers through my hair to arrange it, as i assume it got pretty messy.

"i hope you didn't forget that i have an important game tonight, so i want you to be there and wear my jersey" sokka tells me, poking my chest with his index.

"ugh, like in a cheesy romantic movie?" i ick.

he gives me a quick kiss and runs his thumbs across my cheeks.

"yup, exactly like in a cheesy romantic movie"

&#45;- (time skip)

i'm sitting with the gAang in one of the front rows of the bleachers and the basketball game against the team from Omashu highschool is about to start. sokka and his teammates are standing on the side of the field, in a tight circle, discussing strategies, while their opponents are doing the same thing at a reasonable distance.

i know sokka's been training extremely hard for this match and i can't describe how excited i am to see him in action. i love the enthusiasm that he uses to encourage his team, which makes him a really great captain.

before the game begins, sokka glances at me and we both smile like idiots at each other. i give him a thumbs up and he winks at me, making me blush lightly, then jogs towards the field.

"kick their asses, snoozles!" toph shouts, throwing her fists in the air.

we all yell words of encouragement and he seems to be filled with even more energy than before.

soon, both teams are running around the field, giving their best to score. sokka is literally glowing and it's no wonder he was assigned captain. his movements are smooth and precise, while the communication between the members of his team is clearly on point.

our team is in advantage, thanks to the points that mostly sokka scored, but the opponents aren't so bad either, keeping the match tight.

my gorgeous boyfriend is dribbling the ball at the moment, heading towards the enemies' basket case, avoiding the other players with incredible skill and mobility. he looks at me before he jumps in the air pretty high, in order to throw the ball, but a guy from the opposite team crashes himself into sokka in order to get a grip of the ball.

shaken by the impact, sokka innevitably falls on the ground and my heart drops when i see his leg in a distorted, twisted position, followed by a scream that pierces right through my heart.

my legs start moving without giving me time to think, even before the referee blows his whistle to stop the game. in a matter of seconds i find myself on the field, ignoring the coach yelling at me and saying i'm not allowed to be there.

"sokka!" i shout and crumble on the ground next to him. "oh spirits, oh shit, can you move your leg?"

i'm trying hard not to faint or puke, or both, because my heart is in my throat at the moment. sokka attempts to extend his leg, but i wish he hadn't done that.

"AGH- dammit" he curses. "it hurts like a bitch"

i take his hand in mine and he squeezes it tightly, but i let him do it, even though he's almost crushing my bones. if it helps him with the pain, it's totally worth it.

"it will be ok, you will be ok, you-" my voice breaks and i notice that my face is covered in tears because my vision is blurry.

i didn't even realise when an ambulance got here, everything is happening too fast, i can only perceive images of it. somehow, sokka got inside of the ambulance, but when i try to hop in myself, i get stopped.

"are you two related?" a person from the medical crew asks me.

"n- no, but-" i stutter.

"sorry, family members only" she cuts me off.

at some point, katara joined him in the car and she's begging the paramedics to let me come, but there's no point to it.

"i'm his fucking boyfriend, let me in!" i shout, but all i get is a door shut in my face.

i rub my face with my palms to get rid of the salty tears. i can't believe i'm crying in front of so many people, but this is the last of my concerns right now.

"FUCK" i yell and start running towards the bus station as fast as i can.

i must get to the hospital as soon as possible, i can't possibly leave him alone. when i finally reach the closest station, the only bus that makes a stop at the hospital is just leaving.

shit, the universe really hates me, huh? i check the programme for the buses and see that the next one will be here in 45 minutes. waiting that long is out of the question.

what if his leg is badly broken? what if he won't be able to play basketball ever again? he would be crushed, this sport is extremely important to him.

i begin running and i force myself to keep going, even though my lungs are burning in flames and my heart is threatening to jump out of my chest.

i fucking hate running, but i don't stop until i reach the hospital, despite my legs almost giving in and my entire body shaking badly.

## Chapter 21

zuko's POV

"how is he?" i ask katara as soon as i manage to find her in this huge hospital.

i know i look like shit, i just ran from the other side of town, my face is all red and i'm really sweaty, but i don't give a crap. all i care about is him.

"they're currently running some tests to see if it's anything severe" she informs me with a concerned look on her face. "damn, you look like shit"

"really?" i wonder sarcastically. "i didn't notice! i just ran my ass off, 'tara!"

i take a seat in the waiting room next to her and every fiber in my body is thankful for even a second of rest.

"i'm sorry, zuko, i tried to make them let you come. i yelled at them the entire ride, until i thought they'd kick me out" katara gently pats my back.

i appreciate her effort, but right now it's irrelevant, all that matters is that i'm here now.

the only noise in this bland white room that smells like antiseptic is coming from my foot that is tapping against the floor nervously. i place my elbows on my knees and interwine my fingers while resting my head on my fists. my brain is constantly buzzing with thoughts.

is he suffering? yes, what a dumb question, of course he is, and i'm not there to ease his pain in any way i can.
