what if he has to go through a surgery? what if he can never fully recover? what is he has to give up basketball?  
what if it's so serious that they have to cut his leg off? well, that's a bit too much, but you never know, it might be a possibility!

i can't stop but consider that this is my fault. he looked at me. he looked at me right before he was tackled by his opponent. he was distracted, so he didn't see him coming. if it wasn't for me, he would have kept his damn eyes on the stupid ball and none of this would've happened.

the wait is killing me, because i'm just stuck with my own thoughts, analysing every possible outcome. i keep staring at the clock and it's barely moving, but it's annoying ticking makes sure to stab my brain repeatedly until i feel like i'm going nuts.

i just want to know if my baby is ok.

after a thousand years, i lay my eyes on a chubby nurse with her hair tied up in a tight bun, who is just coming out of the room where sokka is in.

"you can now see him" she adresses katara, but in less than a second, i jump from my seat.

i almost push her out of the way so i can get in there as fast as i can. katara is right behind me when we enter sokka's room and not only him, but also a doctor with square glasses and a bunch of files in his hands is standing there.

my lunatic eyes meet sokka's at last. he gives me a hopeful smile, but i couldn't return the favour even if my life was depending on it.

"sokka-" my voice sounds like someone just squeezed my vocal chords together and tied them in a knot.

with a few steps, i reach the bed that sokka is laying on and i desperstely put my arms around him, like i'm afraid he might vanish right before my eyes.

"it's ok, zuzu, i'm alive, i'm ok" sokka chuckles and brushes my hair with his fingers, bringing me even closer to him.

i break the embrace just so i can look at his face and stare into his ocean eyes.

"oh, baby, why are you crying?" he puts his palms on my face and wipes some stray tears with his thumbs.

"i was so worried" my voice cracks. "are you in pain? will you be alright? will your leg heal?" i question frantically.

i hear someone clearing their throat and i turn my head towards where the sound came from.

"i think i can answer those questions" the doctor says, with an awkward and forced smile plastered across his face.

frankly, i absolutely forgot about his existence, or, as a matter of fact, about anyone else's existence. i gotta stop forgetting that sokka and i aren't the only people populating the earth.

"sokka has a medium degree ankle sprain, nothing that cannot be fixed. we checked vigorously, in order to be certain that there isn't any deeper problem. we put a cast around the affected area, that he will have to keep for around 2 or 3 weeks. we're going to keep him checked in the hospital for a few more days, so that we make sure there are no blood clots, infections, or signs of internal bleeding"

i let out a sigh of relief and squeeze sokka's warm hand in mine. he's gonna be ok. i was worried sick for no reason.

"thank you, doctor" katara tells him and he realises that's his cue to go, so he leaves the room.

i am sitting on my knees beside sokka's bed, while katara, having a better developed brain than mine, takes a seat on one of the chairs in the room.

"toph and aang will be here in a bit" she lets us know.

sokka scoots over and pulls me by the hand, encouraging me to stand up and get on the bed next to him.

"guys, there was no need for y'all to panick like that. see? it's not a big deal" sokka says, trying to sound tough.

this bed is fit for only one person, but i lay on my side, facing him, so i don't take up much space. i lean my head on his shoulder and wrap my arms around his torso, while he hugs me back, diminishing the space between us as much as he can.

"i couldn't have known if your injury was severe or not, sokka. i kept thinking of the worst things possible and i thought i was gonna lose my mind" i tell him.

he gives me a peck on the forehead and i squeeze my eyes shut, asimilating all the affection he puts in every single touch that encounters my body.

"i'm sorry you had to go through that" his words get muffled in my hair.

"your leg got injured and you're sorry for me?"

"well- uh- yeah" he chuckles and smiles in a way that makes my heart liquify.

"dumbass" i mumble in his shirt and he giggles a little more.

how does he succeed in lightening up any situation only with a smile and his positive attitude? spirits, i love him.

"now i assume i'll have to help your ass move everywhere in the house until you take that thing off" katata gestures towards sokka's cast.

"i can do it" i offer before i even get to think properly.

i don't take my eyes off sokka even for a second, and he does the same thing.

i swear, he's the most addicive drug in the world, and i'm a first class junkie.

"sounds good to me" 'tara says satisfied and leans back on her seat.

"you don't have to-" sokka begins, but i cut him off.

"shut up, it's my turn to take care of you" i poke his chest with my index.

he lifts my chin up with his fingers and stares right into my eyes, then at my lips.

"i have no complaints about that" he says and presses his lips on mine.

a wave of electricity strikes my body instantly and i forget that his sister is literally right there.

"you're making me wanna puke my guts out and i can't even see you"

we withdraw from the kiss only to glance at the door and see toph standing there, next to aang.

"sokka!" aang exclaims. "are you ok? we got here as fast as we could"

"yes, it's just a sprain, nothing i can't handle" sokka grins.

we all hang out there and eat some food that toph and aang brought, while joking around about everything, until the night comes. at some point, everyone is bound to go home, but i refuse to do so.

"zuko, please go home and rest, i'm a big boy, i can handle being alone" sokka says once we're the only ones in the room.

"but i can't" i state bluntly. "i won't let you stay in this depressing place all by yourself. and besides, you know i can only sleep well when you're with me, so there's no point in me leaving"

i shrug and he opens his mouth to say something, but he closes it back after a few seconds.

"the nurses won't allow you spend the night here" he says at last.

"hmm, good thing toph bribed them to let me do that" i smirk.

"what?! when did that even happen?"

an expression of confusion covers his face, but i flutter my hand in the air.

"don't worry about it and just kiss me, you idiot" i request.

sokka chuckles softly and places his hand on the back of my neck.

"your wish is my command"

&#45;- (the next day)

unfortunately, i had to leave the hospital early in the morning in order to stop by at home, take a shower and change my clothes before going to school.

"um- zuko?" a voice startles me while i'm searching for the english book in my locker.

i turn around only to see suki standing before me. ok, this is going to be awkward as fuck, because i kinda stole her boyfriend, even though they weren't really dating at that time.

oh shit, i bet everybody figured out that me and sokka are together, after the game incident. i didn't even stop to think about this.

"yes?" i raise my eyebrow curious of what she has to say to me.

"someone asked me to tell you that the coach wants to talk to you in the locker room. like, right now" she informs me.

wh- what does he want to talk about with me? my heart starts pounding in my chest, even though i'm pretty sure i did nothing wrong.

"uh- thanks, suki" i scratch the back of my neck and she smiles awkwardly, then leaves.

i forget about the english book and head to the locker room, but when i enter, there's no sign of the P. E. teacher, but instead, a big part of the basketball team is here. i don't even know any of their names.

"uhm- is coach here? i heard he asked to see me..." i mumble, visibly uncomfortable.

"actually" a tall, blonde guy starts adressing me. "we wanted to talk to you"

i gulp. they're all staring at me. what the hell is this? it doesn't look very good and my skin is starting to tingle.

"i- i think i should probably get to class-" i take a step backwards.

i turn around to face the door, but i meet another solid dude that is blocking the exit with his body.

shit, shit, shit.

"um- can you please move?" i ask the bulky guy that is standing between me and the way out, trying to sound calm.

of course he doesn't, he just grabs my arms and turns me back around.

"hear us out first" the blonde guy replies.

i sink my nails in the palm of my hand, feeling all the fibers in my body get tense.

"what do you want?" i sigh.

he puckers up his lips, pretending to be thinking about it.

"ok, i won't beat it around the bush, we don't like the fact that you infected our captain with..." he pauses a second just to look at me with disgust. "...your gayness"

if i wasn't so anxious right now, i would have probably laughed so fucking hard.

"uhm- sorry to beak it to you, but that's not how sexuality works" i inform him.

he doesn't seem very pleased with my answer.

"then how come he was in a happy relationship with suki for a long time before you came to this school and turned him into a faggot?" he spits out.

suki. she asked me to come here, so i can just assume she has something to do with this.

"i don't see how that concerns any of you" i glance at them, one by one.

"spirits, you're so annoying!" the blonde guy says and, before i know it, his fist makes contact with my face.

i lose my balance, but i am able to regain it, so i don't fall off my feet.

"shit, what the fuck is your problem?" i spit out some blood on the floor.

i'm about to push him, but the guy that was blocking the door keeps me imobilised with his arms. i struggle to break away, but i am soon distracted by the sharp pain shooting from my stomach, making me grunt.

"we don't need to have a twink as our captain, as it's a manly thing, so you better break up with him and go on with your useless life" the blonde says.

"like hell i will" i fix his eyes with mine, not letting myself seem intimidated.

he stares at me for a few seconds, and then he bursts into laughter.

"huh, i assumed you were gonna say that" he grins like a fucking idiot. "boys, help him change his mind, will you?"

and just like that, the guy that was holding me still, throws me on the floor and i'm too shaken by the sudden movement to anticipate the wave of hits that follows after that.

i can barely breathe, but i manage to curl up with my knees as close to my chest as possible, so that my vital organs are protected.

i'm an idiot, why am i accepting this shit? i suddenly feel my anger growing bigger and bigger, until it lets itself out.

"ENOUGH" i shout and some wild flames start shooting from my hands.

all of them back off and i lift myself just enough to sit with my back pressed against the wall.

"shit! he fucking burned us!" a random guy yells.

some of their clothes are on fire, but they manage to put it out pretty fast, while some of them have light burns on their hands, or legs.

"you're fucking insane!" the blonde boy yells at me. "you freak!"

each of them swear at me as they're storming out of the locker room, to take care of their wounds i assume, leaving me laying there on the floor, trying to find the strenght to move.

what can i say?

be gay, do arson.

## Chapter 22

**sokka's POV**

i'm scrolling through tiktok, bored out of my mind, when i hear two quiet knocks on the door. school isn't over yet, so if it's not zuko, who else could it possibly be?

the door opens and, contrary to my expectations, the one who enters the room *is* zuko. he's holding a big teddy bear in one hand and a box of little chocolates in the other.

"hey, zuko, you didn't have t-" i start saying, but i soon forget all of my thoughts.

when the huge plushie isn't blocking my vision of him anymore, i am able to see his face properly. he has a black eye, with purple and blue shades, and his bottom lip is split, some dry blood being very noticeable. i almost jump to my feet, but the cast around my leg reminds me i have to stay put.

"what in the world happened to you?!" i ask, feeling the anger building up inside me.

he ignores my question.

"here, i brought you these so you would feel better" zuko attempts to smile, but fails greatly.

he hands me the teddy bear and the candy, keeping that forced grimace on his face. i put everything aside and pull him on the bed, next to me.

he grunts when he sits down, his hand reaching instinctively to his stomach. i build up my courage and lift his shirt just a little, only to uncover the bruises it was hiding underneath.

it's a lot worse than the last time i saw him like this, after the incident with the random guys on the street. how does he still find power to move? and despide being hurt like hell, he went and bought me all this stuff?

"zuko, *who* did this to you?" my voice cracks. "we should call for a nurse, you're hurting badly"

he shakes his head and i take his face in my hands when he avoids my glare, leaving him no other choice but to look at me with those troubled golden gems.

"stop staring at me like that or i'm gonna start crying" he says.

his voice is weak, full of suffering and it makes me want to drive a knife through my heart just so i can stop what i'm feeling right now.

"zuko, for the love of spirits, tell me right now who i have to murder" i request firmly.

he doesn't answer right away, but he instead throws himself in my arms, burying his face in the crook of my shoulder and squeezing me tightly.

he radiates sadness mixed with exhaustion and i wish i could just take all his negative feelings, make them my own, and fill him with happiness instead.

"please" i beg him. "tell me what happened"

he clutches on my hospital clothes and i pat his back affectionately, but he flinches, which gives me the idea of checking his back as well. i reveal his skin and it's full of purple-ish spots, that make my stomach twirl and twist in every direction.

he retreats from the hug and wipes the corner of his eye with his index, then he starts talking.

"suki came to me this morning a- and told me that coach wanted to see me. i went to the locker room, b- but there was no sign of the coach, just... your teammates-"

i don't like where this is going, i already feel my fists clenching.

"-they apparently don't agree with their captain having a boyfriend" he stares at me with his eyes sparkling because of the tears that he's barely managing to hold back. "they said that it's my fault you broke up with suki, cause i turned you gay-"

*what?* what the actual fuck? did suki ask them to do this? is she this desperate?

"-of course, i said i wouldn't break up with you because they said so. they decided to '*convince*' me... by force"

he gestures to his face and the rest of his body, as if i didn't get what 'by force' means.

"i can't fucking believe this!" i shout.

he puts his index and middle finger on my lips, to urge me to stop talking and let him finish.

"at some point, i exploded and shot fire at them and some of them got hurt" he adds.

as they fucking deserve, i cannot believe the people i shared tears, sweat and laughter with, were capable of something like this.

"you must report this to the principal, it's unacceptable" i growl in disgust.

he puffs and rolls his eyes.

"here comes the funny part" he says in a stern voice.

what in the world could be funny about this whole situation?

"they went to the principal and told him i attacked them out of nowhere, for no reason, so they beat me up in self defense. that complete idiot bought it" he chuckles painfully. "i tried to explain that they are lying, but it was my word against theirs. i got suspended for two weeks"

my whole body is shaking with anger and frustration.

"i'll burn the whole school down after i lock them all inside" i spit out, punching the bed with my right fist. "FUCK, if it wasn't for this stupid leg, i would have killed them right now. nobody hurts my boyfriend and gets away with it"

zuko pushes my chest to make me lay on my back and he carefully does the same, but it's obvious he is suffering. i put my arms around him and he melts in my embrace.

his breathing is irregular, and i assume it's due to his lungs pushing his ribs into the bruised skin that's covering them.

"i'm tired, sokka" he whispers.

the tone of his voice wrecks my heart into pieces.

"tired of what?"

"of all the meaningless hate that i get for being myself" zuko squeaks. "why can't people just mind their own business?"

his voice breaks and he finally lets out some tears. seeing him cry, makes me unable to hold back my emotions and my eyes start watering as well, so we're both straight up sobbing right now.

"you're too pure for this world, baby" i carefully place a peck on his forehead. "i swear, i won't leave your side ever again and i'll give my best to protect you from all the evil things in this world"

"and how do you plan on doing that?" he wonders innocently, letting his words get muffled in my shirt.

"well, with my boomerang, of course" i reply. "and if i ever lose it, i'll just fight everyone who tries to do you harm with my bare hands"

he smiles lightly, rubbing his cheek on my chest to make himself more comfortable and i try to bring him closer, even if the distance between us is practically nonexistent.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

it's like our bodies merge together in a mixture of burning tears, sobs and endless tenderness. his arms become my shelter, making me forget about every little thing that's troubling me right now, while his love and affection could easily replace my need for food and water.

how was i able to live for 18 years without meeting him, without touching him and having him next to me? i know, it's gonna sound like a cliché, but i'm not quite sure that i was living at all.

i was just spinning in an endless loop of just... existing, without a purpose, without a reason to continue surviving for another day. i was stuck in a routine of waking up, doing things to keep me busy, so i don't get overwhelmed with the thought of everything being pointless, going to sleep and then repeat.

i was numb, exhausted, completely empty inside, incapable of feeling... basically anything, until he came into my life and turned my whole world upside down.

with his silly jokes, playful eyes, childish smile and excessively upbeat attitude, he showed me what life's really about, that it's in fact worth living.

whereas i used to walk blindly when i was outside, staring blankly at some uncertain spot in front of me, now i feel like i've been blindfolded my entire life and i'm witnessing everything for the first time.

i never payed attention to any detail of my surroundings, but now i notice when tiny plants are growing out of little sidewalk cracks, and smile involuntarily.

i learned to enjoy the harmonic chirping of the birds that pay me visits in the morning. i used to hate them for being so loud and for scratching my brain with their voices, but now i'm grateful for their presence and i sometimes even leave crumbs of bread on the windowsill so they can have a little treat.

i don't know if it makes any sense, or if i sound as if i belong in a mental institution, but before meeting sokka, my world was all coloured in black, white and some shades of grey, but now...

&#46;..now the grass that touches my feet is greener, it has a life of its own, tingling my bare feet every time me and sokka randomly throw our shoes away and decide we don't need them anymore.

&#46;..now the sun shoots warmer light on my skin, the sky is a hundread times more beautiful, the moon glows brighter than i ever thought was possible and the flowers seem to be colourful and more scented.

he painted my dull life in bright colours  
without realising it.

"sokka-" i squeack, breaking the comforting silence that was settled between us until now.

"yes?"

"i'm scared" i whisper.

he shifts his position so he can look me right in the eyes and his brows furrow, forming a barely noticeable wrinkle between them.

"of what?" he questions.

"i don't- i don't know" i sigh. "of us. of me. of my emotions"

i shut my eyes for a second, to gather up my thoughts, because he's waiting for an explanation. why do i find it so hard to put my feelings into words?

"i feel like i'm getting to the point where my existence is entirely dependent on you and- and it's *consuming* me, swallowing me whole" i confess. "and it terrifies me that... if we ever break up, then-"

i pause for a few seconds, because the mere thought makes my body shiver.

"-then i won't know how to function anymore. i won't know how to breathe, how to move, how to speak... how to live"

my voice trembles and sokka cups my face with one of his hands, moving his thumb slowly over my right cheek.

"zuko, you know i would never-"

"i know" i cut him off. "you wouldn't do anything to hurt me purposely, but... what if we get torn apart by circumstance? what if-"

he gently presses his index on my lips, to prevent me from talking.

"do you know the difference between a hero and a villain?" he asks me without breaking the eye contact.

i shake my head, even though i don't really see how it has anything to do with our discussion, but then he proceedes to explain.

"a hero would give up the person he loves to save the world, but a villain would sacrifice the whole world to save them"

suddenly, all the air in my lungs dissapears and my heart starts bouncing around until it reaches my throat.

"i'm willing to burn the whole world down for you, zuko. without hesitation"

he stares at me as if i am the only thing he has ever laid his eyes on and he leans in to brush his lips over mine. i struggle to keep my own head from falling, and he notices that, so he helps me support it with his warm, gentle hand.

he uses this opportunity to pull me into a deeper kiss, that results in my veins being overwhelmed with millions, thousands, billions of exploding fireworks that make me feel like i've been struck by lightning.

"i would burn down the world for you too" i say in the breif moment between two kisses.

he gives me a faded smile and runs his fingers through my hair before meeting my lips once again.

vivid electricity has completely taken over my body, but i can't complain about it.

i'm willing to let myself drown in the love i carry for him, even if it entirely destroys me.

## Chapter 23

**zuko's POV**

it's been over a week since sokka was released from the hospital and his cast has been replaced with some bandages wrapped tightly around his ankle, so he can pretty much handle himself now. however, this didn't stop me from sleeping over at his house since then... just in case.

"c'mon, zuko, everyone's waiting for us at the cafe. katara will surely murder us" sokka says to me.

i'm sitting at the dining table (at sokka's hose, of course), trying to finish my pancakes peacefully, but sokka is constantly rushing me, because he gulped down his breakfast in a second.

"it's not my fault that i don't eat as if i have to fill 5 stomachs, like *someone* i know" i look at him suggestively, raising my eyebrow.

i pour some more whipped cream on my pancakes and then add some colourful sprinkles for flavour.

"you're gonna get sick from all that whipped cream, you already ate half of the can" sokka warns me.

yes, i know i ate a bit too much of it, but i love it, so kill me for it!

"so what? are you gonna stop me?" i mutter as i fill my mouth with whipped cream until it's overflowing, just to spite him.

before i get to put the can down, sokka appears next to me, grabs my face with one hand and puts his mouth over mine, blocking a gasp of surprise. his tongue wanders around in my mouth in slow, careful motions, gathering as much whipped cream as he possibly can.

he breaks the kiss and we both swallow, staring straight at each other. his face is inches away from mine and he starts cleaning the remains of the cream from around my mouth with... with his lips.

i'm too overwhelmed by his touch to move, so i'm pretty much frozen in my chair, like an idiot.

once he withdraws, he smirks and wipes the corner of his mouth with his thumb, slowly tracing it until he reaches the middle of his bottom lip*.*

*i'm pretty sure this should be illegal.*

"mmm, i don't usually like whipped cream, but damn, that was yummy" he gives me a devilish grin and ruffles my hair without taking his eyes off me.

you'd think i would have gotten used to his kisses and touches in the time we've been together... i haven't. he's still close to giving me heart attacks every single time he comes near me.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

when me and zuko enter the coffeeshop, we quickly lay our eyes on the table where the rest of the gAang is chilling. i can tell from this distance that aang is doing his classic trick in which he spins little rocks between his hands with his airbending. they must be really bored without us if he had to resort to that.

"i'm gonna beat the shit out of you if you make me wait again" toph states once we take the two available seats at the table.

"hello to you too!" i roll my eyes. "no *'i missed you, sokka',* no '*how's your ankle, sokka?'* no, spirits, of course not!"

i throw my hands in the air for show and zuko is the only one that chuckles, which is more than enough for me.

"don't be a drama queen, we waited almost an hour for you!" katara intervenes.

her hair loopies jump around when she talks and i can't take her seriously.

"yeah, me and katara asked you all to come here for an important announcement" aang adds.

he loudly sips from his drink that i assume is a peach milkshake, as he always orders the same thing.

"i'm sorry for making you wait, but we were..." zuko begins saying, but i cut him off.

"we were busy" i glance at him suggestively and he looks down at his hands, trying to hide his smile.

he pushes his shoulder into mine playfully and we both start giggling, as i replay the whipped cream incident in my head.

"ew, ew, ew, we didn't need to know that!" toph exclaims.

"what? they just said they were busy, maybe they had to finish some chores" aang suggests.

"that's *not* what i meant, toph! spirits, it's not my fault you got a dirty mind" i say indignated.

toph lays back on her chair, with her arms crossed, mumbling a '*whatever*' and blowing her bangs out of her eyes.

"ok, i'm gonna ignore everything you just said, because me and aang have something to tell you" katara states.

"you're finally together?" me, zuko and toph ask simultaneously.

both aang and katara furrow their brows and glance at each one of us.

"how the hell did you know what i was about to say?" katara squeacks.

"uhm, you could see from a mile away that you liked eachother, duh? honestly i'm surprised you waited this long" i play with the straw in the smoothie that the waiter just brought me.

"yeah, it was pretty obvious" zuko mumbles before shifting his attention to his iced coffee and taking a sip.

"ugh, straight people" toph imitates puking sounds. "i mean, i'm not saying i don't support you, i just don't agree with your lifestyle... it goes against my religion"

me and zuko burst into laughter and a tiny tear comes up in the corner of my eye.

"spirits, toph!" aang says. "you know i'm pan, so please don't offend me like that!"

we all start laughing even harder, except for katara, but soon, she gives in and lets out a smile.

"ha ha, very funny, it's not like i can choose, you idiots" she rolls her eyes.

"yeah, yeah, we know, we just love making fun of you, little sister" i pinch her cheek with my hand and she makes rude faces at me.

"*anyways*" aang says. "i saw there's some carnival coming into town tonight and i think it would be fun to go, what do you guys say?"

carnival. tunnel of love, ferris wheel, rollercoasters, carousel, aiming contests where i can win things for zuko. sounds good to me.

"YES!" toph almost jumps from her seat. "finally, something fun! i can trick those idiots again with my schemes!"

i recall the events of last year, when we went to a carnival. she didn't give up until she won every single game, leaving all the vendors with their jaws dropped and coming home with a whole bunch of cool stuff. toph really is the queen of schemes.

we all agree that we must go to the carnival and we continue discussing about random things. we're probably too loud, because we get some angry stares from the people sitting at the tables near us.

at some point, me and zuko start playing with our feet under the table, which kinda comes involuntarily, as we constantly crave feeling each other close. personally, i can't resist more than one minute without touching him.

when katara asks zuko a question, i place my hand on his thigh and i move it slowly, while occasionally squeezing lightly. he stiffens up and the words are coming distorted out of his mouth.

he tries to push my hand away, but i put it back as soon as he does. oh, how i adore teasing him, just to get these types of reactions.

"snoozles, you know i can feel that, right? can't you help yourself at least in public?" toph exposes me.

zuko's eyes widen and he starts blushing badly, which makes my cheeks heat up too.

"i don't even want to know what she's talking about, you weirdo!" katara drags one hand over her face and uses the other one to punch my arm.

"sorry you had to see that, toph" i mutter.

"i can't see, you idiot!"

i don't get to say anything else, because the waiter comes to our table with a piece of cake on a plate, which he places in front of zuko.

"uhm- i didn't order this-" he says.

"i know. it's on the house" the red haired waiter smiles and then winks at zuko. "you're really pretty, can i maybe- have your number?"

the *audacity*! for some reason, i find his face extreamly punchable.

"um- i- uh-" zuko stutters and i can tell he doesn't know how to turn him down.

he's too polite to handle this. i call out his name and when he turns to face me, i grab him by the collar of his shirt and pull him into a deep kiss that we maintain for a few long seconds. i then put an arm around zuko's shoulders and shoot a death glare at the waiter.

"you were saying...?" i raise an eyebrow without avoiding his stare.

he starts mumbling something about how he'll bring our check immediately and he's obviously uncomfortable, so i grin victoriously as soon as he turns around and leaves.

"yes, snoozles! protect your territory!" toph punches the air with her fist.

"was that really necessary?" zuko questions. "it was kinds rude"

"um, yeah!?" i state as if it's obvious. "having such a hot boyfriend has it's disadvantages, alright?"

"so what, you're gonna scare off every guy thay hits on zuko?" katara asks sarcastically.

i shrug and take one last sip from my drink.

"i can even fight if necessary" i say and place a peck on the back of zuko's hand.

"did you forget that i beat you when we wrestled the last time?" toph reminds me.

i roll my eyes, because no, of course i didn't forget, how could i?

"i let you win, ok?" i try to defend myself.

that's a lie, of course. i gave all i had, but she still kicked my ass senselessly.

"yeah, yeah, keep telling yourself whatever helps you sleep at night"

&#45;- **(time skip)**

the atmosphere of the carnival surrounds us from the very first seconds we arrive and the soppy smell of freshly made popcorn mixed with cotton candy fills up my lungs instantly.

aang and katara part ways with us, probably heading to something like the *tunnel of love* or whatever cheesy things the straights enjoy (sorry aang), while toph ditches us in the blink of an eye and i can only guess that she's ready to pull up her iconic schemes.

"zuko, zuko!" i pull him by the sleeve of his sweatshirt and point at a nearby booth. "LET'S GET CANDY!"

zuko smiles lightly, rolling his eyes and he lets himself be dragged by my excited ass to the pile of lollipops, chocolate, gingerbread houses, jelly and a ton of other stuff.

"oh, man, how am i supposed to pick from all of these delicious things?" i wonder to myself rethorically.

surprisingly, zuko turns out to be extremely patient, because it takes me forever to figure out what i want to eat. i'm really indecisive, but i'm bisexual after all, i can't decide anything by myself, so don't judge me!

"you know the food isn't going anywhere, right?" zuko raises his eyebrow while watching me stuff my mouth with nougat.

spirits, this is so tasty. i offer some to zuko and he declines, but instead he starts eating some pink cotton candy.

"let me enjoy my sweets, ok?" i speak while chewing and pretending to pout at his remark.

"fine, just try not to spit on me" he replies, wiping a spot on his cheek.

i almost choke, but i manage to swallow before we both start chuckling. after we calm down, i use my cotton candy (that is blue) to make a beard, by sticking a bunch of it to my cheeks.

when i turn to face zuko, he starts laughing so hard that tears form in the corner of his eyes.

"you're such a dork" he mutters, as he reaches to take a bite from my beard.

"hey! i've been growing that for months! and you can't just go around eating people's beards, that's rude!" i attempt to sound indignated.

he looks at me with his puppy golden eyes that melt my heart every time they set on me, and he also brings his bottom lip over the upper one.

"ok, ok, i'll forgive you this time, but be careful not to repeat it!" i warn him.

"oh, thank you for your kindness, mister, but i can make up for the inconvenience" zuko smirks.

he rests his arms around my shoulders and stares right at me, then he takes another few bites from my beard, like the naughty kid he is. before i get to protest, he presses his lips against mine and i can feel the hallucinating mix between the sweetness of the cotton candy and the delicious taste of zuko's lips. he drives me crazy.

"why does everything taste better with you?" i ask once he pulls away from the kiss.

zuko grimaces.

"you're so cheesy that i'm gonna puke" he says and nudges me lightly in the arm.

"oh, you love it, stop acting like you don't" i rub my nose against his cheek and he chuckles.

we walk around, sometimes elbowing our way through the crowd, without a fixed target in mind. i interwine my fingers with zuko's so we don't get separated in the sea of people and i swear, his skin is softer than cotton candy.

i abruptly stop walking and zuko bumps into me, as he wasn't prepared for this.

"zuko..." i squeack. "that- that is the BIGGEST FLYING BISON I'VE EVER SEEN"

i point to a plushie that's almost as big as me and it has a brown arrow on it's head, as well as six feet and pointy horns!!

i hop from one foot to the other without letting go of zuko's hand, until we reach a stand where you have to throw some hoops and aim at the neck of some bottles.

"i'm gonna win that for you, baby" i wink at zuko and he rolls his eyes unimpressed.

how hard can it be?

the merchant hands me ten hoops and i have to put them around eight bottles, so can only miss twice. i can surely do it!

&#42;after 15 minutes of failing a million times&#42;

"dammit!" i shout in frustration. "this game must be rigged, there's no way i could've missed so much!"

zuko couldn't stop giggling the whole time i was trying to impress him with my nonexistent skills.

"ugh- sorry i couldn't win you that bison" i scratch the back of my neck and stare at my shoes.

zuko pats me on the back and then steps to the booth, asking for another set of hoops.

"let me try" he says causally.

"ok, but just so you know, it's harder than it looks!"

he leans over the counter and he takes his time, calculating his every move. as a result, he hits his first target. then also the next one, and the one after that. he misses once, but in the end, all of the bottles have a hoop around their necks.

"no way" i whisper to myself.

he's truly amazing. is there anything he can't do?

"congratulations, choose your prize" the vendor adresses zuko.

"i want the sky bison" he requests victoriously, his face lighting up considerably.

after we leave that booth, he hands me the plushie with a big, innocent grin on his face that makes my ego stop hurting so bad.

"this is for you, tough guy" he taunts me.

at first i hesitate, but i step on my pride and decide that this is extremely sweet, even if i wanted to be the one that won it for him, not the other way around.

"you really are the best boyfriend ever" i mumble and squeeze the bison to my chest.

it's so fluffy and soft, that it makes me want to bury my head in its fur and never let go of it. just like i never want to let go of zuko.

"i know i am" he smiles and damn, i would kill anyone just to be able to see him do that for the rest of my life. "so, what do you wanna do next?"

i grab his hand and we stop by every game that seems interesting, one by one. we go to the bumper cars, crashing and chasing each other all over the place, then we head to a rollercoaster, that spins us up, down and in circles, until i almost puke all the candy i've eaten so far.

after that, we enter a photo booth in which we make funny faces for the first pictures, but we save a kiss for the last one, to capture the profound feelings floating between us.

"you really wanna go on the ferris wheel?" zuko asks uncertainly.

"yes, why don't you?"

"cause it's moving so slow that it's boring, and it surely won't stop working when we're at the top, like in those stupid rom-coms that you see everywhere, if that's what you're hoping for" he explains.

i shake my head and squeeze my lips together, but drag him along anyways.

"who knows, maybe it *will* stop, why are you so pessimistic?" i question him.

"i'm just being realistic, but if you want to go, i'll tag along"

i clap my hands excitedly, so we take our seats and get ready to be lifted in the air.

&#45;-

**zuko's POV**

the ferris wheel did not stop.

cause we're not in a fucking rom-com.

## Chapter 24

**zuko's POV**

in a few days me and sokka will have to go back to school. i'm not even sure how i'm supposed to act after what happened almost two weeks ago. everyone will probably look at me like i'm an insane freak that could go crazy and randomly blast fire at people and i can't do anything about it.

also, i'm worried about what sokka will do when he sees his teammates for the first time since they beat the shit out of me. we talked about this matter and i tried to convince him that acting reckless and starting a fight with them wasn't the answer.

this is our last year of highschool and it's almost over, so getting expelled before having the chance to take his final exams would only be foolish. he protested, like a lot (he is really mad and wants to get revenge), but he gave in at last and admitted that i am right about this, even if he didn't like it.

anyways, i don't have to deal with this crap for a few more days, so i won't stress myself too much. i'll just enjoy the company of the gorgeous boy with mesmerising ocean eyes that is sitting in front of me right now.

"so, what do you think?" i ask sokka with hopeful eyes.

it's my first time cooking spaghetti ever and he's unlucky enough to be the one that tastes it, so i'm sitting across from him, with my chin in my palms, waiting for the verdict.

he fills his mouth with my food and after he chews on it a little, his face loses any expression.

"mh- it's- it's good, zuko" he speaks with his mouth full, and after a few seconds he swallows.

"you're lying" i mutter and i grab a fork in frustration.

i twist some spaghetti around it and as soon as it stays 2 seconds in my mouth, i find a napkin and spit it all out.

"spirits, it's salty as fuck!" i shout dissapointed. "how did you eat that?"

sokka rubs the back of his neck while still staring at his plate, but then he locks eyes with me.

"i know how excited you were about cooking dinner for me and how much you wanted it to turn out good..."

i bury my face in my palms and rub my temples, until i hear his fork making contact with the plate again.

"what are you doing? don't eat that crap" i tell him, pulling his plate towards me.

"you made it, so it's good enough for me, give it back!" he whines.

"you'll get sick from this much salt, let's just order something that is less likely to kill us" i suggest.

sokka sighs and chuckles, than grabs his phone.

"pizza?" he asks, raising an eyebrow at me.

"sure, anything is better than salty spaghetti"

i throw away all the shitty food and i can't help but pout. i really suck at cooking, no matter how many times i try.

"hey" sokka wraps his arms around my waist from behind and rests his chin on my shoulder. "don't be discouraged, i don't mind cooking for you for the rest of our lives"

*for the rest of our lives.*

the mere thought of waking up next to sokka every single morning until the day i die makes my heart backflip repeatedly and the fact that he said it as it's the most natural thing in the world-

"fine, i actually don't mind having a personal chef" i chuckle and turn my head over my shoulder so our lips can meet.

**⚠**️warning, it gets spicy**⚠**️

he places his hands on my hips and rotates my body so i can face him. he then moves his hands on the back of my thighs, lifting me up and making me sit on the kitchen counter. he clears it with one hand and some things fall on the floor, but none of us pays attention to any of them.

i wrap my legs around him, pulling his body closer to mine without breaking our kiss. i reach for his hair tie in order to let go of his warrior tail and i bury one of my hands in his soft hair, while cupping his jaw with the other.

he slides his palm under my t-shirt and starts drawing circles all over it with his cold fingers, sending electrifying shivers down my spine.

"is this ok?" he asks me.

"mhm- yes, but- won't katara come home soon?" i groan, parting our lips just for a second.

"she's staying over at toph's tonight" sokka says breifly and then he goes back to hungrily tasting my lips. "bedroom?"

i only have to nod two times for him to get the message and lift me from the counter and carry me upstairs (how can he handle so much weight without his knees giving in?). as if separating out lips would lead us to our deaths, we somehow manage to get to the bedroom without breaking contact.

he shuts the door behind us, then gently throws me on his bed, and makes his way on top of me, while placing pecks on my neck and collarbone. my hands reach for the hem of his baby blue tank top and i withdraw from the kiss only to question with my eyes if he's comfortable with this.

he answers by pulling his tank top over his head in less than two seconds and throwing it somewhere across the room. i let my hands wander all over his body, taking in the shape of every muscle that his caramel skin is covering so flawlessly.

"can i-" sokka mumbles after a few moments, with his hands on the edge of my t-shirt.

i nod and he carefully helps me undress, my t-shirt meeting the same fate as his. sokka departs from me, staring down at me with the most caring and passionate look i've ever seen on anyone before. i instinctively try to cover my bare skin, tainted with countless scars that i carry around all the time.

"zuko..." sokka begins. "you're so beautiful that it literally makes me want to cry. you don't have to hide any part of yourself from me"

it's like he's stepping on my heart with flowers and bubbles, making it ache, but in the best way possible. i slowly retreat my hands from my body, to let him see me entirely and the right corner of his mouth lifts a little, forming a sad smile.

he goes down to my abdomen and presses his soft lips on an inch long white scar in the shape of a thin line. i slightly arch my back under his touch without even realising, as his warm breath makes contact with my skin, setting it completely on fire.

sokka then proceeds to kiss every scar that i have, one by one, finding them on my ribs, chest and arms. his lips linger on each one of them for a few seconds and i let out a little moan when he reaches one that's right under my collarbone.

i feel completely overwhelmed as he begins climbing up my neck and i start breathing heavily, like i'm certain i'm going to explode any second now. his hands somehow end up on my belt, but i soon grab his wrists and he lifts his eyes in order to meet mine.

"i can't, sokka" i desperately gasp for air.

he lets go of my belt and gets off me, but he stays really close to me, placing one hand on my face.

**⚠**️end of warinig**⚠**️

"i'm so *sorry*" i squeack and i cover my eyes with my palms when i feel my eyes stinging.

"zuko, why are you crying?" sokka asks and i can sense the concern in his voice. "what's wrong?"

he sits up and slightly pulls me up so my head leans on his chest while his fingers gently run through my hair.

"i'm sorry i can't do this- it's just- it's more than i can handle at the moment" i try to explain myself.

"oh, baby, you have nothing to apologise for" he lifts my chin to urge me to look him right in the eyes. "remember that you *never* have to do anything if you're not ready for. *i'*m sorry if i did anything that made you feel pressured to do this"

i shake my head firmly and i sniffle while looking up at him.

"you did everything *perfectly*" i sigh. i'm the one that's messed up and gets overwhelmed by everything to the point where i feel like i'll break down"

i press my face against his chest and he tightly wraps his arms around me and kisses my head gently.

"zuko, there is absolutely nothing wrong with you. there's no pattern that we have to follow with this, and there's no reason for us to rush anything, we have our whole lives ahead of us"

i lift my head to stare at him and his understanding smile is cutting my heart into pieces. every time i look at him i get blown away by his beauty, which right now is highlighted by his hair falling on both sides of his face.

"i love you *so* much, zuko" he adds. "we don't even have to kiss tonight, we can just cuddle... if that's ok with you, of course"

"that- that would be perfect" i nod and we shift our positions so that we are both laying down.

he hugs me from behind, sinking his face in my hair and feeling his bare skin against mine gives me a somehow comforting sensation and i never want him to let me go.

&#45;- (**the next day)**

**sokka's POV**

i wake up way before zuko, which is unusual for us, but as much as i hate leaving this warm bed, i have to. i get up, grab the first clothes that i see and then i write zuko a note on a random piece of paper.

*'good morning, sleepy head. i'm going to the grocery store. don't wait for me. good luck at work!'*

i leave the note on the nightstand and head out. i know he starts work early, so i have until noon to do something that i've been thinking about for a while now. oh, i'm not actually going to the grocery store, and i hate lying to zuko, but it's for a good cause!

i hop in the car and after around 15 minutes, i reach my destination. a nice lady greets me at the gate of the premises and leads me to a fenced area, where a bunch of dogs are running around together.

"take your time and after you've decided, let me know which one you want" the lady tells me and i nod in understanding.

"i will, thank you" i reply.

i then shift my attention to the puppies playing together in the grass. if it was up to me, i would adopt every single one of them and offer them a loving home, but that is impossible.

i analyse each of them, but it's just so hard for me to choose only one, knowing that the rest of them won't get the affection they so desperately crave. i suddenly notice a tiny paw shily stepping out of one of the dog houses, and a puppy appears.

he is all black, except for his paws, which are white, making it look like he is wearing some cute little shoes, but that's not what catches my attention. when it's golden eyes meet mine, i know that he's the one, because they instantly remind me of zuko. they have the same innocent, but at the same time burdened glare.

&#45;-

"sokka, i can't believe you got a puppy!" katara exclaims as soon as she comes home from school. "aw, he's so tiny!"

she squeacks and takes him from my hands, petting him and talking to him with a slightly annoying voice.

"do you think zuko will like this surprise?" i ask her while gesturing at all the dog stuff that i bought.

"he will LOVE it" she assures me, then shifts her attention back to the puppy.

he is still kind of shy, but the lady at the shelter assured me that he will be more playful once he gets comfortable around us.

"um- 'tara... do you think you could help me with something?" i question.

"sure, what is it?"

&#45;-

when i hear the door unlocking, i jump from the couch and rush to meet zuko at the entrance, with my heart stuck in my throat.

"hey" i say quickly. "i have a surprise for you"

zuko takes off his shoes and puts his jacket in the hanger.

"what surprise?" he wonders curiously.

i grab his hand with mine and drag him to the living room, where our new puppy is peacefully sleeping in his fluffy bed. zuko stops a few feet away from him, with his lips parted and his eyes widened. he looks at me, then points at the dog, then back at me.

"you- you got a- you got a puppy?" his voice cracks.

he brushes his hair with his fingers as if he can't tell if it's actually real or not.

"do you like it?" i ask.

he walks to the puppy and gets on his knees, lifting the small being in his hands, then hugs it to his chest. after a minute he puts it back in it's bed and rushes over to me so fast that when he wraps his arms around me, i almost fall from the impact.

"this is the best thing ever" he says and i squeeze him back, burying my fingers in his shaggy hair.

"i'm so happy you like it" i say and pull away from him. "oh, i forgot, i also made you something"

i run out of the room and when i come back, i keep my hands behind me.

"close your eyes" i tell him and he obeys.

i then take the crown made out of pink and yellow flowers that katara helped me put together and place it on his head. i take his hand and lead him to the mirror in the hallway.

"you can open your eyes" i announce him.

his lips start forming a genuine smile and that is everything i want to see from him.

"i remembered that your old flower crown got destroyed by those assholes on the street from... that night" i explain. "so i asked katara to teach me how to do one myself. it's a little crushed here and there, but this is the best my sausage fingers could do"

i scratch the back of my neck and zuko turns around and pulls me in a passionate kiss that says more than a million words.

## Chapter 25

**zuko's POV**

as i open the heavy doors of the highschool for the first time in 2 weeks, i can already feel anxiety running through my veins. my entire body stiffens under the stares of all the students wandering around in the hallways.

i think sokka notices how tense i am, because he slides his hand into mine and interwines our fingers together, squeezing lightly. i instantly ease up, knowing that i can face basically anything head on, as long as he's by my side.

spirits, i feel like i'm an outcast, and by associating himself with me, sokka is too, because everybody steps out of our way like i'm going to set them on fire if they come near me, which is fucking stupid.

"it's ok, baby, just ignore everybody, alright?" sokka says to me and i nod, because the lump in my throat doesn't allow me to form any words.

we stop by my locker so i can get some books that i need, and when i close it, i see suki staring at us from a considerable distance. i'm sure sokka spotted her too, because he stopped talking in the middle of the sentence.

"sokka, don't-" i press my hand against his chest, but it's too late, because he's already walking towards her.

i have no other option than to follow him. when suki sees us getting close to her, she turns around in attempt to walk away, but sokka grabs her by the arm before she can get lost in the crowd.

"uhm- hey... sokka" she mumbles, scratching her forearm. "how's your leg?"

sokka ignores her question completely and gets straight to the point.

"did you ask the basketball team to beat zuko up? and do not lie, because i'm this close" - he draws his index near his thumb, leaving an extremely small space between them - "to snapping right now"

the girl presses her back against the nearest locker and covers her face with her palms, then she... starts sobbing?

"i'm so sorry-" she sniffles. "i had no idea they were gonna go so far with it... i thought they only wanted to scare him off a bit, i swear!"

her eyes are filled with tears and her voice is cracking. she seems pretty honest to me, and sokka must see it too, because he sighs deeply and lightens up a bit.

"ok, ok stop crying please, i believe you" he pats her shoulder awkwardly.

she wipes her nose and eyes with the back of her palm, then she turns to face me, fixing me with her huge eyes.

"please forgive me, zuko! both of you actually..." she looks at sokka as well. "i was *such* an asshole this whole time, but- but i was hurting... and i know that's not an excuse for my behaviour. however, i hope you can both forgive me one day"

my heart stings, as if i am transferring her pain to myself and i realise i never really stopped to consider how she was feeling. it must have sucked really bad to drift apart from her boyfriend so suddenly and see him go on with his life with... a boy that came randomly in their life.

"of course, suki, you're already forgiven. and i'm also sorry that i wasn't considerate of your feelings at all" i say to her.

she seems surprised by how quickly i accepted her apology, and so does sokka, but he soon nods in agreement with me.

"for real?" she smiles, unable to believe my words. "wow, i'm so relieved! so... friends?"

she shrugs and extends both of her fists, encouraging me and sokka to bump ours into hers, which we do, and then we all chuckle together.

"great... so- i'll see you around i guess" she says and is about to leave when sokka calls her name.

"suki, wait-" he says and she raises her eyebrows curiously. "do you think you can tell the principal how things actually happened in the boys' locker room?"

i didn't even think about this. that douchebag didn't believe me, because i was alone against the word of a whole team, but if suki were to tell him the truth...

"i mean- i'm not sure he will believe me, but i'll definetely try. it's the least i can do" she tucks her hair behind her ear.

a sparkle of hope appears in my heart. maybe those homophobic assholes will finally get what they deserve.

&#45;-**(time skip - after school)**

today is also the first day of work for sokka since he sprained his ankle and he is currently taking an order from some girl, while i'm wiping a nearby table.

"okaay, one blueberry tea coming right up" i overhear him saying with his usual upbeat attitude.

"wait-" the girl stops him from heading to the kitchen. "you're really cute, do you have instagram or something?"

spirits, i can't believe she's hitting on him! i mean, i *can* believe, because he's absolutely gorgeous, but it doesn't make me feel less sick to my stomach.

"ugh- i do, but you should know that i'm taken" sokka lets her know.

"by who? i don't see her here" the girl pretends to look around, searching for sokka's *'girlfriend'.*

how some people have the nerve to say something like that to a stranger is beyond my comprehension... i can barely say hello to a person i don't know. i finish wiping the table and head to where sokka is, putting an arm around his waist, making him chuckle softly.

"huh, here *she* is" sokka gives me half a smile and raises an eyebrow.

the girl is obviously confused, as her brows are almost meeting at this point. she looks at sokka, then at me, and back at sokka.

"wh- what?" she mutters.

i roll my eyes and slightly lift the corner of my mouth.

"as he said, he's taken... by me" i clarify and then fully smile at her.

her facial expression shifts from surprise to disgust in less than two seconds, so i hug sokka's waist tighter and place a peck on his cheek.

"you- you must be joking, right?" she puffs.

i live for making homophobes uncomfortable. (it really should be considered an olympic sport)

"if you have a problem with it, i kindly invite you to leave" i say nonchalantly.

i can tell sokka is struggling to repress his laughter, and the nosy girl surely wasn't expecting that.

"ugh, i was about to go anyway, i don't need to see *this* while i drink my tea" she gestures with her hand in our direction.

she grabs her purse and leaves her seat, heading for the exit with loud, quick steps.

"bye bye" i flutter my fingers and grin when she turns her head around, just to take another disgusted look at us.

once she is out of our sight, i cluch on sokka's forearm and lead him to the room behind the counter, where we both start giggling.

"why is literally everyone hitting on us all the time? it's exhausting" i whine.

sokka rests his arms around my waist and presses his lips against mine, while casting my hair away from my face with his warm fingers.

"it's 'cause we're hot as fuck, why else do you think?" he smirks playfully.

"huh, we are, aren't we?" i chuckle.

he crashes his lips into mine once again, and it takes a while to take notice of the door that has just opened.

"ugh, thanks a lot for reminding me of how single i am" jin pouts and grabs some herbs from a glass jar sitting on a shelf.

she soon loses the serious face and we all start laughing lightly.

"sorry, jin" i scratch my nape awkwardly.

"eh, don't worry about it, you're cute as hell together, so i'll let it slide this time" she says jokingly and exists the room.

i can only assume she is going to prepare some tea for the customers, which reminds me that we should probably get back to our jobs.

&#45;-

**sokka's POV**

i forgot how tiring it is to work and i kind of miss *not* having to stand up and serve tea for so many hours, but the fact that zuko was there the whole time made it a lot more bearable.

"i can't wait to hit the sack" i yawn and rub my eyes.

we're standing on zuko's porch and i'm waiting for him to unlock the door. once he does, we are greeted by a tiny pile of fur that is running around in circles and attacking our legs with it's impatient paws.

we brought our puppy (who we named appa) to zuko's house so his uncle could keep him company while we're at school, but iroh is at work right now, so appa has been waiting for us by himself.

"who's the sweetest boy in the world? who is? you are! yes, you are" zuko talks to the puppy in a high pitched voice.

the dog starts waving his tail back and forth, occasionally jumping on zuko, who is now in the living room, on his knees, playing with him on the carpet.

appa lays on his back, urging zuko to pat his cute little belly and of course, zuko obeys immediately, talking gibberish without stoping. i giggle when i see zuko literally rolling on the floor alongside appa, and i can't help but join them, so we both start acting like 5 year olds.

at some point, zuko is laying with his back against the floor, holding appa up in his hands, so i take advantage of the situation and slide myself on top of him so that his arms are framing me. he clearly didn't expect that, because he freezes for a second, but then he puts appa down and pulls me by my collar into a kiss.

i can feel the puppy scratchig my shirt, searching for our attention, but zuko pushes him aside gently and i'm way too absorbed by his lips to let him go. after a few moments, appa gives up and curls up in his bed, shooting us angry looks because we stopped playing with him.

i break the kiss and support my own weight by placing my elbows on each side of zuko's head.

"if we ignore our puppy for this, i wonder how we'll act when we have kids" i chuckle, but i soon realise what i've just said.

i know for a fact that zuko is as shocked as i am, because he fixes me with his sparkly amber eyes and his lips are slightly parted, as if he is about to say something.

"i-" i begin an attempt at making an excuse, but he quickly flips me over, killing any thought that was forming in my mind.

now i'm the one pressed against the floor and zuko is only halfway on top of me, crossing my body with his left leg and his palms pressed next to both of me ears.

"you wanna have kids with me, huh?" he smirks, uncovering his teeth.

the way his messy hair is falling and almost touching my face, makes my mind go completely blank. *i'm so in love with him.*

"i- i mean-" i mutter, trying to get rid of the heart that's stuck in my throat. "i mean, we can't really have our own kids, but we can- like- adopt? or something...? but not right now, obviously, we're still in highschool, so we ca-"

he stops me by closing the distance between our lips and i bury my hand in his hair, pulling him even closer.

"you talk too fast when you're nervous" he whispers in my ear, sending shivers down my spine.

yeah, he's right, i really tend to do that.

"i love that you're thinking about us having kids one day" zuko smiles lightly, while moving his fingers all around my chest.

it's like a heavy weight was lifted off my chest. i thought he'd think i'm insane for thinking about children right now, but the truth is, i could easily spend every day next to him, for the rest of my life.

"i'm relieved, i thought you'd freak out... i didn't even think before i said that" i explain.

he traces a fine line with his index from my right temple, to my cheek and jaw, analysing every detail of my face, then he gives me a peck on the nose.

"it's even better that you said it involuntarily. that means it came from here" he pokes my chest in the place where my heart is positioned.

i'm about to reply, but our attention is distracted by the ringing of zuko's phone. he gets up with a frustrated groan, grabs it from the couch, then comes back on the floor next to me. he frowns and shows me that the number calling him is not one that he has registered in his list.

"hello?" he answers the call confused.

after 2 seconds, he sits up abruptly and if he is extremely pale on a daily basis, now all of the colour in his face has faded away completely. he gulps and his hand starts shaking while clutching on the phone until his knuckles turn white. after a few seconds of silence, he finally lets out one word.

"*father*?"

## Chapter 26

**zuko's POV**

even if it's already been a few hours since the conversation i had with my father, it's still the only thing in my mind and i can't sleep. it's the middle of the night, sokka is hugging me tightly from behind and i'm pretty sure he's asleep, because i've been staring into space for what feels like an eternity.

"you still can't fall asleep, can you?" sokka's raspy voice startles me, breaking my train of thought.

he starts drawing circles on my arm with his index and i turn around so i can face him.

"why does he want to see me, sokka?" i ask as if he's supposed to know how to respond.

i can tell that he wishes he had an answer for me, but he doesn't. his fingers are brushing my hair delicately, spreading it all around the white pillow, and his eyes are fixing me with both concern and care.

"i don't know, baby, but i really don't think you should go" he says. "not after everything he's done to you"

i sigh and i press my palms against my eyes. uncle iroh told me the same thing, but he also said that he supports me regardless of the decision i make.

"what if he regrets it?" i wonder. "what if he can finally accept and love me?"

yes, i'm aware of how stupid i sound for saying that, but the child in me still longs for the fatherly love that he never offered me.

"i find that hard to believe" sokka mutters. "a monster that would burn his own child doesn't seem capable of love"

i know sokka means well, but his mention of that incident makes my heart sting. i reach for my scar, letting myself feel the wrinkly skin and remember the excruciating pain that *he* inflicted on me.

i don't feel the tear crossing my cheek, until sokka's hand wipes it gently. he leans over and starts kissing all over the left side of my face, tickiling me with his soft lips.

"how are you not grossed out by my scar?" i genuinely ask. "it took me *months* to even look at it without breaking down, and every time i was touching it, i would get shivers and i'd start crying"

he stares at my face for a few seconds, like he wants to memorise every insignificant detail about it, then he finally answers.

"no part of you could possibly gross me out, dummy" he states and chuckles softly. "i love *all* of you. no exceptions"

i try to hide my flushed cheeks by burying my face in his shoulder, but he knows me too well, so he pulls me in his arms. i place my head on his chest and hug his waist, while he also wraps his arms strongly around my body.

we sit in silence for a while, my head filled with heavy thoughts, and i don't know if sokka fell asleep or not, but i start talking anyway.

"i think it's weird that he called me exactly today" i mutter.

"how so?" sokka asks in a low voice, with his eyes still closed.

the way he twirls some locks of my hair around his fingers is oddly comforting.

"because-" i pause for a few seconds. "tomorrow marks 10 years since my mother's death"

i still find it hard to mention her, even if it's been so long since i've seen her last, but it's not like i was an infant when she died. i was eight, so i still have some memories of her.

"do you think it's a coincidence? or maybe he has some hidden motives for reaching out to you?" sokka suggests.

"i don't even know what to believe anymore" i whisper surrendered. "but i assume that the only way to find out is by actually meeting him tomorrow"

i feel sokka's grip tightening on me, as if he's trying to protect me from something that's not even here.

"it's up to you" he mumbles. "but just so you know, i don't like this at all"

"i know, i know, but- i mean, you can come with me if you want" i propose.

he reaches and kisses my forehead, then he tucks some hair behind my ear.

"of course i'll come" he says as if it's only natural. "i'm gonna make sure that he won't hurt you ever again"

&#45;- **(time skip - the next day)**

**sokka's POV**

i offered to give zuko a ride to the cemetery, and he was thankful for it, even if he's been feeling down since this morning. i know damn well how it is to lose your mother and it's absolutely heartbreaking.

we're walking through the graveyard in silence, both of us lost in our thoughts, with our hands in our pockets. i don't try to hold his hand, or make him laugh, partly because i myself feel like shit, but mostly because i know he needs his time to grieve.

i know my mother died too, but it's different for him. i had time to make peace with the idea that she would be gone, because she was sick for a long time before eventually passing away. i sometimes think it was better this way for her, as she didn't have to suffer and struggle anymore, even if that left me, katara and our dad with our hearts wrecked, but at least we had eachother.

zuko, on the other hand... wasn't expecting it, and he couldn't find support from his horrendous father. his mom was the only one that offered him love, his sole pillar in the abusive household he grew up in.

even worse, he was the one to find her. he didn't give me a lot of details, but he didn't have to, because it's obviously a traumatic experience. after that, he had nobody but his uncle, who he rarely met, to share his feelings with, so he bottled it up, because he was taught that emotions are a sign of weakness since he was little.

we arrive at his mother's grave and he carefully puts down the flowers that he bought for her, but i can tell that his hands are shaking badly, even if he's trying to hide it. he sits down on his knees and stares deeply at the tombstone with her name on it: ursa.

"mom-" zuko starts talking to the grave, but then he pauses.

he looks down at his hands and sighs, his dark hair falling on his face.

"i can give you a moment alone, if you want" i offer after seeing his hesitation.

maybe he's uncomfortable because i'm here, so i get ready to go take a walk.

"you can stay, sokka"

i nod, even if he's currently looking at the tombstone, not at me, twiddling his thumbs nervously.

"wow, this is gonna be cringe" he chuckles, but nothing in his voice gives off hints of happiness.

i sit down next to him, also on my knees and put an arm around his shoulders, patting his back. i take his trembling hand in mine and he squeezes it firmly.

"it's ok to let your emotions out" i tell him.

he sighs deeply before begining to talk again.

"i miss you, mom- *every single day*" he emphasises each word. "i really wish you were here so you could see that i finally found my happiness, even if it used to seem an impossible thing to achieve"

his voice breaks and he looks at me with watery eyes, before leaning his forehead on my shoulder. i place an affectionate peck on his head and then he wipes his cheek with his palm.

"he would have loved you. i'm sure of it" he adresses me this time, rubbing my palm with his thumb.

he then goes on sharing some memories he has with her, happy ones, and i can already tell what an amazing person she was.

he tells me about how, one time, when ursa was bringing him home from kindergarten, they spotted a bird with a broken wing on the sidewalk and she immediately took it with them. they cared for her (behind his father's back, because he would have killed it on the spot), until the bird was able to fly again and they released it.

another time, she saw zuko trying on her lipstick and her high heels. he was petrified when she caught him in the act, knowing that his father was so strict about him being manly, but all she did was smile. then, she wiped his lipstick off, because he was so clumsy with it that it had even reached his cheek, so she helped him do it right, telling him how beautiful he was.

most times, she would go against his father's word, only to please zuko, even if that meant she would face dire concequences.

"why did she leave me, sokka?" zuko squeacks once he finishes narrating. "why did she leave me with *him*?"

he starts sobbing quietly, so i pull him closer in my arms, brushing his hair gently and he clenches his fists around my shirt.

"why was i the one to find her? why did i have to see her... *like that*?" he wonders. "it was horrible, sokka! her neck was twisted in a weird position and- and her eyes were staring right through me, because they were *so* emotionless, and she wouldn't move, no matter how hard i shook her body"

the pure pain in his voice sends electric shocks through my whole body and i can't imagine what an 8 year old child felt while finding his own mother's motionless corpse.

"she loved you, zuko, you have to remind yourself that" i try encouraging him, but i am aware that there aren't any words that could make him feel better.

"if she loved me so much, why did she leave me with that *monster*?"

his words make my veins feel like they're full of ice. i don't have an answer for him, but i wish i did. i just want to take away his pain and make it my own, but it's not possible, so i can only make sure i'm permanently by his side, helping in any way i possibly can.

we sit there, on the ground, until my legs go numb, but at least zuko stopped crying, so that is the most important thing. we get up and he starts walking away, but before i follow him, i press my hand against his mother's tombstone while he isn't watching.

"thank you for bringing him to this world" i whisper when i'm sure he can't hear me. "i promise i'll take care of him for you"

&#45;- **(later that day)**

**zuko's POV**

this day has been emotionally exhausting so far, and i have no idea what i'm doing to be honest. sokka stops the car in front of my father's mansion and we get out of it.

"spirits, you never told me your father is stupid rich" sokka says staring at the enormous house.

we begin walking throught the gate that his butler opens up for us and i can't believe he needs people to do this for him.

"sir ozai is currenly attending an important meeting, but he will join you in a short amount of time" the man lets us know. "until then, please enter the house and head to the main room-"

"i know where to go, i used to fucking live here, alfred" i snap at him.

*ugh*, why was i so rude? i know he's just doing his job, but i feel like i'm walking on glass and i might break at any moment.

me and sokka reach the front door, and once we step inside, i can already feel my intestines tying themselves into knots. the amount of memories - and trauma - that i accumulated in this place is astronomical.

my legs stop working immediately and i suddenly feel something touching my shoulder, which makes me flinch really bad. it turns out it was just sokka's hand, which he withdraws quickly after my reaction, and stares at me with concern imprinted in his icy blue eyes.

"it's not too late to turn around and leave" he reminds me.

i shake my head, trying to get rid of the anxiety that has taken control over me.

"no" i state. "i have to do this"

i then take his hand in mine and lead him to the main room, which consists of a stupidly long table, surrounded by silk covered, red chairs. i gulp when i lay my eyes on the fireplace on the other side of the room and i instinctively reach out to my scar.

"is that-" sokka gestures to the flames that are dancing around in the fireplace.

he's very observant and i'm amazed of how easily he can read my emotions and thoughts.

"yeah" i mumble, playing with the edge of my shirt.

he seems really pissed and ready to fight anyone that comes near me, and i wonder what i would do without him.

i take a seat at the head of the table, and sokka occupies the spot to my right, holding my hand tightly. my foot won't stop tappin nervously against the floor and i think my heart is going to explode while i'm waiting for time to pass.

after some time, the large doors of the room open and a very familiar silhouette steps in.

"hello, zuko" my father says with a grin on his face that i wish i could wipe right now.

## Chapter 27

**zuko's POV**

"father" i say as he takes a seat at the opposite side of the table.

i instinctively reach for sokka's hand under the table, in search for emotional support. i hate to admit it, but i'm still reluctant whenever i see him, and i try to keep my shaky hands under control as much as i can.

i think it's pretty pathetic, but his presence makes me go back in time, to when i was just a scared little puppy, obediant to his agressive owner. i like to tell myself that this isn't the case anymore, but i can't help but be a little shaken by his image before my eyes.

"i see you brought a *friend* with you" he points out, putting both of his elbows on the table and bringing his hands together.

"he's not a friend" i cut him off. "he's my *boyfriend"*

i feel sokka stiffening next to me as soon as i correct him, waiting for any bad reaction that my father might have, and getting prepared for it. however, all that my dad does is grimace for less than a second, and for someone that doesn't know this man, it may have been indistinguishable, but i know him too well to be tricked by his sly attempt at seeming ok with me being gay.

"i see..." he mutters, trying not to show the disgust boiling inside of him. "so it was *not* just a phase"

*of course it was not just a fucking phase, you bastard, that's not how this works.*

"why did you invite me here?" i get straight to the point, ignoring his offensive remark.

i want to get out of this house as fast as i can, so there's no need to respect any formalities, as if he deserved even an ounce of respect from me, after all he's done.

"am i not allowed to miss my only son and wish to see him?" he asks, pretending to have his feelings hurt by my blunt question.

he's so phony, i can't believe he's for real. *what the hell is he after?*

"a normal father would" i reply. "but we both know you don't fall into that category" i stare right into his eyes.

he shuts his eyes and inhales. he looks like he's barely holding together his anger, repressing his urge to close the distance between us and beat the shit out of me. *just like the good old days.*

"i am your father, whether you like it or not, and you can't change that" he says. "besides, what did i do so severe to deserve this treatment from my own son?"

i can tell that sokka's been trying to keep his mouth shut this whole time, but he finally bursts, practically jumping to his feet and slamming his clenched fists against the wooden table.

"you burned his *fucking face*, destroyed his entire childhood by abusing him, and kicked him out of the house, and you have the fucking guts to request his respect? well, fuck you!" sokka spits out and then sits back in his chair angrily.

"who are you again?" my father furrows his brows, staring at sokka as if he was only a peasant compared to him.

i think sokka is literally about to jump at his throat, but i grab his arm, forcing him to stay put.

"it's ok, baby, i can handle it" i assure him and try to put on a fake half-smile that of course doesn't fool him.

my father finds it even more difficult to gather up enough strenght to hide his disgust when he hears me calling sokka baby, and it's more than obvious that he's putting on an act.

"just tell me what you want, so we can all go back to living our lives and forget this ever happened" i say.

"fine" he sighs, slightly annoyed. "a guest will join us shortly and explain to you something that was brought to my attention only a few days ago"

what is he talking about? what guest? what is going on, why can't *he* tell me?

after what seemed like the longest few minutes of my life, a tiny man, with slicked-back hair, wearing some small round glasses and a weird moustache enters the room.

*who the hell is this midget?*

he presents himself (his name is gerard), the place he works and he doesn't catch my attention with his blabbering until he mentions my mother's name.

"you must be zuko" he glances at me and smiles softly. "your mother used to talk about you all the time"

all the blood in my body has dropped to my feet and i can't seem to be able to move any muscle. how did he know my mother?

"i believe you are pretty confused right now, so i will get to the point" he chuckles. "ever since you were little, your mother started putting money aside into a bank account, just for you. she saved up a considerable amount from different businesses that she ran, while some of them still making profit and transferring money into your account"

*wh- what?*

"that bitch" my father spits. "going behind my back like that..."

gerard ignores him, but from his words, i can only assume he wasn't aware of her businesses and of the amount of money she was able to make without his consent.

"she gave me strict instructions before- passing away" he continues. "that i should reveal this information only after 10 years have gone by, because now you are old enough to understand these matters and make your own decisions. she didn't want your father to influence you in any way"

so *this* is why he wanted to see me and acted all nice, like he didn't scar me emotionally and physically for life. he wants to dig his dirty claws into the legacy my mom left me by trying to make me forgive him.

"also, she left you a house on Ember Island, that you have complete ownership of, as well as a mansion in Yu Dao" the tiny man adds.
