Yu Dao is a big city, a few hours away... how is this happening? this doesn't seem real. my mom, the scared, fragile woman that bowed her head before the monster she somehow married... she was actually a strong ass business woman that made bank behind my father's back?

"we will have to deal with a lot of paperwork, but i will make sure to guide you throught the whole process" gerard smiles at me.

"i- i don't even know what to say-" i mumble, completely shocked and unable to form any coherent thought.

the man giggles slightly, arraging his moustache with his fingers. he then starts taking out some papers from the black breifcase that he brought with him.

after an eternity of signing and listening carefully to his explanations, he hands me a credit card and his phone number, in case i ever have any questions, then packs up all of his stuff, getting ready to leave.

"oh, silly me, i almost forgot" he slaps his own forehead and turns back around. "your mom also asked me to give you this"

he puts his hand in the pocket inside of his coat and brings out an envelope, that he places in front of me.

"it was a real pleasure meeting ursa's son" he shakes my hand with a warm grin on his face, he says goodbye to everyone, and then heads out.

&#45;-**(a few hours later)**

i've been standing on my bed, with my legs crossed and the envelope on my lap for over an hour, but i still can't bring myself to open it. my head is buzzing with thoughts and all i can do is stare into nothing, with my heart stuck in my throat.

uncle iroh was blown away by what i found out today as well, so he had no idea what my mom had planned for me. she's taking care of me even after she's gone-

"hey, hey, hey, why are you crying?" sokka wraps his welcoming arms around me and pulls me closer to him.

he wipes some stray tears that are falling down on my cheeks with his thumb and caresses my hair affectionately. i put the envelope away, on the nightstand, and then sit on his lap, with my legs around his waist.

i feel like a little baby, so vulnerable in his arms, but i desperately need affection, so i let myself melt into his embrace, and he doesn't seem to be bothered. i bury my head in the crook of his shoulder and take in his familiar and comforting scent, that makes me feel like home every time i sense it.

i'm not crying, but i squeeze him as thight as i can, because he never seems to be close enough, even though we're basically glued together. having him here makes me clear my head and put my thoughts in order, so we sit like this for a while.

"i think i'm ready to open it" i mutter in his shirt, and then retreat from the hug.

i get off him and grab the misterious envelope, which i start opening carefully. sokka notices how badly i'm trembling, so he places his hand on mine to stop it from shaking.

"you can do this" he encourages me, with a soft smile imprinted on his face and i nod, trying to convince myself that he's right.

i open the envelope and uncover a yellow-ish letter folded in two, but i also find a necklace in there. it has a round locket that i open only to discover a picture with my mom and i inside of it. i put it around my neck and squeeze my fist around it, swearing to never take it off again.

i take a deep breath and somehow find the strenght to unfold the letter, but as soon as i do, a tear crosses my cheek.

i recognise her handwriting immediately. i searched for weeks through any paper of hers i could find, hoping that she had left me a note... explaining why she did it. i was dissappointed when i didn't find anything, but i guess this is it.

i can't even hold the letter still, because of the wave of emotions that has gotten over me, but i finally start reading it, and i allow sokka to peek over my shoulder, so he can see it as well.

&#45;-

*dear zuko,*

*oh, my dear boy... my baby boy, you have no idea how hard it is for me to write this right now... i* *just dropped you off to school and i have to write this before your father comes home from work.* *i'm sure you were confused about the exagerrated affection i gave you today, but this was my way* *of saying goodbye to you, my greatest pride in this world...*

*i can mentally picture you reading this, so if you are doing this right now... it means you are not a* *child anymore. you are probably angry at me, or sad, or maybe both, but i knew you wouldn't* *understand what was happening at the age 8 years old, so i talked to a childhood friend of mine* *and he helped me plan everything out. i'm talking about the man that handed you this letter,* *gerard. he was not supportive of my decision, but he fufilled my desire of giving you a better future* *in the way that i thought was best.*

*i know i wasn't able to offer you the ideal childhood. you suffered a lot because of my fear of* *leaving your father and i hope one day you will come to forgive me for that. believe it or not, your* *father wasn't always the violent person you got to know, but money and power changed him* *completely. i spent so many years hoping he would return to his old self, at least for your sake, if* *not for mine, but that did not happen* *unfortunately.*

*i truly wish that my decision, even if it seems drastic, will open his eyes and make him realise that* *what he's doing is wrong, and maybe he will change his attitude towards you.*

*you didn't deserve the violence you were forced to face... no child should ever go throught that, but* *i know that you are strong enough not to let yourself be crushed by it. you are so precious... so* *pure, despite all of the pain you encountered, and you deserve to live happily ever after.*

*oh, how i wish to see how you grew into the person you are today... i'm sure you turned into a* *handsome young man with a heart of gold, just like your pretty amber eyes.*

*always remember who you are, be proud of yourself, make your voice heard* *and fight for what you* *believe in until your last breath. don't let anyone bring you down because they might see you as* *weird, or different from them, and be unapologetic about who you are.*

*never forget that everything i've done, it was for you. just know that even if i can't be next to you* *physically, i am always watching over you from above. i am proud of you, no matter what point in* *life you've reached right now, because you could never dissapoint me. i'm sure you are the best* *version of yourself, and that you are evolving, improving and maturing every single day.*

*mommy loves you so much, zuko... and even if you couldn't say goodbye to me properly, don't* *worry, because i know you loved me too. i hope one day we will meet again, but before that, go live* *your best life with the people you love most, and maybe, just maybe... you'll keep me in a corner of* *your heart forever.*

*--*

i don't think i've ever cried so hard in my entire life, but i keep casting my tears away, to keep them from staining the letter. i feel as if someone just ripped my heart out of my chest, cut it into pieces, stepped on it repeatedly and fed it to the turtleducks. my stomach feels hollow and at the same time, i'm nauseous, my eyes are probably going to fall out of my sockets from so much sobbing, and i find it hard to breathe.

sokka takes the letter from my quivering hands and puts it away for me, because i am unable to move. he then brings me into his arms and i clutch onto his shirt like it's the only thing helping me keep my head above the water.

"it's ok, it's ok, i'm here" sokka's voice is cracking and i can tell that he's crying too.

at this point, i'm pretty sure that he's the only thing keeping me sane. i don't know what i would do without him.

## Chapter 28

**sokka's POV**

"so you have a beach house on an island just for yourself?!" toph exclaims, smashing her palms on the lunch table, and zuko nods slowly.

he filled everybody in with what happened when we met his father, and they were as shocked as we were when we found out.

"so *why* aren't we there right now?" the blind girl raises her eyebrows.

"toph, this is all new to him, too, so try to calm down" i intervene.

i know it would be hard for zuko to go to ember island, since it would constantly remind him of his mother. he told me that she grew up on that island, in that house, and that she always used to talk about taking him there one day. unfortunately, she never had the chance to do so.

"it's ok, sokka" zuko smiles lightly. "we can go there next week, since we'll have the spring break"

the whole gAang lets out noises of excitement and, don't get me wrong, going to a private beach with my friends and having a house all for ourselves is amazing. however, i can't help but consider how it would affect zuko's emotional health.

i really hope he will be alright, but if he's not, i'll make sure to be there for him every second until he feels better.

"sweet!" toph shouts.

she leans back in her seat and throws her hands behind her head, grinning from ear to ear.

"i can't wait for it!!" aang squeacks and turns to katara. "are you excited, sweetie?"

my sister giggles and her cheeks heat up as soon as she hears that word. they keep calling eachother '*sweetie*' and i think it's gross, so i don't abstain from letting them know how i feel about it, every time i hear them.

"ugh- you're giving me oogies" i open my mouth and point inside of it with my index, pretending to puke.

toph agrees with me, making zuko chuckle into his closed fist and show off his sweet dimples, with his eyes shut. he seems so innocent, that i zone out while staring deeply at him, mesmerised by his breath-taking beauty.

*spirits* *, i love him so much.*

"shut up, sokka!" katara breaks me from the spell that zuko unintentionally had me under, and she shoots lasers at me with her eyes.

she then proceedes to blabber about how she urgently needs to go shopping to find a swimsuit for when we go to the beach, but all the noise around me soon starts fading out.

i lay my head on my hand and keep staring at the way zuko is eating his lunch, occasionally joining the conversation that has ignited at our table. i have no idea what they're talking about. "why are you looking at me like that?" zuko giggles softly. "do i have something on my face?"

he touches his cheek with his hand, in search of whatever he thinks i saw on his face, but i shake my head.

"there's nothing on your face" i assure him, but he just looks at me confused.

"then why were y-" he begins saying, but i cut him off by grabbing the collar of his shirt.

with one steady move, i bring him closer to me so his lips can meet mine for a few seconds. at first, he seems surprised, but he soon returns the kiss and relaxes.

"spirits, sokka, and you were taking about me and aang!" katara yells at me after we break the kiss.

i see that zuko is all flustered and has a shy smile imprinted on his face while he plays with his food nervously. i shrug and my cheeks turn pink as well, because we don't usually kiss in front of other people. not because we are ashamed or something, but because we think that couples kissing in public are annoying.

"sorry, i couldn't help it, you're too gorgeous" i brush some of his hair off zuko's face and he nuzzles his cheek into my palm.

"i didn't mind it" he stares at me with those magnificent golden eyes of his and interwines his fingers with mine under the table.

&#45;-

once the lunch break is over, i head to my biology class, pretty upset that i had to part ways with zuko, even for an hour. i take a seat in the back of the class and after a minute, i see suki walking over to me and sitting down.

"great news" she smiles at me, slapping the desk with excitement. "i talked to the principal and he believed that what zuko did to those guys was self defense"

i can't help but grin as well, and my heart starts filling with content immediately.

"for real? he just took your word for it?" i wonder, still unable to process the good news.

suki looks away, puckering her lips and scratches the back of her head awkwardly.

"well-" she rolls her eyes dramatically. "i *might* have convinced some of the cheerleaders to back up my story and say that they witnessed the incident, so it would sound more believable"

she made people lie for us? for zuko? who she considered like, her sworn enemy?

"but, don't worry about it" she flutters her fingers in my face. "it was for a good cause and, it's not like i was lying, i just made sure the truth would come to the surface"

she shrugs and brushes her hair over her shoulders theatrically, making us both chuckle.

"damn, suki" i exclaim. "that you so much, you're the best!"

i almost jump from my seat because of how excited i am. i can't wait to let zuko know that the assholes that bullied him are about to get what they deserve.

"it's the least i can do, after the way i acted in the past" she lowers her glare to her shoes.

i pat her back gently, to let her know that it's alright, but before i can add anything, the teacher enters the class and starts teaching her lesson.

&#45;- **(time skip - after school)**

i'm chilling in zuko's bed, holding a sleepy appa in my lap and running my fingers through his smooth fur. zuko was really happy when he heard that the principal was thinking of a punishment for the basketball team, and that his name will be cleared soon.

anyway, i've been just staring at zuko, who is painting something on a large canvas for i don't know how many hours, but he won't let me see it until it's done. he's wearing a white unbuttoned shirt, that is framing his body perfectly, and all of the creases of the cloth seem to be in perfect harmony with eachother.

the milky skin on his chest and abdomen is beaming under the tender touch of the golden rays thrown by the sun that will soon reach the horizon. i will not even mention the way he tied his hair eren yeager style, which makes me forget how breathing works.

"can i see it now?" i ask impatiently, hoping that he changed his mind about making me wait.

"nope" he replies briefly.

i scoff and pretend to pout, but he doesn't even take his eyes off the painting for one second. i've gotten used to how committed he is to his work, so i don't mind.

i've watched him make art many times, and i can sincerely say that it's one of my favourite things in the world. of course, next to his contagious smile, piercing eyes, full lips, silky hair, heartwarming laugh, awkward jokes, and- well, literally anything about him.

"you're my favourite thing in the world" i hear myself saying without actually intending to give voice to my thoughts.

he stops what he's doing in order to look at me, as his paint brush is stuck between his teeth, his fingers stroking the canvas instead, and some of his hair falling out of place, on his face. this image of him makes me chuckle, and i find myself wondering once again what i did to deserve him. at what point in my life did the universe decide to make me the happiest person to ever walk this earth?

"you're being cheesy again" zuko puffs, but i can see his lips are starting to form a smile.

"i'm just being honest" i say and he gives me a side stare.

he puts the brush down, wipes the paint off his fingers, then gets up from his chair and makes his way to the bed, next to me. i let appa get away from my grip, and zuko soon takes our puppy's place, making himself comfortable in my lap, with his legs around my waist.

"is it necessary to say that you are my favourite thing, too?" he smirks and softly presses his lips onto mine. "or is it already obvious?"

i groan and put my hand on the back of his neck, so i can pull him in a deeper kiss.

"hearing you say it more often wouldn't hurt" i grin and he rolls his eyes.

"we wouldn't want your ego to reach an astronomical size, now, would we?" he kisses me shortly. i laugh, but it doesn't last, because i can't stay away from him for too long, so i make our lips meet once again.

"did you paint with your tongue?" i giggle, pulling away from the kiss. "or do you like eating paint just for fun?"

he looks at me for a few seconds with his usual puppy eyes, and then bursts into laughter.

"that reminds me" he says, wiping a tear from the corner of his eye. "i finished the painting"

my face lights up instantly, and i push zuko off me a little too excited, which costs me a few annoyed mumbles from him, but i hurry to take a look at the final result. as soon as i lay my eyes on the painting, my lips part without my consent, and all i can do is analyse it, becaude i'm speechless. it's a picture of me and him.

in the painting, i'm wearing a blue tank top, while zuko is dressed in a white shirt, with a red plaid vest over it. also, i'm reaching over to him, kissing his forehead, and a pink flower crown is resting in his beautiful hair. we're both smiling sincerely, with our eyes closed, and i can feel the warmth of his embrace, just by looking at the picture.

"ok, i change my mind" i finally find my words. "you're my second favourite thing in the world. this painting is on the first place now"

"dammit" he spits dramatically and it makes me giggle.

he gets off the bed as well and comes behind me so we can both admire the masterpiece that he created. i hug his waist from the side, laying my head on his shoulder and he places a peck on my forehead, while wrapping his arms around me.

"so i take it you like my painting?" he asks a rather rethorical question.

i look up at him, as the sun is shooting us with it's orange light, and i kiss him on the cheek.

"i love it" i state firmly.

i then shift my position so i can face him, and i bring my arms around his neck, making our forheads rest against one another.

"and i love *you*" i add as well, poking his bare chest with my index.

i let my hands explore the soft skin on his body, which results in him arching his back under my touch. i can tell he's getting goosebumps, so i place my palms on his lower back and bring him closer, into a kiss.

"i love you too" he slowly whispers under his breath directly into my ear.

ok, now i'm the one getting major goosebumps after feeling his warm breath on my skin, while saying those words. i soon feel his hands undoing my worrior tail and putting my hairtie around his wrist, without beaking the kiss we are sharing.

"haven't i told you a million times that you look hotter with your hair down?" zuko asks me when we pause for a second, in order to catch our breaths.

he pushes me towards the bed, while we start making out again. he urges me to lie down on my back and he straddles me, cupping my face between his hands.

"do you want me to keep my hair down so everyone would faint around me?" i manage to say between kisses. "also, don't you feel special that you're the only one seeing me like this?" i raise an eyebrow.

i can feel him smirking, so i can just assume that he's pleased with my answer.

"yeah, i guess you're right" he chuckles and buries his fingers in my hair.

i keep moving my hands all around his back, and after some time, i flip him over, because i can't let him live under the impression that he's the top in his relationship, can i?

i stop kissing him, just so i can stare down at him, admiring the way his silky dark hair is spread all over the pillow, and how his chest is moving up and down in the rythm of his quick breathing.

"you look so beautiful when you're under me" i tell him without breaking the eye contact.

his cheeks turn even redder than they already were, and i can almost hear his heart beating in his chest. he grabs the collar of my shirt and pulls me to him, so i can have another taste of his soft lips.

"hello, boys, i made some te-" a voice appears in the background, startling us.

we both turn our heads towards the open door, where we spot iroh, holding a trey with two cups of tea on it. i quickly get off his nephew and i am painfully aware of how this might look to him.

"i- i should have knocked, sorry" iroh chuckles awkwardly. "i guess tea can wait... have fun! um-use protection, you know the drill"

with that, he exists the room, shutting the door behind him, leaving us filled wih embarrassment. i crash onto the pillow next to zuko and we both turn our heads to look at eachother, but we can't stay serious for long, so we start laughing.

"we should lock the door next time" zuko suggests, being all flustered, even up to the tip of his ears.

i put my hand on his face and caress his cheek with my thumb in slow motions.

"yeah" i chuckle. "i guess we should"

## Chapter 29

zuko's POV (time skip - spring break)

"finally we're here!" sokka sighs and throws all the luggage he was carrying on the porch.

it took us forever to drive all the way to ember island, and we're all extremely exhausted, mostly because of the unbearable heat. it's a lot hotter than in our home town.

the good thing is, the whole gAang is here and, additionally, i encouraged toph to invite her girlfriend, june, as well. this way, besides hanging out as a group, each of us can enjoy some quality alone time with their partner, on a private beach, which is a few dozen meters away from the house.

"sparky, hand me the keys" toph says. "if i don't lay down in a soft bed right now, i'll murder someone"

i chuckle and hand her the keys, but it was a poor choice, because she starts walking in the opposite direction from where the lock actually is. june grabs her hand and turns her towards the door.

"i think it would be better if i did it" i suggest and she throws me the keys back.

"ugh, i can't see shit on this wooden porch and it's so frustrating! i can only tell where y'all are by the sound your annoying voices" toph stomps her foot against the ground angrily.

her girlfriend tries to encourage her and makes sure to let toph know that we're all there for her if she needs anything. the blind girl, of course, says that she doesn't need any help and that she can take care of herself.

i step in front of the door, introduce the key in the lock and twist it to the right, until it makes a click.

"i need to take a shower ASAP" katara pushes me aside and enters the house, followed shortly by aang.

she got pretty angry on our way here. she mumbled whenever her brother would take a wrong turn, which would result in sokka urging her to drive in his place if she wasn't pleased with how he did it. aang was there to make sure they didn't slit eachother's throats, trying to calm them down. toph and june would just share a pair of headphones, listening to music and ignoring all the arguing. meanwhile, i decided to just admire the scenery by looking out the open window and letting the wind blow in my hair.

"screw this" i hear sokka say and i turn my head when i'm about to get in the house.

he takes off the beige t-shirt that was glued to his body because of the heat, then starts untying his shoelaces. he throws away his sneakers like he doesn't give a shit about them, and then he begins running towards the sea, making the sand jump after each step he makes.

without any warning, a smile paints itself all over my face and i soon follow his lead, taking my shoes off, but i leave my shirt on. the burning sand against my bare feet feels so welcoming, and it's like i'm running on cotton candy, because there are no pebbles or seashells, it's just smooth sand.

"wait for me, you idiot!" i shout after sokka and he turns to face me while he's running.

the soft breeze is making the hairs that escaped his ponytail make a weird dance around his face, and the way his caramel skin looks like under the sunshine is just breathtaking.

when i get to where he is, sokka grabs my hand, and we just start running around like 5 year old kids, laughing like we just escaped a mental facility. he doesn't let go of my hand for even a second, and as i sense the salty air filling up my lungs, i feel freedom taking over my body.

we both start yelling excited sounds, spining around with our hands thrown in the air, but at some point, i stumble on my own feet like the clumsy idiot i am, and i fall to the ground. because our hands were still linked, sokka soon follows me, and we burst into laughter.

"h-how can y-you trip on a flat su-surface" sokka manages to say between histerical chuckles.

he rolls over on top of me and wraps his arms around my body, while i cup his face with my palms and squeeze his cheeks. i then reach and kiss his puckered up lips.

"you taste like sand" i giggle and poke the tip of his nose with my index.

he nuzzles his nose in the crook of my shoulder and i laugh, because it tickles really bad.

"oh yeah?" he replies indignated. "you're about to eat more sand, mister!"

with that, he locks me tighter in his grip and starts rolling us around in the sand, just like children do with small snowballs so they can grow in size. we somehow reach the shore by spinning on the ground, because we get hit by a wave crashig against our heated bodies.

"aagh, so cooold" i complain and sokka stands up, lifting me as well.

"this is cold? i shower with water at this temperature" sokka lifts an eyebrow.

"yeah, sure you do" i agree sarcastically.

i roll my eyes and nudge him playfully. he returns the favour, but doesn't realise that i'm not as solid as he is, and i almost fall. he catches me by the wrist just in time.

"sorry, i forgot you're a fragile twig" he teases with a smirk on his face.

"i am not!" i defend myself, trying to look pouty.

he knows i'm not actually that skinny, i have some muscles - well, not like him, but still - he just loves to taunt me. he unexpectedly throws me over his shoulder and walks into the crystal clear sea.

"no, sokka, let me go, it's too cold!" i beg him, fluttering my legs in attempt to escape his grip.

i don't succeed and, before i know it, i find myself soaking wet, with my clothes fully drenched and my hair stuck to my face. i try to keep a straight expression, but as soon as i put the locks of hair away from my face and meet sokka's joyful eyes, i can't hold my smile.

"asshole" i roll my eyes again and push my palms against his chest, making him fall under water.

i chuckle, but he grabs my forearm and pulls me down with him. i barely manage to shut my eyes in time, but i feel sokka's hand reaching the back of my neck and crashes his lips against mine.

it feels weird to kiss someone under water, it's salty, slightly sandy and really wet, obviously. also, it's like my hair has a life of its own, because it keeps floating everywhere. we soon jump to the surface, coughing up water and gasping for air.

"well, that was kinda hot" sokka concludes, brushing my wet hair back and kissing me once again.

i don't disagree.

&#45;-

sokka's POV (a few hours later)

after zuko and i got out of the water, we went straight inside the house to take showers (separately unfortunately) and get some dry clothes. everyone else seemed to have settled in, and they chose the rooms in which they would be staying.

me and zuko decided to take one of the big bedrooms upstairs, while the other couples are staying on the ground floor. right now, we're all hanging out in the huge living room, chilling and talking.

surprisingly, zuko is doing pretty well, giving the fact that we're staying in the house where his dead mother spent all of her childhood. he seems to have made peace with himself and his feelings, which makes me the proudest boyfriend in the world.

all of us retreat to our rooms pretty early, as we are still exhausted from the time we spent on the road, but we decide to do some fun activities the first thing in the morning, tomorrow.

"ugh, i'm so worn out that can't even keep my eyes open" i sigh and throw myself on the bed, letting my body sink in the bedsheets.

zuko chuckles, laying down next to me and i force my weary eyes to open just so i can look at him.

"i'm not even tired" he says, staring at the ceiling with his arms bended under his head.

"you're not human" i mutter and roll over to him.

i put my arms around his waist and bury my head in his chest. he unties my hair and begins running his gentle fingers through it, which relaxes me and makes me even sleepier.

"i don't know, just- being with you guys fills me up with energy" he clarifies.

i give half a smile that he can't see anyway, but i'm glad that he feels this way, even if i'm too lifeless to form a reply. after a few moments, i break the silence.

"zuko, i'm hot, help me take my shirt off" i look up at him with puppy eyes that i know he can't resist.

"what?" he giggles.

"please" i squeack. "i'm so tired... i can't move" i place the back of my palm on my forehead for some theatrics.

ok, maybe i am exaggerating, but it's more fun to be undressed by someone else, than to do it yourself, especially if that someone is the precious bean sitting next to me.

"fine" he rolls his eyes, but i can tell he's hiding his smile.

he grabs the hem of my t-shirt and pulls it over my head. as soon as he does that, i throw the piece of cloth on the floor and lean in to kiss him, forcing him to lay on his back, with me on top of him. "i thought you were too tired to move" he imitates me in a squeacky voice, then punches my chest jokingly.

i puff and roll my eyes, while playing with his fluffy hair.

"pft- i forgot all about it when i saw how hot you were while undressing me" i wink at him and smirk.

he looks away, trying to seem unimpressed, but his red cheeks are betraying him and he knows it.

"just go to sleep" he pushes me off of him and gets under the blanket.

i follow his lead and he picks up a book that he brought with him, from the nightstand. he really can't sleep, huh?

"read to me" i request, staring into his eyes. "please"

he accepts and i quickly nuzzle into his belly, as he sits up. i can't focus on any word he's saying, but his calming voice soon starts to fade away, and i finally fall asleep.

&#45;-

zuko's POV (the next day)

after we all wake up, we have some breakfast, get in our swimming suits, and then we hit the beach. even if it's morning, the sun is already burning up like crazy.

"ugh, the sand makes my vision foggy, i hate it" toph puffs.

she soon engages in a conversation with her girlfriend that i don't pay attention to, because i look around and i can't spot my boyfriend.

"have you seen sokka?" i ask katara and she shrugs.

"i think i saw him go behind those rocks over there" she gestures towards a place a few meters away.

she then goes back to applying sun cream on herself and helping aang do it as well. i thank her breifly and head to the place she pointed to, but as soon as i get there, sokka jumps in front of me.

"no, no, go back, you can't see it yet!" he turns me around by placing his palms on my arms.

"see what?" i frown confused.

"just wait and i'll call you when i'm done" he says and i have no choice but comply.

i return to the gAang and sit on the sand with my legs crossed, a little pouty. now i'm curious to find out what the hell he's doing there that i can't see.

"did you guys know that if a jellyfish stings you, someone has to pee on you so it doesn't hurt anymore?" aang informs us as a matter of fact.

"i didn't know, actually" i giggle at how random it was.

katara tells us that she saw something about that on friends and toph mumbles something about how disgustingly straight that show is. 'tara rolls here eyes, even if toph can't see her, and then she grabs aang's hand and drags him into the water with her.

i lay down with my hands behind my head and i decide to just enjoy the warmth of the sun on my skin, the soft breeze, and the sound of the seagulls that keep flying around. after a while, when i am about to fall asleep, i hear sokka's voice shouting for me.

i open my eyes and the way he's jumping up and down in the distance makes me smile. i get up and start walking towards him, and so does the rest of the gAang. i guess everyone's curious to see what all the secrecy was about.

"come!" sokka urges me excitedly, by putting his hand in mine.

"ok, ok, i'm coming!" i stumble as he's pulling me a little too hard.

he leads me behind the rocks and extends his arms forward.

"ta-da!" he gestures towards something and it takes me a moment to realise what it is.

it's a pile of sand, gathered together, with some seaweed on the tip, and a few shells that appear to be forming two eyes and a mouth.

"sokka, what in the world is that?" katara asks with her arms pinned on her hips.

sokka seems surprised that we can't tell what it actually is.

"it's zuko, of course!" he explains, like it's so obvious.

katara, aang and jane start laughing so hard that it's a wonder they're not rolling on the ground.

"i think it really looks like sparky" toph remarks and everyone but me and sokka starts laughing even harder, because she can't actually see it.

"oh- i'm so sorry, zuko" aang says as he wipes a tear from his eye.

sokka pouts, his shoulders drop and he starts poking the sand with his toes.

"i love it, sokka" i state and everyone looks surprised.

and i'm not lying. the sole fact that he thought of making me out of sand makes me so genuinely happy that i couldn't care less of what it actually looks like.

"really?" he lifts his eyes to look at me with hope imprinted in them.

i nod with a smile, and his entire face lights up. he throws his arms around my neck to hug me tightly, and i return the embrace.

"ugh- don't tell me the idiots are kissing" toph slaps her forhead.

me and sokka giggle, while everyone else mumbles something about getting horrible oogies.

"you better leave" sokka advises them, as his lips meet mine, which makes everyone let out puking noises and walk away.

sokka places his palm on my lower back and we somehow get down on the sand, where we keep making out, like- a lot, until our lips feel numb.

## Chapter 30

zuko's POV

the last couple of days have been really great on the island. we actually had time to relax and enjoy ourselves, by having water fights 10 times a day, and eating junk food, like ice cream, pizza and burgers all the time.

yesterday, we buried sokka neck-deep in sand and laughed our asses off when he was shouting to get him out because he couldn't move. (we made sure to take pictures)

now, as the sun came down completely, we decided to make a camp fire and sit around it to tell creepy stories while eating marshmellows. sokka has just finished narrating something about an angry platypus bear attacking some villagers (it was not scary at all, but i couldn't bring myself to tell him that).

"i think i liked the man with the sword for a hand better" aand says, reffering to a previous, also not horror story that sokka told us before the platypus bear one.

sokka crosses his arms to his chest and brings out his bottom lip, giving up on trying to make us frightened.

"you really don't know how to have fun, losers" toph remarks and starts digging a hole in the sand with her feet.

she has gotten used to 'seeing' in the sand, so she stopped complaining about it whenever she had the chance.

"i know a story" katara intervenes when she sees that nobody else has any ideas. "but it's a real one, that mom told me, from when she was a little girl"

silence takes over us and eveyone makes themselves more comfortable on the beach towels we are sitting on, waiting for her to speak.

"one winter, a snow storm buried mom's entire childhood village for weeks" 'tara begins. "she realised after a while that she hadn't seen her friend, nini, for a month, so she went to check her home"

i feel sokka moving slightly closer to me, while still paying complete attention to his sister, who is narrating everything with a deep, profound voice.

"nobody was there, except for a fire flickering in the fireplace. she searched the house for a little bit, then she suddenly heard a trembling voice: 'it's so cold, and i can't get warm' - " katara continues and uses a high pitched voice for her last words.

i get some shivers down my spine, and i'm pretty sure so does sokka, because he slides his hand into mine. i notice that june got closer to toph as well, and aang is gradually narrowing the space between his eyebrows.

" - and that's when she saw nini standing by the fire, looking like she was frozen, so she rushed outside to ask for help, but when she returned, nini was gone" she goes on.

"where did she go?" sokka asks concerned.

he really is caught up in this story. maybe he's curious, because it's something that happened to his mother.

"no one knows" katara answers while staring into nothing. "nini's house stands empty to this day, but sometimes, people see smoke coming out of the chimney, like nini is still trying to get warm"

nobody lets out a single sound, all of us staring at the girl with hair loopies that just shocked us with her story.

"ok, i admit, that was a good one, sugar queen" toph raises her arms in defeat.

we quickly get out of the stiff atmosphere and return to joking around and roasting those marshmellows that sokka begged us to buy from the store the other day.

after a few hours, when it gets pretty late, everyone but me and sokka retreats to their rooms, leaving us two alone on the quiet beach. the fire is almost out, but there are still a few embers shining in the dark.

"let's take a walk" i suggest.

i don't want to go inside just yet, because the night is so beautiful, and the stars look magnificent on the cloudless, almost pitch black sky.

"do you ever think about how meaningless we are, compared to the infinite universe?" i ask sokka, as i can't take my eyes off the full moon that is accompanying us on our walk.

he takes a second to think about it, but then he finally answers.

"sometimes" he says. "i try not to, because when i do, i start to believe that nothing that i do is important"

"i mean-" i reply. "whatever we do, the order of things won't change. we could die right now and the universe wouldn't be affected at all. we're meaningless like that" i chuckle.

to be honest, i kind of find some comfort in that, knowing that nothing matters. so why not do whatever i want and makes me happy?

"well, if you died, my universe would pe destroyed" sokka turns to me and stops walking.

he cups my face with one hand and stares into my eyes, the only light reaching to us being the one cast by the moon.

"that's because you are my universe" he adds, and presses his lips against mine, softly.

we're standing right on the shore and the chilly waves of the sea occasionally crash against our bare feet. i shiver a little, because of the cold and sokka pulls away.

"are you cold?" he asks me, and i nod. "i know just the way to warm you up"

he smirks, and our kiss becomes deeper, as the seconds pass. we decide without actually speaking to lay down, and we do that, not caring that our bodies are halfway on the dry sand, while our legs are being touched by the cool water.

**⚠**️warning - it gets ✨ spicy✨ **⚠**️

sokka caresses my cheek with his thumb, while his lips are busy dancing with mine. i slide my hands under his tank-top and i start moving them around in circular motions, which results in sokka groaning a little and sending vibrations into my mouth.

he positiones himself better between my legs, which i wrap around him, so i can bring him closer to me. he starts kissing my neck, nibbling on certain spots, and i know for a fact that it's gonna leave marks, but i don't really care, to be honest.

with my approval, he takes my t-shirt off so he can make his way better to my chest. i bury my hands in his now untied hair and i shut my eyes, letting out soft moans occasionally, which makes sokka smirk every time.

**⚠**️end of warning**⚠**️

"wh- wait- sokka" i put my palms on his broad shoulders and he lifts his head so he can look at me.

"do you want me to stop?" he questions carefully, already getting off of me.

i clutch his arm with my hand to stop him from departing and i shake my head firmly.

"n- no" i assure him, patting him on his chest. "but i think i'm ready"

i stare up right at his ocean eyes and by the way he looks back at me, i'm convinced that he's the only person i'll ever be able to love this way, and that he feels the same way i do.

"ready for what?" he asks, but i'm pretty sure he knows what i'm talking about.

i roll my eyes and he grins, waiting for my response.

"you know-" i raise my eyebrow. "to do it"

i open my eyes wider to suggest what i actually mean, and he thoughtfully brushes some locks of hair out of my face.

"you sure?" he asks me once again. "don't feel forced to do it or something"

"yes, i'm sure, sokka" i state firmly. "there's no one else i'd rather do it with for the first time- or-any time, actually" i chuckle, and i feel my cheeks heating up rapidly.

sokka's smile widens and he gets up, offering me his hand, which i take and stand up as well.

"let's go inside, i don't think it would be very comfortable on the beach" sokka giggles and i agree with him.

i mean, at least the first time should be on an actual bed, after that... we'll see.

we start kissing again, and we somehow stumble until we reach the house, then we check to see if everyone's asleep. fortunately, sokka and i spent quite some time on the beach, so nobody is awake at this time.

we make our way upstairs, trying not to make too much noise, and as soon as we enter, sokka pushes me on the bed and climbs on top of me, without making our lips part.

"uhm- do you have- um-" i stutter and he gets out of bed.

"of course i do" he puffs and goes to his backpack.

i lean on my elbows so i can take a better look at him, but then i frown.

"wait, you knew we were gonna do it here?" i ask.

"of course i didn't know, i'm not psychic" he says indignated. "but i had to be ready, just in case, you know?"

he smiles and stands right up as soon as he finds what he was looking for, then he locks the door with a click.

&#45;-

sokka's POV

when i wake up, i find a shirtless zuko curled up to my bare chest, breathing regularly with his arms around my waist. i glance at the time on my phone and i see that it's almost one in the afternoon, but as much as i'd hate to move out of zuko's grip, i really need to pee.

i get out of bed, careful not to make any sudden moves, because i bet zuko's still pretty tired from last night. ;)

i go to the bathroom, do my business, brush my teeth, and then i decide to go downstairs, where i find the entire gAang in the kitchen, eating some pancakes that katara probably made. dessert for lunch? i wonder who had this brilliant idea.

"morning" i greet them while i grab a green apple from which i take a bite.

they turn their heads so they can look at me, and they continue to stare for a long moment, until i can't handle the pressure anymore.

"what?" i burst at last. "why are you all looking at me?"

aang starts giggling, and he covers his mouth with his hand, trying to stop himself when katara throws him a scolding look.

"did you have fun last night?" my sister looks at me accusing.

oh shit, oh shit, oh shit.

"wh- what?" my voice cracks, sounding more like a squeack.

i start rubbing the back of my neck, full of embarrassment from head to toe. someone kill me now, please.

"aw, how sweet, he's trying to act like he doesn't know what we're talking about" june intervenes, making toph smirk.

fortunately, i don't have to answer anything, because zuko walks down the stairs, rubbing his eyes.

"hey guys" he mutters in a raspy voice that gives me goosebumps.

he's welcomed by the same stares that i got a few minutes ago and he seems confused.

"wow, sparky, i'm surprised you can walk after last night!" toph exclaims and i choke on my apple.

i start coughing and i glance at zuko, whose eyes have widened and his face has turned all red in less than 2 seconds.

"toph, you're gross" katara rolls her eyes.

"hey, i am just being honest, it really is a miracle!" the blind girl defends herself.

zuko leans over to me and is about to whisper in my ear.

"sokka, i think they know... how do they know?" he says in a shaky voice, but his words turn out loud enough so anyone can hear.

"yes, sparky, of course we know" toph points out. "this house is old and the ceiling of our rooms were squeacking, you idiots"

i slap my forehead, and so does zuko, at the same time.

"besides" toph continues. "you weren't exactly quiet - yes, sparky, i would be looking at you if i wasn't blind! i think the whole island was able to hear you"

ok, ok, that might be my fault... but- yeah, it was nice hearing him scream my name, i have to admit. not really the time to think about that, is it?

"can we please stop talking about it?" zuko whines, while taking a seat at the table and burying his face in his arms.

i sit down next to him and pat his back gently. he looks like he's about to puke from all the embarrassment.

"it's fine, baby. next time we can do it on the beach, so we can be alone, or there's this place i saw-"

"sokka!" katara throws a roll of paper towels at my head.

her aim was perfect, because she hits me, but she's right, it's probably innapropriate to talk about this around my sister.

"sorry, sorry, i'll shut up now" i say and start stuffing my face with some chocolate pancakes.

## Chapter 31

zuko's POV

we decided it would be fun to explore the island, outside of the private property i inhereted from my mom, so here we are, in the town center.

it was an amazement for all of us to witness how... alive this island actually is. i don't usually enjoy crowded places, but seeing so many people, of various races and speaking different languages, all gathered together, gave me some kind of comfort.

everywhere i look, there are people laughing, dancing, children running around and playing games, merchants selling traditional food, clothes, and jewlery... everyone seems so happy.

"oh... my... spirits..." sokka gasps and speed walks to one of the stalls.

i follow him, in order to see what all the fuss is about, and i see him picking up a green and yellow bag, which he immediately buys.

"uhm- sokka?" aang raises an eyebrow. "what do you need a purse for?"

sokka glances at him, insulted, while the bald kid giggles.

"it is not a purse!" my boyfriend defends himself. "it's a man bag!"

we all laugh, because it actually is a purse that and, if you ask me, it's hideous.

"whatever you say, big bro" katara mocks him and takes off with aang, still chuckling.

sokka pouts, but he brushes it off quickly when him and i start wandering around from booth to booth, taking our time to admire everything. after about an hour or so, we call the others and we decide to go to a coffee shop.

we sit down at a table outside, under a huge ranibow umbrella, meant to shelter us from the burning sun. we get our cold drinks and start chattering, when i spot a group of people our age from another table glancing our way.

they are talking to eachother, while occasionally looking at us, until a girl dressed in pink clothes, with a long, braided ponytail stands up from her seat. she pulls by the sleeve another girl, dressed in all black, with two buns on the top of her head and a bored, annoyed look in her eyes, and they head our way.

as they're walking towards us, i notice another guy from their table, one with platinum blond hair and green eyes, glancing at me. we make eye contact, but he doesn't look away when i catch him staring, but instead, he smirks and winks at me. i turn my head back to our own table and i blame my burning cheeks on the heat.

"hey guys!" the girl wearing pink adresses us. "i'm ty lee, and this is mai"

she points at the girl in black, who just picks on her nails, rolling her eyes. she seem to have been forced to come here, but mumbles a breif 'hello'.

"anyways, you guys seem really cool and me and my friends-" ty lee gestures to their table. "-were wondering if you would like to come to a party we're throwing tonight"

we look at eachother, exchanging glances, and nobody seems to be against potential entertainment, so we shrug and accept.

"we'd love a party!" aang exclaims and smiles from ear to ear.

"great!" ty lee squeacks.

she proceeds to fill us in with all the details, about the location, time and whatever, then the two girls return to their table.

"ok, that was random" katara says and we have no choice but to agree.

"they seem nice though" june shrugs. "well, ty lee does, because the other one looked like the was thinking of ways to murder us"

we all giggle, but i believe she might be more than she lets others see. i also hate meeting new people, because i'm always awkward and incapable of making small talk, and i start stuttering like shit. she might feel the same, so i'm not gonna judge her for being withdrawn.

"i can't believe the last party we went to was one that jet invited us to" sokka spits out. "it was ages ago!"

wow, it really was a pretty long time ago, wasn't it? i smile to myself when i think of that night, because it was that day that me and sokka kissed for the first time, and so my life changed forever. it will be hard for this party to compare to that, but let's hope we'll at least have fun.

&#45;-

when we get back to our house, i take a quick cold shower, because the heat is unbearable, and so does sokka, then we start preparing for the party.

first, i put on some smudged black eyeliner, and sokka asks me to help him do his, which i obviously accept.

"you know what would look good on you?" i ask, analysing his pretty face once i'm done with his eyes. "some white freckles"

he raises an eyebrow in disbelief, while i search for some white eyeliner in my makeup case.

"just trust me on this one" i say and i sit down on his lap so i can get a better angle.

i make some different sized white dots where freckles are supposed to be, then smudge them a little, by tapping my finger on them. i take a step back so i can look at the final result and i genuinely feel like i'm falling for him harder and harder each day.

he looks like he just jumped out of an anime, with those shimmering blue orbs, and the white freckles are just like shining stars on his caramel skin.

"you look gorgeous" i tell him and he hurries to look at himself in the mirror.

"oh, spirits, i really do!" he shouts. "damn, you're a genius, i look even hotter with these!"

he wraps his arms around my waist and his lips meet mine in less than a second, filling me with his warmth. he sinks one of his hands in my hair and starts playing with it, then we take some steps without breaking the kiss, until we reach the bed.

sokka pulls me on top of him and i place my knees on each side of his body, cupping his face with both of my hands. he starts moving his hands up and down my back, bringing me even closer.

"mm- zuko?" sokka groans between kisses. "when are we gonna repeat the thing we did the other day?"

i smile and keep kissing him, as i recall that night... it was truly amazing, and i wonder why we didn't do it sooner.

"well" i part our lips. "surely not now, because we have to get ready"

i brush my index on his nose, and he pouts, but he knows we agreed to avoid doing it if there is even the slightest chance of someone hearing us.

"maybe we can go on the beach after the party" i suggest, and sokka's face lights up instantly.

"can't wait!" he grins and i get off him.

after a few minutes of searching through the clothes we brought with us, we finally find some decent ones that we can wear to the party.

i decide to dress in some white large pants, with a black short sleeved shirt, which i unbutton halfway, while sokka jumps in some black cuffed jeans and puts on a blue tank top, that matches his eyes perfectly.

"i guess we're good to go" sokka says, after we take some pictures, because we look really good.

we get downstairs to see if the others are ready, but apparently, june is helping katara straighten her hair, and they also have to do their makeup, which they will take forever. me and sokka decide to go ahead and meet them there, because it makes no sense to wait here all dressed up.

&#45;-

"wow, they even hired a bartender to make the drinks!" sokka remarks once we step inside the house.

ty lee greeted us at the entrance, but she soon got caught up in a conversation with other people, so she just told us to have fun and feel like home. how does she trust we won't, like- steal anything or destroy stuff around the house? she doesn't even know us.

anyway, we spend a minute admiring the house, that has an entire wall made out of glass, which allows us to see the pool filled with people in the backyard. there really are a lot of teenagers here and i am a little anxious, but not like i used to be.

i really think being with sokka all this time made the discomfort i felt around others diminish, because when i'm with him, i don't really care if people see me or not. i just know thay he's the only one that truly matters to me.

we dance a little, pretending like nobody is around us, then we eat some snacks and decide to go to the bar and drink something. i'm clueless when it comes to this stuff, but sokka orderes some alcohool cocktails for both of us, so i trust him.

i get a pink little umbrella in my drink, while sokka gets a green one, which he is weirdly excited about. i take a sip and at first it burns my throat, because i'm not used to drinking, but it's actually pretty good.

we chill at the bar for a while, and at some point i lay my eyes on a person that is staring back at me. it's that blond boy from the coffee shop, and he smiles at me, but it's kind of unsettling, so i shift my attention somewhere else quickly.

"look, the rest of the gAang is finally here!" sokka gestures to the door, as they're coming in.

they soon spot us and head our way. i put my drink on the bar and turn around to greet everyone.

"you sure took your time" i chuckle and toph puffs.

"i was about to drown myself when i kept hearing sugar queen blabber about her stupid hair" toph complains.

katara rolls her eyes and they start arguing, but aang stops them pretty fast. he really is a pacifist, he can't stand seeing people yell at eachother.

"come on, guys, let's dance!" sokka encourages everyone.

i finish the rest of my drink, then we all join the dancing crowd, jumping and moving around on the rythm of the music.

after a while, my head starts buzzing and my brain feels like it's being stung by dozens of needles. i rub my temples, hoping it will go away, but nothing changes. it's probably because of that drink i had, so i decide to brush it off.

however, as the minutes pass, i start feeling worse. i look at sokka, to see if the alcohool had the same effect on him, but he appears to be fine, as he looks like he's enjoying himself. my vision is getting a little blurry, and i try to cast away the fog before my eyes by blinking repeatedly. it doesn't work.

"i'm gonna go to the bathroom, i'll be right back" i let sokka know and he nods in understanding.

i walk away from the crowd, stumbling on my feet, despite trying really hard not to fall. i feel like i'm stepping on water, about to sink at any moment, but i somehow reach the stairs that lead to the next floor. there has to be a bathroom there, then i can splash some water on my face and i'll be fine, right? this is all happening because i'm dizzy and the heat is too much for me, right?

as i'm climbing the apparently endless stairs, i hit something, but i'm not sure what it is, until i lift my eyes and meet some green ones. it's that blond guy again.

"are you lost?" he asks me.

i rub my forehead, trying to see clearly, but it's to no avail.

"i- i'm looking for the... bathroom" i mutter.

he probably knows this house, so i guess he could tell me where it is.

"come with me, i'll take you there" i see him smiling, even if my vision is fuzzy.

he sees me having a hard time walking on my own, so he lets me lean on him, which i'm kind of thankful for. we reach a door, and he opens it for me. i step inside and look around me, but i am extremely confused.

"this- this isn't the bathroom" i stutter and panick starts to flow through my body.

there is a pause that lasts only a few seconds, but the pressure i feel is infinite.

"don't you know that you shouldn't leave your drink unsupervised?" he says and i get shivers down my spine.

i turn around and see him locking the door behind him.

## Chapter 32

zuko's POV

‼️**⚠**️WARNING - sexual assault**⚠**️‼️

as the blonde guy walks towards me, i back down, one step at a time, trying to get away from him, until i feel my back meeting the hard surface of a wall. i can't wrap my head around this, i'm still hoping that it's just a joke and he's about to laugh in my face, but his expression does not support my theory at all.

"i think i should go-" i barely manage to say, but now he's a lot closer.

when i am about to try to walk away, he slams his hand on the wall, blocking me and pinning me back against it. he is only inches away from me and i feel my stomach tying itself into knots at how close his face is to mine.

"but why would you wanna leave?" he asks, acting all confused.

he moves his fingers around on my chest, fixing me with his eyes.

"p- please" i whisper. "i- i have a boyfriend"

he starts laughing, and the mere sound makes me nauseous. i try to swallow the lump that has formed in my throat, but it just keeps growing bigger and bigger.

"what? you mean that ponytail guy?" he puffs unimpressed. "i bet i can show you a much greater time than he ever could"

he winks at me and slightly bites his lower lip, while staring at my lips and my scar, which he starts touching with his fingers.

"you know... i kinda have a thing for scars and yours is really attractive" he smirks and caresses the left side of my face with his index.

then, without any warning, he smashes his lips into mine aggressively. i try to get him off me, by pushing my hands against his chest, but my limbs are made of jelly because of whatever he put in my stupid drink, and i can't compare my strenght to his.

he then moves his lips on my jaw, making his way down to my neck, which urges me to rip my skin to shreds just so i can get rid of this horrific sensation.

"please-" i squeack. "please stop"

i shut my eyes and grimace, but he completely ignores me. he slides his hands under my already unbuttoned shirt, exploring my body with his fingertips.

"spirits, you're so hot" he gasps. "i've been waiting all night to do this"

he rips the rest of the buttons and throws away my shirt, leaving me exposed. he keeps moving his hands all over my bare skin and he presses himself against me harder, until i can feel something... poking my inner thigh.

i swear, i'm going to puke, why can't i just die right now?

he takes his shirt off, too, and while he does that, i try to use the last bit of my power to get away, but he is faster than me. he puts me back in my place and then i feel his hand on my crotch, but i instinctively push his arm away.

"please stop" i manage to mutter, and after hearing my own voice, i realise i am sobbing.

"oh, don't cry, darling" he says and wipes my tears with his fingers. "i promise i'll make you feel good"

i won't. i won't feel good ever again. all i want to do is stop existing, at least for now. i begin shaking my head frantically and begging him to stop, but he starts working on his belt and unzipping his pants.

**⚠**️end of warning**⚠**️

i squeeze my eyes shut, ready to take on whatever he wants to do to me, but soon i feel he's not that close to me anymore, which confuses me. i open my eyes and see another person is here - when did they come in?

i wipe the tears in my eyes with the back of my palm and try to focus on what's happening around me, even though i can't think straight at the moment from various reasons.

"get the fuck out, or i swear i'll fucking stab you!" a girl with black hair shouts. "RIGHT NOW" she emphasises.

is that... mai?

she's pressing him against the wall, away from me, with a knife held against his throat. i see their lips moving, but i have no idea what they are saying, because i lean against the wall, with my head feeling heavy. my legs decide to stop working, so i slide down until i ultimately reach the floor.

everything around me is spinning... or am i the one spinning? i couldn't tell.

i bury my face in my hands, when someone starts shaking my shoulders. i lift my head and barely open my eyes, because my eyelids are almost glued to eachother.

"hey! are you ok?" the girl - mai - asks me, but she seems to be talking from far, far away.

she gently slaps my face, probably trying to make me come to my senses, but i feel myself drift off gradually, until everything turns black and it's finally quiet.

&#45;-

i partially gain conciousness at some point, as i can hear people talking around me, but i can't distinguish the voices. i can't even open my eyes.

"what happened?"

"no time to talk, let's just get him home quickly"

"oh, spirits, is he gonna be alright?"

"shit, how did this happen?"

"he'll tell you when he's ready"

i then black out completely.

&#45;-

i open my eyes with a groan, because of the light coming from the window, and i bring a hand to my forehead, attempting to stop the buzzing that has fortunately faded by now. i sit up in order to look around me, and i notice that i'm laying on a bed, covered with a white sheet.

"you're awake!" i turn my head, startled by the sudden noise, and i see sokka sitting on a chair by the bed.

he reaches out to touch me with his hand, but as soon as he does, i flinch and i almost jump out of bed. he retreats his hand quickly, as if my skin was a burning oven.

for a second, i had miraculously forgotten what happened last night, but every single detail is starting to come back rapidly. a shiver pierces through my entire body and i start to panick, because every inch of my skin is tingling like crazy, and i can't shake off the sensation.

i gasp, and i feel like i'm going to drown, but even though i'm not actually underwater, i can't seem to get enough air. i hear some mumbling and i think that's sokka talking, but i can't make out what he's actually saying.

i start rubbing my skin, at first slowly, then i fasten the pace and grow the intensity, as i'm hyperventilating and trembling badly. i suddenly feel some warm hands around my cheeks, lifting my head and forcing me to stare at two blue eyes.

"zuko, baby, you're hurting yourself" sokka says and the look in his eyes makes my hands move slower.

my brows wrinkle when i come back to my senses and i hug myself, as i start sobbing violently. i cluch on the material of the sheet and squeeze it to my chest, until my knuckles turn white.

"can i- can i hug you?" i hear sokka asking me.

the way his voice cracked has just destroyed me in less than a second, and i can tell he's on the verge of tears. i cover my face with my clenched fists and shake my head, with tears escaping through my fingers.

"i'm sorry-" i squeack. "i- i can't-"

images from last night start popping up, digging in my brain like some evil, long claws. sharp touches, cursed kisses, poisonous words, a mischievous smile.

"what should i do?" sokka questions with a trembling voice. "i don't know what to do..."

i don't either...

i lift my head up and glance at him, only to see him with his hands pressed against his face. he seems so crushed, so hopelessly defeated, with tears running down his face.

"don't- don't cry, please" i stutter and he looks up at me.

i sniffle and hug my knees to my chest, letting out the air i've been holding in my lungs.

"i want to help you, zuko" sokka whispers. "just tell me what to do to make it better. i'll do anything!"

there's nothing you can do to fix it.

"i- i can still feel his hands on me, sokka" i state with desperation imprinted in my eyes.

he grimaces in both pain and anger and presses his eyes with his palms. his shoulders drop and he starts shaking his head.

"i wasn't there-" he sighs. "i should have been there. i should have come with you. i shouldn't have left you alone..."

"please do not blame yourself for this-" i squeal. "it's my fault. i was wearing that shirt and it was showing too much skin, and i shouldn't have left my drink on that bar-"

tears don't stop sliding down my face for even a second, and i wonder how many of them i have left.

"no, no, no" sokka cuts me off and our eyes link. "i don't want to hear you saying any of that bullshit. you did nothing wrong, it was not your fault! the only one to blame is that- that piece of shit that i'm gonna fucking crush"

sokka clenches his fist and all i can see is wild fire burning in his eyes.

after a while, i ask him how i got here, and sokka tells me about how mai ran to get him as fast as she could after she found me. apparently, she was looking for a room where she and her girlfriend, ty lee, could be alone, and as she was walking down the hallway, she heard me begging someone to stop what they were doing. in addition, she busted the door and scared him off with the pocket knife she always carries around.

"wh- what if she hadn't-" i sob quietly, biting my lip so i can repress any sound threatening to get out.

i don't even want to imagine that. i wouldn't be able to take it. only the thought of it makes the fist around my heart squeeze tighter, until i can't breathe anymore.

"let's not think about that" sokka suggests, but it's inevitable.

i'm sure that, once the night comes, and i'm left alone with my thoughts, the image of that boy will creep up in my mind, making sure to keep reminding me of how i had no control over my fate.

i was completely useless, unable to move or change the course of events, which made me a puppet in his hands. a rag doll, waiting to be messed with.

i spend the rest of the day in bed and i refuse to see anyone besides sokka. i'm too ashamed to face them, because i'm sure they will look at me with pity, and i hate it when people do that.

sokka brought me food, but i wasn't able to eat very much, and we just stayed on opposite sides of the bed, in silence. he gave me space, he didn't insist on hugging me and he didn't ask me for details of what happened, which i'm thankful for.

i wish i could sink in his arms, but every time i feel someone else's hands on me, i instantly get transported back to that unknown room, where i went through some of the most terrible moments of my life.

for now, just feeling sokka's presence is more than enough for me, to calm me down and make me feel alright. he doesn't even have to say a word, just knowing that he's there is all the support that i need at the moment.

i'm laying on my back, with my eyes plastered on the ceiling, even after many hours have passed since the sun went down. i'm afraid to go to sleep, because every time i shut my eyes for more than 5 seconds, i can perfectly see that face, those green eyes and that blonde hair.

i know sokka isn't sleeping either, even if he's not letting out a single word. he's laying on his side, without taking his eyes off me, as if he did, i would dissapear from his sight and never return again. i'm pretty sure he actually is blaming himself, though i told him not to.

i guess that makes us even, because i can't stop putting the blame on myself either.

## Chapter 33

sokka's POV

i couldn't shut my eyes all night, even after zuko fell asleep. i had to wake him up at some point, because he was twitching and turning, obviously having a bad dream, but he somehow went back to sleep.

my mind flies to the moment when when mai came rushing to me and started dragging me by the forearm, after only saying 'it's your boyfriend', which made me panick instantly, because i was waiting for him to return from the bathroom. when i found him unconscious on the floor, half naked and with red marks all over his neck i- i collapsed on the floor next to him.

and he wouldn't wake up, no matter how hard i shook him. why do bad things keep happening to him, when i'm not there to do anything about it? i should have gone with him to the bathroom, i should have made sure he was alright...

but i didn't, so now he is suffering and i can't do anything to make the pain go away.

i look at the time on my phone and see that it's almost 8. i decide to text mai (we exchanged numbers last night, after we brought zuko home), even though she probably won't answer this early in the morning. i ask her to call me as soon as she sees my message and, to my surprise, my phone starts ringing after less than 5 minutes.

i turn the sound off as quick as possible, and glance at zuko to see if he was woken up by it. fortunately, he's still sleeping, so i gently brush some hair away from his forehead and then i leave the room.

i answer mai's call from the hallway and i ask her to help me find the douchebag that dared to lay his hands on my precious baby. she tells me his name is luke and that she knows the place where he hangs out with his buddies all the time, but that he won't be there until around 11.

i ask her to come to our house at that time, so she can lead me to where that place is and she quickly agrees. until then, i go downstairs and decide to make some breakfast for everybody, so i can stop looking at the clock every single minute out of impatience.

i make a bunch of sandwiches for the whole gAang, but i shape zuko's so they look like little hearts. the cuts i made are uneven and not very smooth, but you can still tell what they represent! (at least i hope so)

i put the sandwich hearts on a plate and go upstairs, only to find that zuko is awake, rubbing his eyes and yawning, with his hair being a complete mess.

"good morning!" i greet him in a cheerful tone, hoping it would make him feel better.

i don't feel cheerful at all, but i can't let him see how down i am, as he already looks so drained and exhausted, even though he just woke up.

"hey" he says breifly.

he doesn't even look at me for more than two seconds, because he then zones out and just stares into space.

"i made you breakfast" i pull him out of his trance after a quick moment.

i hand him the plate with food and he puts on a faint half smile, that crushes my soul.

"thank you, sokka" he mutters.

no making fun of the weird shapes that -who am i kidding- don't look very much like hearts? no jokes about how i have two left hands and can't cut anything evenly? nothing.

my heart sinks and my shoulders drop in defeat for a second, but i brush off the shitty feeling that has taken over me. for him.

"i'm gonna make you some tea" i force myself to smile. "be right back"

he lifts his eyes to meet mine and he nods, but even after i leave the room, his empty stare is still ravashing my insides, like someone has just stabbed me and is twisting the knife repeatedly in my body.

&#45;-

zuko's POV

sokka cut the sandwiches he made me in the shape of hearts and all i said was a plain 'thank you'. i feel like the worst boyfriend ever, maybe he thinks i'm ungrateful, when i know how much he's trying to be there for me, and i appreciate it so fucking much.

he probably doesn't know, but i can damn well see past his comforting smiles, because his eyes tell a whole different story. i know he's worried, he's desperate, he feels like shit because of me.

why can't i just forget about what happened and move on? it could have been way worse. some people have gone through so much more terrible things, so what right do i have to make everyone around me feel bad? i am so weak for being stuck in that moment, even in my dreams. i saw him in my nightmares, he wouldn't let me go and this time, mai never showed up to rescue me. i was all alone, terrified and helpless... good thing sokka woke me up in time.

while sokka is downstairs, making some tea, i get out of bed and head to the bathroom so i can brush my teeth before eating. i didn't stop to look at myself in the mirror, more specifically, at my scar. he... he liked it, he found it attractive. he tried to... do things to me because of this stupid scar that i can't get rid of.

i touch the scar with my fingers, and i can somehow feel his touch once again, in the exact same spot my fingers are right now. i can basically sense his breath on my face from when he was talking to me, and my lower lip starts to tremble without my consent.

my eyes involuntarily climb down to my neck and i feel like puking, because i haven't analysed myself properly since that night long enough to notice the red marks all over my skin.

i brush my hand over them and i get shivers down my spine, as i take a few steps back, away from my reflection, until i hit one of the bathroom walls with my back.

everything starts replayig in my head and i can sense his hands all over my body, so much that it makes me wonder if he actually is here or not, because it does feel extremely real.

**⚠**️warning - self harm**⚠**️

i let myself slide until i reach the floor and i start rubbing my arms, my chest, my abdomen with my hands, trying to get rid of the sensation, but it only intensifies. i desperately take off my shirt, because it's getting in the way and my fingers seem to have developed a life of their own. i sink my nails in the skin on my arms and begin scratching, the strokes becoming more and more frequent and intensified as time passes.

i choke on hot tears and keep going until the tingling on my skin that drives me mad is replaced with sharp pain. i then move on to my chest, where his hands wandered a lot that night. i start scratching with my nails, as if i am trying to dig a hole until i reach my heart so i can finally pull it out and make it all go away.

**⚠**️end of warning**⚠**️

i get startled by a loud thud and i turn my head to see sokka standing in the doorway, with a cup of tea shattered on the floor at his feet.

"zuko-" his voice cracks.

he rushes over to me, falling to his knees and i throw myself in his arms as soon as he's next to me.

"m- make it stop-" my words get muffled in his shirt as i tighten my grip around him. "p- please make it stop"

he gently combs my tangled hair with his hand, keeping my face into his chest. he doesn't seem to care that his shirt is going to be soaking in my tears, he just hugs me tighter.

"shh, it's gonna be alright" sokka whisperes in a weak, trembling voice. "you're gonna be ok"

i don't know who is he trying to convice of that, me of himself? either way, he lets me sob in his arms until there is no way i could squeeze anymore tears out of my body, so i finally leave the embrace. i glance down at his white shirt, and i notice it has some red stains on it, so i frown.

"sokka, where is that blood from?" i ask confused and he looks at me concerned.

his eyes go down to my chest, followed by my arms, and i decide to do the same, only to discover the scratches i gave myself are bleeding, my skin is bright red, and so are my fingertips. my palms start shaking as i stare at them and can't believe i hurt myself that badly.

i gulp, but sokka takes my hands in his and kisses my knuckles, then looks at me with patient eyes, like he's facing an insane person. maybe i am insane, so why does he even bother to stay with me?

"let's take a bath, alright?" he suggests and a nod slowly after a few seconds, without looking at him.

he lets the water run, occasionally checking its temperature, until the bathtub is almost full. i take the rest of my clothes off, except for my underwear, then get in the water, where i hug my knees to my chest and lean my forehead on them. sokka kneels down next to the bathtub.

"can i-?" he asks me, gesturing to the wet towel he's holding in one of his hands.

i nod, then he starts patting the red areas on my skin, cleaning up all the blood. it stings like hell, but at least i can't feel like he's touching me anymore.

"is this ok?" sokka questions when he finishes with my arms and is about to continue with my chest.

i nod again, and let him take care of me. he is gentle with the way he makes the towel touch my body, and he apologises every time i wince due to the stinging sensation, even though it's not really his fault. he then proceeds to help me wash the rest of my body with a sponge full of bubbles.

"hair too?" he asks me after he uses the shower to make the soap go away.

"if you want..." i reply with a lifeless voice.

i hate to bother him this much. i know i'm useless, but am i really this incapable of showering by myself?

he pours some shampoo in my hair and starts moving his fingers slowly on my scalp in circular motions. i shut my eyes and let my head fall slightly backwards, so he can get a better angle. this is actually very relaxing, and the scent of the shampoo is somehow calming. i didn't know i needed this until now.

once i'm done with the bath, i get dressed in some large, comfy clothes and sokka wraps me up in a blanket, despite it being pretty hot in our room. he then gathers up the shattered pieces of the cup of tea he dropped earlier and wipes the liquid off the floor.

"hug, please?" i look up at him as soon as he finishes, and he immediately fuffils my wish.

he gets in bed next to me and i place my head on his chest, wrapping my arms around his waist. he kisses the top of my head and brings me closer to him by embracing me.

"promise me you won't do something like that ever again" sokka mutters in my hair.

he sounds hurt, helpless, defeated, and i hate myself for causing him all of these bad feelings with my reckless behaviour.

"i promise" i almost whisper and nuzzle my face in his chest.

i can feel sokka's heart pounding below my ear, as he's caressing my back with his fingers.

"you have no idea how much i love you, zuko" he begins talking after a moment of silence. "and i hate seeing you hurt, especially now, when i have no idea what to do to help you... trust me, if i could, i would transfer your pain to me in less than a second, just so i can see you happy"

he trails the back of his fingers across my cheek and lifts my chin so he can kiss my forehead, making me melt in his arms.

"maybe you don't realise it, but your mere presence next to me makes it a lot easier to deal with anything" i explain. "and i don't think there's something in particular that you can do. i just have to let time heal all wounds"

i have no other option but to mend at some point. maybe not today, or tomorrow, maybe not even months from now, but however difficult it might seem to wake up some days, i know that i will be able to get past this. forgetting is impossible, and i probably won't ever fully recover, but at least i will be able to function normally, without breaking down like i did today.

suddenly, sokka's phone buzzez two times and he picks it up from the nightstand.

"i gotta go" he says breifly and gets out of bed quickly.

what? why is he in such a rush, what does he have to do that's so important?

"where?" i wonder perplexed and i grab his hand before he can walk away.

"don't worry about it" he brushes my hair with his hand. "i won't be long. eat your breakfast"

he kisses my head, shoves the plate in my hands, then exists the door.

&#45;-

sokka's POV

i hate leaving zuko all confused like that, but i know damn well he would try to stop me if he knew where i am actually going. i go downstairs, where everyone is chilling in the living room, chattering and watching tv.

"make sure zuko eats and hydrates until i return" i say without stopping until i reach the door and put my shoes on.

"where are you going?" katara asks, but i ignore her.

i open the door and i see mai sitting down on the porch, picking on her long, black nails with a pocket knife.

## Chapter 34

sokka's POV

"so this is the place, huh?" i ask mai as we're standing in front of a black door covered in half ripped posters and some weird scribbling.

"yeah" she replies. "i'll wait for you here, but if things go south, just yell or something"
