she seems unbothered, but i guess that's how she appears to be most of the time. despite that, she didn't hesitate even a second before accepting to help me make that asshole, luke, get what he deserves, so i'm grateful for that.

i nod in agreement, then press on the doorknob and get in. at first, my eyes have to adjust to the obscure light in the chamber, contradictory to the bright light coming from the sun. the place isn't especially big, and all i can see are five people about my age, and a few pool tables spread around the room, as well as some arcade games.

two boys are circling one table, clearly being involved in a game of eight ball, calculating their moves, while another three are sitting at a bar, chatting. the air in here reeks of alcohool and cigarettes and it turns my stomach upside down.

"ha! take that, asshole!" one of the guys playing pool shouts, throwing his hands up.

i can only assume he just scored, because his opponent is clearly upset. i don't have time for formalities, so i get straight to the point.

"which one of you is luke?" i ask and everyone stops what they are doing, just to look my way.

it seems like they didn't even notice me coming in, so they look pissed that i interrupted them.

"who's asking?" a guy with blonde hair brings his arms to his chest, lifting one eyebrow.

the rest of the guys glare at me interested and i can only assume they aren't used to getting uninvited guests to their little gatherings.

"just answer the damn question" i spit out, already sick of this place.

i'm starting to get really impatient, and my fists are more than eager to smash the face of the cunt that made zuko have a mental breakdown earlier today.

"he's a tough one, boys" the same guy says with a smirk and they all start laughing. "i'm luke, what the hell do you want?"

suddenly, his face is the only one i can see, and everyone else just vanishes. he's the one that hurt zuko. he made my baby boy suffer and he's gonna pay for it. before i know it, my feet gain a life of their own and i find myself rushing over to him.

he doesn't get to process what's going on, because i grab the collar of his shirt and smash his back against the pool table. his friends immediately want to intervene, but he gestures something with one of his hands, making them stop.

"it's ok, i can handle this" he says with a smirk plastered all over his face. "you can go outside"

the other dudes take a while to walk away, but they eventually exit the building. as i watch them leave, luke gets the chance to push me off him and straightens up, dusting off his shirt.

"it took me a second, but i think i recognise you" he remarks, still smiling like an idiot. "you're the boyfriend of that cute, pale guy with the scar, right?"

as soon as he mentions zuko, i feel disgust filling up my body and all i can see is zuko laying on the floor, crying and struggling to breathe.

"you piece of shit" i swing a punch at him, but he ducks and his fist meets my stomach instead.

a sharp pain takes over the area he hit, but i ignore it, thinking of zuko's suffering in order to fuel my anger and set aside anything else.

"what are you so mad about? i just had a little fun with him, that's all" he mocks me.

he has the audacity to act like he did nothing wrong. i'm gonna kill him. i manage to punch him in the face as hard as i can, seeing a few drops of blood leaving his mouth. i then bend him over so my knee can hit him in the guts, which makes all the air leave his lungs with a growl.

he comes to his senses pretty quickly and strikes my face with his fist, making me take a step back. he wipes some blood from the corner of his lips, grinning, like he's enjoying it.

"y'know, you're pretty lucky to have him." he says. "his lips are so soft and taste so good, and his skin" he pauses and shuts his eyes, letting out a small groan. "just magical"

at this point, i forget all about any sign of pain in my body, because images of him touching and kissing zuko take over my mind completely. i take a swing at him, before he can realise what's going on and he loses balance, relying on the pool table for support.

"you have no idea-" i kick him in the stomach once again and he crumbles to the floor, coughing. "how- much- shit- he's- been- through-"

i take blows at him with my foot after every word, while he's still on the ground. he tries to protect his vital organs with his hands, curling up in a pathetic ball. i get down on top of him and start smacking my clenched fists against his not so smiley face.

"you fucking traumatized him for life, do you know that?" i grab his hair so he can stare me in the eyes as i talk to him. "what gave you the right to think it was ok to do that to him?"

he tries to get me off him, but he's too weak from all the beating, so he has no chance. his face is covered in a red liquid, that has transfered to my hands as well, but that doesn't make me stop. no physical pain that i may inflict on him could be compared to what zuko is feeling right now.

and that probably wasn't the first time he assulted someone. he may have done it in the past, and succeeded in taking advantage of his drugged victims. he makes me sick.

i suddenly lose all the control i have over my body. it's like someone else has taken control over me, and i'm moving mechanically, punching him continuously. it's like the sound of his flesh splitting is the only thing feeding me and giving me satisfaction.

"s- stop" he mumbles. "p- please"

somehow those words make me even angrier at him. i get up and stare at him laying helplessly on the floor.

"huh, that's strange!" i laugh manically. "he said the exact same thing that night, but you didn't stop, did you?"

i yell at him and keep kicking and stepping on him, until i can feel some arms pulling me away from that piece of trash.

"sokka, stop, you're killing him!" i look at the person talking, and i see mai, with her eyes widened as she's looking at the almost lifeless boy coughing up blood on the floor.

i unclench my fists and let my body relax. she's right, the last thing i need is having to go to prison for making an asshole pay for his mistakes.

"i hope you burn in hell" i growl at luke one last time before leaving the building.

&#45;-

as soon as i enter zuko's beach house, everyone except for toph gasps when they look at me, probably because my clothes are stained with blood, and my hands are also painted red.

"what the actual fuck, sokka?" katara shouts and runs over to me.

they all start bombing me with questions that i have absolutely no will to deal with, so i give them some breif answers, hoping i could get away quickly.

"you beat the crap of the dude that assulted zuko?" toph gasps and i see a wide smile forming on her face. "and i thought you were a softie, snoozles!"

she laughs and wants to high five me, but i tell her i shouldn't do that, because i'll get blood on her as well. she somehow gets even more excited when she hears i have that much blood on my hands.

"toph, how that hell can you encourage him? violence is not the answer" katara scolds me and i roll my eyes.

"so what, you wanted me to just do nothing? you wanted me to watch zuko break down every 5 minutes, knowing the piece of shit that is to blame is enjoying his happy life?" i burst angrily.

she opens her mouth to say something, but she closes it back, squeezing her lips together, and the look in her eyes becomes more understanding. she nods and backs off.

"you know i'm completely against violence of any kind, but that guy definetely deserved it" aang adds, scratching the back of his neck.

"thank you!" i throw my hands in the air, happy that even the pacifist of the group agrees with me. "now excuse me, i'm gonna go check on my boyfriend"

i open the door of our room slowly, and find him sitting on the bed, reading, but he shuts the book and throws it away as soon as he sees me.

"hey" i salute him as i enter the room, waving one of my hands lazily.

his eyes widen and he jumps off the bed immediately. i probably should have cleaned up first, so he wouldn't have to see me like this.

"sokka, what the fuck happened?" he comes to me in a rush and places one hand on my cheek.

he brushes his finger over my bottom lip, and i guess it's split in half, because it stings when he touches it. however, my eyes wander to the almost untouched plate of food that is resting on the table, with only half a sandwich eaten.

"why didn't you eat, baby?" i ask him, but that just makes him frown harder, emphasising some tiny wrinkles on his foredead.

"whose blood is this, sokka?" he gestures to my stained shirt, sounding concerned.

"did you drink any water today?" i stare at him in the eyes with an innocent look.

i think he's pissed off like crazy, because he pushes me by the chest in annoyance.

"stop avoiding my fucking questions and just tell me!" he snaps impatiently.

i sigh and look down at my hands that are playing with the hem of my shirt. i then lift my eyes and lock them with zuko's golden ones, that are still waiting for a response.

"i couldn't let him get away with hurting you" i explain with a gentle voice, and his expression softens.

the look he's giving me is a mixture of sadness and care, and his eyes start sparkling, as tears begin forming gradually. he throws himself in my arms, hugging my waist, and i'm taken aback by this sudden wave of affection, but i don't hug him back, because my hands are still full of blood.

"zuko, you'll stain all of your clothes" i warn him, but he doesn't move.

he buries his face in the crook of my neck and mutters an 'i don't care', so i wrap my arms around him as well. his warm breath against my skin gives me shivers and i melt in his touch.

"you got hurt" he squeacks, still mentaining the tight embrace.

he's probably refering to my bruised face, but i frankly couldn't care less about that.

"you should've seen the other guy" i chuckle and he puffs, smiling a little.

he retreats from the embrace and puts his arms around my neck, fixing me with his precious eyes.

"we should get cleaned up" he suggests and i giggle, agreeing with him completely.

we take a shower and change our clothes, then zuko asks me if i want to hit the beach for a little while, so of course i say yes. it'll be good for him to get out of the house and breathe in some fresh air.

i make an excuse and tell him i need to get some water from downstairs while he gets ready to go out, and i quickly instruct everyone to keep their mouths shut about what happened to zuko. he would most likely be extremely uncomfortable answering any questions and would want to crawl back to his room and not get out of there for days, so i better make sure that doesn't happen.

"ok, let's go" i go back upstairs and grab his hand.

he follows me, but i can see he's a little skeptical and anxious about facing the others, considering that everyone knows what he's been through. i know he hates being pittied.

"hi, guys" zuko says weakly, trying to force a smile as soon as we get to the living room.

they all greet him as if nothing happened, and i can feel him ease up a little. we exchange a few words, then i drag him outside, still holding his hand.

we chill on the beach for hours, and i try my best to make him at least smile, by attempting to make jokes, or by putting myself together a wig out of seaweed. i get a few giggles from him, which are more than i ever expected, and i'm truly happy that he is able to get his mind off things, at least for a little while.

## Chapter 35

zuko's POV

school has started for less than a week, and i already feel like breaking down. being in the terminal year, i get bombed from all directions with questions about what i want to do in the future, what college i want to attend, what job i want to get...

and i have no idea. absolutely none.

i feel anxious every time uncle brings it up, even though i am aware he means no harm, but the amount of stress is astronomical. i just find it hard to picture myself working my ass off every single day for the rest of my life, until i finally retire and wait for death to come. it's so fucking stupid.

the final exams are right around the corner, so i should send my application to a few universities, but i try not to think about it that much. i can't bare the unsettling feeling i get when i do.

"are you alright?" i sense a hand being placed on my shoulder.

i flinch from the unexpected contact that broke me from my thoughts, and sokka retreats his hand almost instantly.

"sorry, i kinda zoned out, but i'm fine" i mumble and try to focus on the literature teacher.

she's blabbering about i don't know what boring, outdated book she's making us read, so it's extremely hard for me to pay attention.

"you were staring into space, is something on your mind?" sokka insists, fixing me with his big, blue eyes.

"i said i was fine, ok?" i snap at him, but as soon as i do, i regret it deeply.

the teacher tells us to shush and threatens to send us to the principal if we don't stop chattering.

i ignore her and bury my face in my palms, pressing them hard against my eyes. i really can't do anything right, can i? sokka is the most amazing and caring boyfriend i could ever ask for, and he keeps trying to make sure i'm doing alright, and how do i repay him? by yelling at him?

i'm a horrible person. i don't deserve him, and i never will.

"i'm so sorry, sokka" i whisper and glance at him. "i didn't mean to sound rude, i know you mean well"

his eyes soften and he grabs my hand under the desk, interwining his fingers with mine. the sensation that his skin leaves on mine makes me feel as if everything will turn out ok.

"don't worry about it, i know you're pretty tense at the moment" he smiles at me with understanding and my heart melts.

i've talked to him about what's been bothering me lately, and he's been encouraging me, and telling me that it's not the end of the world not to have my life figured out at 18. i think he's right, but everyone else keeps making a big deal out of it, so i tend to get lost in my thoughts. luckly, he's always there to keep me from drowning in them.

&#45;-

the classes passed painfully slow, but finally, it's time to go home, so me and sokka jump in his car and head to his place. katara finished school early today, so she won't catch a ride with us today, as she normally would.

as soon as we enter his house, i throw my backpack on the floor, and so does sokka. i play with appa for a little bit, while sokka is making something to eat for us and for katara, that is probably in her room, doing... whatever she does all day.

"so, what do you wanna do now?" he asks me as soon as we finish eating lunch.

i think about it for a second, while sokka puts away all the dishes that were spread around the table.

"hmm, i don't know, we can make out" i suggest with a shrug.

he chuckles and approaches me with a devilish grin plastered all over his face.

"i like that idea" he adds, and as i'm leaning against the table, he places his hands on each side of my body.

i smile and cup his face with both of my hands, softly pressing my lips against his. he lifts me up so i can sit on the table, and i wrap my legs around his waist. he moves his hands on the small of my back, making the distance between us diminish completely.

he then lowers our bodies until i have my back flat against the table and he is leaning on top of me. our kiss grows deeper as the seconds pass, but the sudden noise of someone clearing their throat disturbs us.

"katara, can't you see i'm busy? go away" sokka groans, trying not to separate our lips too much.

he doesn't even turn around to look at her, he just goes back to touching me everywhere and i can't complain about that.

"ugh- sokka..." a deep, manly voice replies, instead of katara's soft one.

we both instantly turn our heads towards the man standing in the room with us. he has his hair tied up in a ponytail similar to sokka's, only the man's hair is longer on the sides.

"dad?" sokka asks rethorically and gets off me completely.

i jump off the table instinctively and panick takes over me. sokka's father. he caught us making out on his kitchen table. he saw his son... kissing another boy, he's gonna be mad, he's gonna hurt sokka, he's gonna forbid him to see me again.

i glance at sokka, but i don't see any trace of concern over his face. instead, a smile forms on his lips and he runs over to his dad and hugs him tightly.

"spirits, i missed you so much! why didn't you tell me you were coming today?" sokka questions him, as they are breaking the embrace.

"i wanted to surprise you" he answers, but then his eyes shift to me.

i feel so awkward and my cheeks are burning and i don't know what to say, cause i never had to meet the parents of my boyfriend. what if he doesn't like me? what if he hates me and thinks i'm not good enough for his son? what if he kicks me out of the house and i can never come here again?

"you must be zuko" the blue eyed man smiles at me. "sokka has told me so much about you, i'm glad i can finally meet you. i'm hakoda"

ok, i did not expect that. sokka never mentions his father much, but the fact that he talked to him about me makes my heart grow in my chest. i smile weakly and extend my hand so i can shake his (that's what people do when they meet for the first time... right?), but he ignores it and pulls me into a hug instead.

at first, i am puzzled by his gesture, but he squeezes me in his arms, and all i can say is that he gives amazing hugs, so i return the favour.

"hey, where's bato?" sokka asks as soon as me and his father depart.

right after he says that, the door opens and a man with even longer hair than hakoda enters the house, carrying a bunch of luggage.

"a little help?" the man requests, struggling to move with all of the weight.

i'm a little confused. who the hell is this? sokka and his dad rush over to the misterious man and take the bags from his hands, then they leave them somewhere in the living room. spirits, i have no idea what to do, so i just sit here like an idiot.

"hello, i'm bato, sokka's step dad" the man with long hair introduces himself, urging me to shake his hand. "i assume you're zuko"

sokka's what now? he has two dads?! my jaw literally drops and for a second, i leave his hand hanging in the air, but then i remember i have to shake it, so i do.

"sokka never told you this?" bato giggles, and so does hakoda, who comes near us and leans on his husband's shoulder.

"he didn't bother to" i mutter and throw sokka an angry look.

he shrugs and starts scratching his neck. he's lucky he is extremely cute, or i'd be mad that he omitted to tell me this.

"you never asked" he smiles innocently and shrugs, hoping he'd be excused.

i roll my eyes, and then slap my forehead at the absurdity of the situation.

"and i was scared your father is homophobic" i whine and everyone laughs, even if the struggle is real.

&#45;-

i took the job of making some tea for everyone, as sokka is horrible at it, so now all of us are in the living room, eating cookies and drinking hot leaf juice. and by 'all of us', i mean me, sokka, katara and their two dads.

"you have no idea how excited i was when sokka first told us about you" hakoda adresses me while taking a sip of tea. "for a while i was worried that both of my kids are straight"

we all stare suggestively at katara and after a few moments of silence she bursts.

"oh, don't look at me like that!" she throws her hands in the air. "you think i chose to like men? you think i wouldn't rather be attracted to women? have you seen them? they're gorgeous, but you all know i can't do anything about it!"

she sinks in the couch, with her arms crossed and her bottom lip out, pouting. hakoda pats her hair and brings her to his chest.

"you know i'm just joking, honey" katara's father places a peck on her head and she puffs.

"we love you, even if you're straight" bato adds and sokka giggles, while stuffing his face with some cookies.

bato and hakoda begin a conversation about who knows what, and katara keeps scrolling on her phone, but i only stare at sokka. some crumbs of food have fallen on his t-shirt and he has some chocolate around his mouth, which makes me giggle.

"you eat like a pig" i taunt him and he stops chewing for a second.

his cheeks are filled with cookies, making him look like a hamster, and i brush his shirt so i can cast away all the crumbs.

"shut up, you love me" he mumbles, with his mouth still full.

he's not wrong, and he knows it. i bump my shoulder into his jokingly, and after he drinks some tea, he leans his head on me, wrapping his arms around my body. he starts playing with my hair and i shut my eyes, enjoying the calming sensation.

"hey, zuko, do you want to see some pictures of sokka when he was little?" hakoda asks me and i accept immediately.

sokka protests a little, trying to stop his dad from embarrasing him, but hakoda happily brings some photo albums to show everyone. there are pictures from when sokka and katara were little kids, playing in the garden, eating with their bare hands, or drawing together.

"he never really liked wearing clothes, until like, pretty late" hakoda explains. "he kept taking them off- wait, i think i have a picture"

he shuffles through the album, until he spots what he was looking for: a picture with a five years old sokka, completely naked, facing the camera. i can barely hold my laughter, but the others don't even try to hide their amusement.

"ha ha, very funny" sokka rolls his eyes. "hey, at least i've grown a lot since then. zuko can confirm that"

he looks at me and winks, but the only thing he gets in response is silence from everyone. does he even put his thoughts through a filter before opening up his mouth?

"i hope you didn't mean what i think you did" katara rubs her temples.

i widen my eyes and i feel my blood climbing all the way to my cheeks. i punch him in the arm and he lets out an 'ouch'.

"spirits, i can't believe you said that in front of your parents!" i exclaim indignated.

i hide my face in my palms, full of embarrasement. he really has no shame whatsoever, and just suggested we had sex with his dads right there.

"ugh- let's pretend i didn't say that" sokka grins and scratches his neck nervously.

"how about i tell you what happened to me back when i was working in this place..." bato changes the subject and i'm thankful they're acting like nothing happened.

&#45;-

we listened to what stories hakoda and bato had to share with us until sunset. it was a really comfortable atmosphere, they kept trying to include me in their conversation so i wouldn't feel like i'm an outsider and overall it was a lot better than i expected.

i took a break from everything to go to the bathroom, so now i'm walking in the hallway, but for some reason, i stop before entering the kitchen. i know i shouldn't be eavesdropping, but sokka seems to have a heated conversation with hakoda.

"but- i can't leave him!" i hear sokka saying, and he seems pissed.

i can only assume that he's talking about me, but... to leave me? what are they talking about?

"i know it's hard, but think about the advantages!" hakoda replies. "if you move abroad with us, you can go to college there and have a better future than you would if you stayed here. besides, our family wouldn't have to be apart anymore if you'd study in the same country that we work in"

my heart drops after hearing these words, and i have to lean against the wall so i don't crumble on the floor.

"at least promise me you'll consider it" his father says and it follows a moment of silence.

i can hear the way blood is being pumped into my veins so loud that i think my ears are going to explode while waiting for his answer.

"fine, i'll think about it" sokka replies at last.

## Chapter 36

zuko's POV

'i'll think about it'

he'll think about it. he'll consider whether i'm worth staying here for or not... but i already know that i am not.

i take in a deep breath, because i have to act like him leaving wouldn't make my world crumble. i take a few steps back, then start walking loudly towards the kitchen, so hakoda and sokka can notice i'm there.

"uhm- it's getting pretty late, so um- i guess i- i should go home now" i stutter from the doorway.

hakoda gives me a warm smile, and i force one too in response, but sokka stares at me with concern. his dad doesn't know it, but we've never really slept apart that much since we got together. it was mostly because sokka made the nightmares i had about my father go away, and now it has become a habit.

judging by the look sokka is giving me, i am pretty sure that he's well aware i heard the conversation he had with his father.

"do you want me to give you a ride?" hakoda asks me, but i shake my head.

"thank you, but i'm ok with walking" i reply and he doesn't insist.

i say my goodbyes to everyone, then sokka walks me to the front porch of his house and closes the door behind him.

"are you sure you don't wanna stay over?" sokka stares at me with his big, blue eyes. "my dads would totally be ok with it"

he grabs my hand and makes that puppy face of his.

"maybe next time" i smile faintly and he pouts a little.

as i'm about to turn around and leave, sokka pulls me into a hug in which i sink immediately. i wrap my arms around him and bury my head in his shoulder, while he caresses my hair gently, squeezing me tightly.

"you know that i love you, no matter what, right?" he speaks after we hold the warm embrace for a moment.

somehow his words make me even sadder and i wanna break down right there. it may be just in my head, but why did that sound like a goodbye?

i nod, because i can't let out any words, then i finally leave him behind and start walking towards my house.

&#45;-

i've been laying in my bed for hours, staring at the ceiling while holding the stuffed turtle duck that sokka gave me, unable to fall asleep. the sheets are so cold without him here and i don't like the fact that i've become somehow dependent of his presence. i know it's not healthy, but i also don't ever want to be apart from him more than necessary.

i can't stop thinking about the possibility of him leaving to another country... without me. i know he cares about me a lot, but- am i more important to him than his future?

it would be selfish of me to ask him to stay here just for me, and i can't do that when i know it would be best for him if he left. setting aside the better university choices and higher paid jobs, he wouldn't be forced to stay far away from his parents anymore.

i can't make him choose between me and his family, it wouldn't be fair. i guess i should take a step back and encourage him to do what's best for him, because his well being is way more important than whatever i'm feeling.

he would literally rip my heart out and take it with him if he actually left, but knowing that he would live a better life is worth all the suffering.

but for real now, why on earth would he refuse such an opportunity? for me? huh, that would surely be dumb. yes, i do know he loves me, but i also know i'm not that special, so it's not like he wouldn't be able to replace me at some point in his life. even if it would take some time... he would get over me, it's innevitable.

i suddenly hear my phone buzzing on the nightstand, and i extend my arm so i can grab it. i look at the time and i see that it's 2am, and i got text from sokka:

ponytail guy: are you awake?? i can't sleep without you, i already miss holding you in my arms... : ((

i find myself grinning at the screen like an idiot and i squeeze the turtle duck harder to my chest.

me: i miss you too.

i'm about to write something else, but sokka's face suddenly pops up on the screen, as he's video calling me. i chuckle and answer immediately.

"hey there, pretty boy" he says from behind the screen in a raspy voice.

he has his hair falling all over his face and he's hugging the stuffed sky bison that i won for him at the carnival..

"hi" i mutter and hide my face with my hand.

i can't believe he makes me blush with his smile even through the phone.

"you know what i was thinking?" he shifts his position and now he's leaning on his elbows. "we never went on a date"

i frown and stop to think about it for a second.

"what do you mean? we hang out all the time and we practically live together" i point out confused.

"yeah, but we never had like- an official first date" he explains, raising his eyebrows.

wow, he's right. what idiots are together  
for months without having gone on any dates?

"huh... i guess we should fix that" i suggest. "wanna go on our first date this weekend?"

"yes!" he immediately replies. "and i already know the perfect place"

he sounds excited, so i wait for him to go on about what he has in mind for our date, but he doesn't explain.

"well?" i urge him to talk. "where are we gonna go?"

"it's a surprise" he smirks and i scoff, rolling my eyes.

he knows damn well that i hate surprises. i don't like being unprepared for something, i like to take my time and think about everything before actually doing it.

"don't worry, i'll take you somewhere nice" he reassures me once he sees my concerned face.

"fine, but if we go to a restaurant, i'll murder you" i threaten him.

he giggles, but i can see his eyes are almost shut and he is obviously tired.

"how can you insult me like that?" he tries to sound hurt, but his murmurs are getting quieter. "only straight people go to restaurants..."

he yawns, covering his mouth with the back of his palm and stretching a little bit. he nuzzled his head in the bison and he soon starts breathing regularly.

"sokka?" i call his name, but he doesn't move, so he must have fallen asleep.

i smile and just sit there for awhile, admiring how peaceful he looks, with his hair falling on his face and on the plushie.

he didn't mention the conversation he had with his dad, even though i bet he was thinking about it. i know we have to talk about it at some point, we can't avoid the subject for much longer.

we have to consider our possibilities, especially the one of him leaving. what would happen to us then? would it be worth having a long distance relationship? wouldn't it be unfair of me to keep him tied up and unable to move on and find someone wherever he is going?

i shake my head to cast away all the negative thoughts. even if we do break up, i should enjoy the time i have left with him, and be happy that i got the chance to be so close to him, despite it not being... forever. even thinking that makes my eyes water.

i don't turn my phone off, but istead i place it on the nightstand at an angle from which i can see sokka and he can look at me too, in case he wakes up.

&#45;-

sokka's POV

i know zuko heard when my father proposed me to go and live abroad with him and bato. he didn't confront me about it though, so i didn't mention it either, but i'm more than certain that zuko's overthinking it.

i could barely sleep without him in my arms. it felt so lonely and cold, as he's a human heater that radiates warmth continuously, mostly because he's a firebender. talking to him on the phone helped a little, and the fact that he didn't end the call even after i fell asleep like a baby makes my heart tingle.

i just want to hold him forever and not give a fuck about anything in the world, so when he refused to stay over last night, my heart slightly broke. however, i understood his reluctance. after hearing that i might have to move far away, he probably just wanted to put his thoughts in order and he couldn't have done it unless he was alone.

we have to talk about it though, even if it's scary for both of us.

&#45;-

i drive to zuko's house, first thing in the morning, without even having breakfast, because i'm too impatient. i run up to his door and use the spare key he gave me to enter the house. his uncle is not in the kitchen as usual, so he must be at work.

"zuko?" i call for him as soon as i step inside, and he comes rushing downstairs.

i lay my eyes on him, dressed in a grey t-shirt and black sweatpants, while his hair is messed up in every direction. he's so adorable, i want to pull my eyes out of my sockets- and also rip all of his clothes off.

"what are you doing here so early?" he asks me confused.

i mean, there is more than an hour left before we should head off to school, but i couldn't wait that much longer.

"i missed you" i say and pull him into a hug.

he returns the embrace and i feel the hole in my chest, that i didn't know was even there, getting filled again. he's so cuddly and warm and he smells like cinnamon mixed with vanilla... i desintegrate in his arms.

"why are you acting like you haven't seen me in years?" he chuckles and withdraws gently.

maybe it seems like im exaggerating, but i realised that i genuinely can't stand him being far away from me. i ignore his question, and i hit him with another one.

"can i kiss you?" i ask while staring at his rosy lips.

all i want to do is touch them with mine, but i have to make sure he is ok with it, especially after what happened on ember island. i don't want him to feel uncomfortable.

he answers my question by pulling me by my belt and crushing his lips into mine. he tastes like mint, so he probably just brushed his teeth before i came in. i place my hand on his nape so i can get a better hold of him, and a little moan escapes his mouth, sending vibrations all over my body.

"did you have breakfast?" he asks me when we pull away so we can catch our breaths.

i stare at his bright golden eyes and i bite my lower lip at how tempted i am to fuck him right here and now.

"no, but i can eat you instead" i give him a devilish smirk and tap on his nose with my finger.

he looks away and i notice his cheeks turning bright red at the sound of that idea. he then fixes me with his eyes with a more serious look.

"and what's stopping you?" he raises his eyebrow and starts drawing circles on my chest with his index.

i didn't expect him to say that, but i'm glad i'm not the only one who's horny as hell.

i brush his hair with my fingers and he shuts his eyes, nuzzling his head in my hand when i reach the back of his head and pull it lightly. i then cover his mouth with mine, devouring him like i haven't eaten in days.

"may i respectfully rip your clothes off?" i whisper in his ear and then nibble on his earlobe, causing him to shiver.

"y- yes, you may" he stutters, all flustered.

damn, i adore getting these reactions out of him, and i can't possibly hide my smirk. i take his t-shirt off and throw it on the floor, then put my hand around his neck and pin him against the wall, maybe a little too rough.

"are you ok? was it too much?" i check up on him, to make sure i don't do anything he might not enjoy.

he clenches his fist in my shirt and pulls me into a kiss, exploring my mouth with his tongue.

"i liked it" he replies and stares at me.

the way he looks at me, begging for more with his eyes, while having his back up against the wall and his hair messed up, really drives me crazy...

i bite and suck on his sweet spot until i hear him moan and breathe heavily, which turns me on even more.

let's just say we didn't get to school on time for the first class... or the second.

## Chapter 37

zuko's POV

is it weird that i woke up a little nervous because today i'm going on my first date with the guy i've been together with for months? he told me to dress casually, but it's really frustrating that i have no idea where we're going.

it takes me more than half an hour to pick an outfit, and i settle at last for a pair of beige pants, a white shirt and a black wool vest over it. i add a pearl neckleace, some rings and the pink and yellow flower crown that sokka made for me, then i put on some eyeliner and paint my nails black. i also tie my hair eren style, because i know how much sokka loves the way it suits me (i have to admit, it does look pretty great).

i finish getting ready just in time, because i hear the doorbell ring and i rush downstairs. i impatiently open the door and i am met with the most gorgeous person in the world.

sokka is leaning with one of his hands against the outside wall of the house, with his head slightly tilted down, but he's looking right up at me. his shimmering, blue eyes are identical to the brightest stars that glow on the night sky, and his hair is down, perfectly framing his face.

his smile is warmer than the sun and he is wearing a navy blue, short sleeved shirt with vertical white stripes all over it, unbuttoned enough that i can see the flawless caramel skin on his chest. he also has some chains hanging on the side of his white jeans and he is holding a bouquet of black roses.

"hey" he smirks and i already feel like fainting because of how pretty he is.

he hands me the flowers and when i look at his hands, i notice some black stains on them, as well as a few scratches.

"did you- did you paint these yourself?" i wonder and he scratches the back of his neck.

"huh- i tried to find black roses in every flower shop in town, but none of them had any, so i bought normal ones and made them a little special" he explains. "also, i took out the thorns so you wouldn't accidentally poke yourself, cause you're a dum dum"

my heart can't take it at this point. he must have spent such a long time paninting this whole bunch of roses, just for me, and he got scratched, so that i wouldn't. i don't deserve him.

"you- you don't like them?" sokka asks me, seeming a little hurt.

i don't understand where did he get that idea from, but then i realise i haven't said a word and i'm staring into space like an idiot. i shake my head frantically.

"no, no, sokka, i love them" i assure him and throw myself in his arms, careful not to crush the roses.

i chuckle and he brushes my hair with his fingers, squeezing me closer to him and placing a peck on my head. i then break the embrace and get back in the house, i rapidly put the flowers in a vase, then we both head to sokka's car.

"wait, wait" he extends his arms in front of me, before we reach the car.

he jogs ahead of me and opens the car door, then gestures with his hands, urging me to step inside. i giggle and get in the car, then sokka shuts the door and circles the vehicle so he can sit in the driver's seat.

"where are we going?" i question him, but he starts the engine and turns on the radio.

"you'll see when we get there" he responds vaguely and i roll my eyes. "but it will take some time"

during the ride, he sometimes puts his right hand on my thigh, so i place my left one over his and interwine our fingers. we sing along on some of the songs that come on the radio, we kiss every time we have to wait at a red light, and i laugh at sokka's bad jokes every time he makes one (way to often).

after more than an hour, he stops the car in the middle of nowhere, where there are hills all around us, and tells me to wait in the car, then he runs around the car and opens the door for me once again.

"you know i have hands, right?" i ask sarcastically, but i can't hide my smile.

"just let me be nice, ok?" he raises his eyebrows and opens the trunk of the car.

he pulls a basket out of there and locks the car, then he grabs my hand and kisses it.

"we have to walk from here for about 5 minutes" sokka explains and i nod.

i don't mind it, as we had to stay put for so long in the car on our way here, so this is a good chance to stretch out a bit. we start making our way through the tall grass, breathing in the fresh air while holding hands.

when we reach our destination, i gasp as soon as i see the view, and i'm left speechless. sokka brought me to a wide field of sunflowers, all coloured in bright yellow, with butterflies flying all around us.

"it's just like-" i start speaking, but the words remain stuck in my throat.

sokka puts the basked down and hugs me from behind, kissing my neck breifly. i can't take my eyes off this place.

"yeah, it is" sokka smiles, already knowing what i'm thinking. "it's very similar to the painting you made on the wall of the Jasmine Dragon"

i chuckle as i recall the way we threw paint at eachother and laughed our asses off, then we left our handprints one next to the other. back then we weren't together, and i would have never imagined that i'd be sitting here right now, with sokka as my boyfriend.

we walk through the field of sunflowers, feeling that we're part of a fairytale that only portraits the two of us. sokka chooses a spot under a huge tree that can shelter us from the sun and extends a bright red rug on the ground, contrasting with the emerald grass.

we lay down and sokka starts taking stuff out of the basket he brought, a lot of food, snacks, a bottle of champagne and... cake. a chocolate cake.

"hey, food first, dessert after!" sokka slaps my hand away, right after i grab a spoon and try to get a piece of cake.

i bring my bottom lip above the upper one and frown, but he casts away my pouty face with a kiss. his lips are a thousand times sweeter than any dessert i could imagine, so i give in instantly.

we eat for a while, chattering in between, and stopping to kiss a few times. sokka insists on feeding me some strawberries covered in chocolate, staring right into my eyes while introducing them in my mouth slowly.

he has no right to be this hot.

finally, it's time for what i've been waiting for the most, but instead of simply handing me a spoon, sokka takes out two glasses. he then gives me one and urges me to do what he does. he stabs the cake with his glass and fills it, so i do the same.

"it's kinda weird to eat it like this" i chuckle while i take a spoon full of cake.

"but it's more fun" sokka winks at me exaggerately. "wait, you got some chocolate on your face"

"where?" i ask him and start randomly rubbing my face with my fingers.

he puts his glass down and comes over to me, cupping my jaw with his hand and staring into my eyes. i get a flashback from when something similar happened as we were eating pancakes with the gAang, way before we started dating. my heart is pounding in this moment, just as it did last time, regardless of the fact that he is my boyfriend now.

i assumed sokka would just wipe the chocolate with his thumb, but instead he brings his face closer to mine, without breaking the eye contact, then he starts licking around my mouth.

"mm, so sweet" he smirks, but he doesn't back away.

he brushes his tongue against my lips, gently sucking on the lower one, then we melt into a kiss, forgetting all about the cake. he delicately makes me lay down on my back, holding my head so i don't hit it on the solid ground, then proceeds to move his lips over mine.

"oh, i almost forgot, let's drink!" sokka pulls away suddenly and leaves me all flustered and dizzy on the ground.

he grabs another two empty glasses, hands me one and pours some fancy champane in each of them. after we drink, he puts everything away, stands up and lends me his hand to help me get up too. we dust off our clothes, then sokka looks me dead in the eyes and taps my shoulder.

"tag, you're it!" he says and starts running away.

is he for real? i don't move a muscle and he notices it, so he stops at a reasonable distance.

"you know i hate running, why would i do it willingly?" i whine.

he raises an eyebrow and crosses his arms to his chest.

"maybe you're just scared you can't catch me" he shrugs and grins.

"i can totally catch you!" i puff and roll my eyes.

"yeah? then do it" he provokes me, knowing damn well that i won't let him win so easily.

he smiles victoriously when i start running after him down the hill, wind blowing in my hair and the fresh scent of nature filling my lungs. i manage to catch him at some point, despite my feet burning up from all the effort, but i kinda think he let me win intentionally, because he doesn't even look tired.

"tag-" i gasp for air. "you- you're it" i breathe havily and struggle to come to my senses.

i throw myself on the grass, under a tree, feeling the embrace of the earth, the tickling of the grass against my skin, and the vastness of the sky above me. sokka lays down next to me and delicately kisses my forehead, chuckling softly.

"you lasted longer than i expected" he mocks me and i bump my fist against his shoulder.

"shut up" i scoff. "you run for fun, and that's embarrassing"

he starts laughing and the sound makes my heart overflow with happiness, so i roll over and snuggle into his arms, unable to bare the absence of his touch any longer.

we sit in silence as we watch the sun go down, painting the sky in shades of orange, yellow, red and a little bit of purple. just like in my painting.

"zuko, you know what colour reminds me of you?" sokka asks me out of the blue.

he's sitting up, with his back leaning against the tree, and i'm resting my head on his lap, admiring him by looking up. i raise my eyebrow to signal him to go on with his answer.

"yellow" he says, and i wait quietly for an explanation. "it's such a bright colour, it shines with optimism and happiness, and it radiates warmth, just like you do"

i sit up so i can look at him from the same level, and he doesn't take his eyes off me for even a second.

"you light up my life with your pure smile, and every moment that i spend with you feels like a blessing from the universe" he continues and smiles weakly. "i know it sounds silly, but i mean it, zuko"

with his stare, he sends out more emotion than he could ever put into words, and i can tell that he's one hundread percent honest with me. his eyes are galaxies that could swallow me whole and i'd thank them. i guess i already got lost in them, but i never want to be found.

i feel some warm tears escaping my eyes, but sokka wipes them away with his thumb and i nuzzle my cheek into his hand.

"you make me aspire to become the best version of myself that i can be" sokka goes on. "i can't imagine going to sleep without you in my arms, or waking up next to anyone else. you are my yellow, zuko"

he shoves his hand in his pocket and takes out a tiny dark blue box. my heart is beating so fast, like it's running a marathon and i can almost feel it in my throat. is that...?

"don't panick, i'm not asking you to marry me" he says when he sees that my eyes have widened. "well, not yet anyway" he chuckles and scratches the back of his neck awkwardly.

he opens the little box and there are two identical rings in there. they each have two little hands at the ends almost touching one another.

"consider these to be promise rings" sokka takes one out of the box. "if you want, obviously, promise me we won't let anything or anyone come between us. ever"

i feel like anything i could say right now would be meaningless.

"of course i want that, dummy" i reply, almost sobbing. "and even if it was a marriage proposal, i would have still said yes. but you're right, it is a little early for that"

he lets out a laugh of relief and slides the ring on my finger, making it look like it's hugging it. with my trembling hands, i take the other ring and put in on his finger.

"stop crying, because then i'll cry too" sokka exclaims, but it's too late, cause now we're both balling our eyes out.

but these are happy tears. we are happy. he makes me happy. i often think about how i would have probably killed myself at some point if i hadn't met him, so i pretty much owe him my life. spending eternity by his side is the best thing that could possibly happen to me.

the cold metal on my ring finger feels odd, but i cannot stop staring at how gorgeous it is. the golden sunshine falling on sokka's body looks ethereal, and i still find it hard to believe that i could pull off this beautiful person.

wait. i completely forgot something, and i am just now struck by realisation.

"sokka-" he immediately turns to me, feeling the concern in my voice. "are you gonna move abroad with your father?"

i did it, i spoiled the mood, and now his face is serious and his eyes burdened, but i couldn't put off talking about this any longer.

"he keeps nagging me about it, insisting and trying to convince me to do it, even though i told him i don't wa-" he starts speaking, but i cut him off.

the most crazy idea has just hit me like a meteorite, and i voice it out loud without thinking ahead.

"let's run away" i say and he goes silent. "we can buy a trailer and travel the entire world together with the money my mother left me. we can settle for a few months in each city we like, get some jobs there only to earn enough to keep us going if we run out of money"

this thought has brought me so much euphoria, that i'm a little too excited and i bet i sound like a lunatic. sokka is staring at me, without saying a single word.

"huh, forget i just said that" i laugh nevrously and look down, with my hands rested against my knees, fidgeting with my fingers. "it's a crazy idea, we can pretend i never suggested it"

he doesn't want to leave me, but what if his father makes him? what if we have to maintain a long distance relationship? i would die without being able to touch him for so long.

"zuko, that is the most genius idea i've ever heard in my entire life" sokka's words break me from my thoughts.

he places his hand on my chin and moves my head so i can look right into his deep blue eyes. a sparkle of hope has just ignited in my heart.

"i am dead serious when i tell you there's nothing i'd rather do more in my life than travel the world with you" he says with a profound voice. "let's do this"

i throw myself in his arms, forcing him to fall on his back and i wrap my arms around him so tight, that i think his intestines are going to burst out of his mouth. i didn't expect him to actually say yes immediately.

the idea was a surprise even for me, sounding too alluring to be true, but i know that, whatever life will throw at us, we're gonna be alright. we'll overcome any obstacles, however serious they might seem, as long as we're together.

"i love you so much" i mutter in his shirt and he brushes my hair, while drawing circles on my back with his fingers.

"i love you too" he replies and kisses my head gently, as the sun finally sets.

&#45;-

THE END
